- Board latest-action marking treats Aemeath `hidden-hand` and `voyage-star` as point actions, alongside ordinary moves and flip-stone. This moves the red latest-action ring onto the hidden-hand placement when 小爱出击 resolves and prevents the ring from remaining on the previous ordinary move after 远航星 erases its source point, avoiding false visual evidence that the derived skill was centered elsewhere.
- Bright School board guards keep DOM-only board effect layers transparent and restore their pseudo-elements so legacy broad surface cleanup cannot turn QiuYuan's slash overlay into a blank paper panel or a plain bar over the board; the row-slash owner rule also keeps `.board-row-slash` at `max-width: none !important` so the portrait mobile all-element `max-width: 100% !important` safety layer cannot cap the scar back to board width. Mornye's protocol marker remains a pointer-events-none point child and never changes board hit testing. The playable board surface is shared through `--board-wood-texture`, currently `/assets/boards/go-board-background-reference-color-vertical-2048.webp`, a low-saturation vertical bamboo/wood WebP asset reused by the base room CSS and Bright School guard layers.
- Board move preview and mobile touch-confirmation markers are point-local visual effects centered by the `.point` absolute coordinate contract, so ChangLi's extra-turn ordinary placement hints stay on the same intersections as stones and SVG grid lines. Touch confirmation uses only the inner `.touch-confirm-marker`; `.point.touch-confirming::before` stays hidden, and mobile point press/confirmation states preserve `translate(-50%, -50%)` even when adding scale or reduced-motion overrides, preventing duplicate blue rings, apparent marker drift, and stones animating from grid cells back to intersections after placement.
- Default black/white board stones are ringless: shared stone CSS and Bright School default overrides avoid `border` and `inset` shadow outlines, while semantic skill states can still draw glow/state halos.
- Chisa's `liberty-purge` now has a post-banner targeted Pixi cast: `Board` first shows a delayed pending placement on the target point using that player's selected stone decoration, then `BoardSkillEffects` reads `removalMarkIds` and draws several saturated red scissor slashes in sequence only over the points that will receive removal marks, excluding the newly placed stone. The server derives the board-effect window from `removalMarkIds.length`, so rooms with many removals keep the pending preview open until every removal slash has had time to play; the persistent skill result appears immediately after the slashes fade. A resolved Chisa placement that remains on the board carries the persistent `liberty-purge-stone` eerie red glow instead of reusing the latest-move ring, while resolved `.liberty-purge-removal-mark` crosses remain point-local DOM effects centered with `left/top: 50%` and `translate(-50%, -50%)`.

## Modal Layering Note

- Browser request toasts render through a `document.body` portal, keeping them outside `.mobile-room-screen` so mobile board sizing and position do not shift when draw, counting, or result-confirmation request toasts appear or disappear.
- Chisa (`chisa`) uses the `liberty-purge` active skill. The server resolves a normal legal move first, then removes all one-liberty groups from a board snapshot, records `removalMarkIds`, clamps negative overclock deltas to zero, clears ko, and keeps `.liberty-purge-removal-mark` red crosses until the opponent's next real turn ends. While those red crosses are active, the marked `clearAfterColor` treats the same empty intersections as forbidden points for ordinary placement and legal-move skill targets, so desktop and mobile previews/click confirmation must read the shared `libertyPurgeMarks` helper instead of relying only on point-local data. Because `liberty-purge` places a real stone, the placed point records `skillEffect: "liberty-purge-stone"` / `skillEffectOwner` for its persistent red glow when it survives the purge, while `lastMarkedAction` does not add the generic latest-move ring to that skill placement. Admin users own Chisa by default until recruitment is implemented.
- Shared `.modal-backdrop` stacks above room `--room-floating-z` surfaces so request and confirmation modals dim skill chips, chat controls, and room member popovers together. `RoomBattleStage` uses a bounded two-level room stack: ordinary fallbacks top out at `140`, and only the latest interacted surface receives `141`, so first-hover skill details beat untouched chat/member controls without repeated interactions ever climbing past the modal backdrop at `160`. 房间内申请和棋、申请数子与确认数子结果的定时 toast 由流程响应、阶段变化或自动消失关闭，不提供手动关闭按钮。
- Character duplicate chain counts remain in user data for reward/progression logic, but player-facing desktop and mobile UI hides character-chain badges on portrait surfaces.
- Current built-in character, costume, and Denia candy-effect portraits use normalized WebP assets under `/assets/characters/portraits/` and `/assets/costumes/portraits/`. `npm run portraits:normalize` alpha-trims each configured local asset, proportionally fits the complete visible subject into a shared 792px safe box, and bottom-centers it on a transparent 900×900 per-frame canvas. Static inputs remain lossless; animated inputs use the union of all frame bounds, retain their frame count, delays and loop metadata, and apply one shared transform to every frame. The Denia candy catalog entry explicitly requires animation, so `npm run check:portraits` rejects a static-first-frame regression in addition to missing, opaque, non-WebP, oversized, off-anchor, or non-normalized repository portraits. Remote HTTP(S) portraits remain supported but are reported as skipped without network access. Legacy portrait files stay at their old paths so saved room/replay snapshots retain their original URL and framing; startup only migrates exact untouched built-in character/costume catalog rows to the new paths and resets those costume rows to `100/0/0`, preserving admin-customized rows and all user/history data.
- The login title uses its dedicated visible `/assets/login-sigrika-mascot.webp` instead of reusing `CHARACTERS.sigrika.portrait`. The supplied artwork is cleaned of its bottom export residue, alpha-trimmed and proportionally fitted on matching transparent 640x640 PNG/WebP canvases, with exactly 8 transparent raster rows retained below visible alpha; at the 252px desktop display size this becomes about 3px, matching the structural-rule thickness so the character meets the rule's top edge without covering it or floating above it. The runtime WebP remains the compressed delivery asset. `AuthScreen` wraps the panel in `.auth-composition`, while semantic `.auth-panel-header` and `.text-window-title` continue to own the club-title structure and 霞鹜漫黑 title face. The composition custom properties jointly define the 440px panel, panel padding, mascot size, horizontal overhang, top reserve and header-rule offsets, so desktop centering measures the complete mascot-plus-card silhouette rather than the card alone. The Bright School repair owner draws a pointer-transparent `--bright-border` / `--bright-blue` two-layer rule whose negative left inset reaches through the overhang and whose main stroke shares its bottom anchor with the mascot, making the divider a visual shelf under the character without extra interactive DOM, blur or motion. Desktop uses a 252px square mascot, 118px left overhang and 117px top reserve; the existing `max-width: 900px` mobile-layout family collapses the left reserve, scales the mascot through `clamp(148px, 42vw, 164px)`, covers portrait phones, common mobile landscape widths and 768px previews, and keeps horizontal overflow at zero while the auth screen retains short-height vertical scrolling. The form keeps its default state terse: registration shows only the username-width and `8-64 位` label notes, while concrete causes appear beside invalid fields. Mode changes retain the username but clear passwords; password visibility controls remain 44px; submission uses a synchronous lock, abortable request, pending state, first-invalid-field focus, and an accessible in-app session-conflict dialog instead of `window.confirm`.
- The login title column reads the application version from the root `package.json` and renders it as `v{version}` directly below the club title. The label shares the existing `SigrikaGo` subtitle's `.text-display-accent` typography, uses the same 4px title gap as the subtitle, and shifts 8px beyond the title's right edge on desktop and mobile to form a restrained three-level stagger, without introducing a second version constant, badge, background, border, or interaction.
- Character-detail dialogs expose the active character `palette` as a local theme token: ordinary and derived skill-name labels use a light mix of that character color with black text, while the acquisition label uses a neutral gray background with a square-corner border. Mobile character-detail dialogs keep the character name horizontal and single-line, place the optional CV label below the name, place the compact BGM preview capsule at the top-right of the heading row, left-align skill and character-description copy, and omit the standalone skill-cost badge; Bright School theme layers and the final `mobile-adaptive` safety layer repeat these constraints so narrow phones cannot fall back to vertical names or centered body text.
- Bright School portrait phone polish that must outlive theme overrides is centralized in the final mobile safety layer. The shared profile dossier stacks portrait, doubled identity/nameplate, and context actions as three centered hero rows so the enlarged nameplate cannot squeeze the portrait or action controls; `.profile-record-panel` remains the only profile-body vertical scroll owner, the summary renders as 2×2, recent results stay on one ten-slot row, and the semantic character table remains a single-line six-column row with no horizontal scrolling. Home player-plaque fitting, compact WuWa rank digits, hidden chain badges, single-line coin wallet treatment, room tabs, login chrome, and desktop/narrow-desktop image-entry contracts retain their existing owners.
- Bright School card/list surfaces that keep hard paper shadows inside scroll or clipped modal shells reserve explicit right/bottom shadow bleed at the content layer instead of changing the shadow style. The house manual character grid, leaderboard rows and pinned current row, warehouse item grid, shop left information panel, and friends list keep their existing visuals while their desktop and mobile owner CSS gives the trailing and lower edges enough padding/scroll-padding for shadows to render.
- `ProfileResumeView` is the single self/social profile renderer. It explicitly orders mode tabs as 星炬、标准、五子棋 and owns the identity hero, four-item summary, recent form, and fixed 角色/对局/胜/负/和/胜率 table. The near-fullscreen desktop shell contains a centered dossier capped at 1160px; only `.profile-resume-view`, record-panel/overview rails, secondary-action rail, and the mode-tab wrapper stay transparent. Bright School renders the hero and character section as large cream cards, each statistic as an independent medium card, recent results as its own medium card, and each semantic character row as one short-shadow 7% palette-tinted cream card painted across its six cells. Identity and record portraits use an outer visible carrier plus an inner `.profile-portrait-mask`; within this dossier, portrait paint ignores scene-specific costume enlargement/offset, stays centered, and uses 80% `object-fit: contain` sizing so intrinsic proportions are preserved. Character-record masks are additionally borderless and transparent, while any chain badge remains a sibling outside the mask. Summary values use the normal UI face with tabular digits; the stylized `text-rating-value` face is deliberately not reused because its crossbar treatment reduces large summary-number legibility. Self context keeps “成就、个性化、金币、关闭” in that header order with 44px controls and a 56px header; social context exposes top-hero “点赞、加好友、加入黑名单、举报” icon controls in that order, with only the like count visible. Self context places an accessible icon-only replay button on the right of the total-games card, removes the recent-results heading and character-section title, stretches the desktop recent-results card to match the adjacent summary group while keeping portrait results compact, horizontally centers the result markers (including partial and empty records), and uses a compact 20px aligned column header; social context places replay beside the “最近十盘” heading. Both contexts instantiate the same portaled `HouseReplayDialog`; the social footer no longer owns relation actions or a relationship label. Mode requests preserve the last successful tab and matching content until success, expose loading/retry feedback, reject duplicate requests, and ignore stale mode/user responses. Tabs use tablist/tabpanel linkage plus roving keyboard focus, and every outer or nested sheet uses `ModalDialog` focus/Escape behavior.
- Desktop replay-list tables reserve a non-ellipsizing, left-aligned time column for full zh-CN timestamps, while mobile replay lists keep the card-style layout, left-align the replay time, and use final Bright School portrait overrides. The shared standalone replay dialog keeps its title/close area fixed and uses `.replay-dialog-list-scroll` as the bounded desktop/mobile scroll and pagination owner; its inner table is overflow-visible. Player-facing Bright School internal list/grid scroll owners hide scrollbar chrome while preserving mouse, keyboard, touch, and momentum scrolling.
- Friend-list action rows and room member username popovers keep unavailable social actions as native `disabled` controls with gray inactive styling across the base, Startorch HUD, and Bright School theme layers; the not-yet-open direct-message entry stays disabled in both surfaces.
- User profile details place like, friend, blacklist, and report actions directly in the identity hero. All four use 18px icons; friend/blacklist/report are icon-only with accessible names, like retains only its number and uses a minimum 72px desktop width so its icon matches the other controls without crowding the count. Likes keep the Asia/Shanghai daily state, existing friends expose that state through their accessible name, and pending operations lock duplicate requests. Report uses a light blood-red surface and opens the existing viewport-centred nested dialog; report failures remain inside that dialog and preserve its draft. Submitted reports continue to feed the read-only admin “用户举报” tab. Blacklist opens its own confirmation dialog before mutation and no relation-action footer or redundant “关系操作” label remains.
- Mobile leaderboard rows use a compact card grid with no extra left list inset, smaller rank badges, larger character portraits, and a centered username/rank stack so the rank label shares the same vertical center axis as the username/nameplate. Compact leaderboard `UserIdentity` names provide a fit font-size token so legacy/generated usernames shrink before they would fall back to clipped ellipses. Leaderboard rows representing the current user, including the pinned "my ranking" row, use a light green surface on desktop and mobile so they remain visually distinct from ordinary leaderboard entries, while every rank-position number keeps a transparent background so row-level highlighting remains the only fill treatment.
- Leaderboard top-three emphasis belongs on the whole row, not the rank-number badge: rank 1 uses `/assets/leaderboard/top-rank-gold.webp`, rank 2 uses `/assets/leaderboard/top-rank-silver.webp`, and rank 3 uses `/assets/leaderboard/top-rank-bronze.webp` as subtle imagegen satin-paper textures with a light readability overlay on desktop leaderboard rows. The top-three rank number itself stays a plain readable number with no triangular badge background. Desktop leaderboard portraits render through a dedicated `leaderboard-avatar` frame with character id metadata so full-body portrait thumbnails can be positioned inside the avatar slot without shifting the text columns or mobile card layout.
- Selected tab-like controls and selected option buttons generally share a pressed visual contract across desktop and mobile. Watch and profile dossier mode tabs share the same `.window-mode-tabs` class and final computed inactive/selected button contract instead of maintaining a profile-only approximation. The profile wrapper remains transparent, borderless and shadowless, uses `width: auto` inside bounded margins so the third tab cannot be clipped, adds vertical breathing room, and removes transform feedback under reduced motion.
- Achievement and personalization modal headers keep their close button in the same grid row as the title on desktop and mobile; the final Bright School mobile layer repeats this contract so global absolute close-button rules cannot push nested modal close controls onto the divider line.
- Room player info panels use `.active-turn` as the current-turn visual contract: the player whose turn it is turns yellow on both desktop and mobile, and late theme layers such as Bright School must preserve that yellow turn-state cue.
- Room player disconnect state is shown through the portrait background, not a text badge: active rooms add `.disconnected-portrait` to the disconnected player's portrait wrapper and render a light red portrait background on desktop and mobile, while finished rooms suppress the disconnect state.
- Desktop resume headers keep the title and one right-aligned action group in a single row; that group renders “成就”、金币、关闭 in DOM order so global close-button positioning cannot collide with the wallet.
- Mobile resume headers retain the same single-row DOM order. The title shrinks within available space, while achievement, the coin capsule, and close remain reachable 44px controls without creating a second header row.
- Desktop resume and profile modals keep the near-fullscreen outer window fixed inside the viewport with hidden outer overflow. The centered `.profile-resume-view` is capped at 1160px; `.profile-record-panel` assigns explicit `status / overview / characters / actions` areas, so missing optional status markup cannot shift the long character table into an intrinsic row. Desktop and portrait both display a transparent/borderless/shadowless `.profile-character-table-head` outside `.profile-character-table-scroll`, collapse the duplicate semantic `thead` visually while retaining accessibility, and let only the character rows scroll; no sticky header is used, so transparent header text cannot overlap rows. The social profile’s visible “角色战绩” heading uses the shared window-title font and `1.5rem` size. The labelled row scroller hides native scrollbar chrome without disabling wheel, touch, or keyboard scrolling, preserves trailing scroll padding, and keeps the blacklist footer visible. Desktop character rows retain table layout with a 70px rhythm; portrait rows remain native table rows at 60px, keep all six columns and headers on one line, allocate explicit widths to protect the win-rate column, and allow only the character name itself to ellipsize. The social title and close control share an explicit 44px header grid track on desktop and portrait layouts. Bright School's final profile owner is imported last in `qa-guard.css` after older commerce/mobile/effect owners, clears terminal-system leakage only from `.profile-character-table`, and leaves the separate house-manual `.character-record-row` contract untouched. Character detail headers separately reserve right-side space so the compact BGM player cannot collide with the close button.
- 履历积分问号说明明确提示：对局积分根据对手实力动态增减，友谊赛不会增减积分；段位问号说明最近十盘的升降规则。两种提示均向统计卡下方展开，所在卡片在 hover/focus 时抬升层级；移动端聚焦触发器后按提示框实际边界微调 `.profile-record-panel` 的滚动位置，并由履历最终移动规则覆盖误命中的房屋资料 fixed-tooltip 样式，保证提示框完整位于资料窗口内容区且不被模式选项卡或后续卡片裁切。该说明只修正用户可见文案与展示行为，不改变结算逻辑。
- Self and social profiles share the same “最近十盘” section and result-marker layout, and both place replay beside that section heading with vertical center alignment.
- Personalization sections now expose one "style picker" entry per equipment category. Each picker opens a nested modal containing default plus unlocked decorations, closes immediately after selection, and updates the local try-on preview while the existing save action remains the only persistence step.

## Music Management Note

- 莫宁（`mornye`）已接入完整角色语音：技能、出战、对局开始、读秒开始、剩余读秒次数、10 到 1 秒倒计时、胜负和结果，以及 `house-detail` 角色详情语音。部员手册中打开莫宁角色详情或点击详情内的“描述”文本区域会通过现有 `house-detail` 事件播放 `/assets/voice/mornye_detail.ogg`；关闭角色详情弹窗或关闭整个部员手册时会调用 `stopVoicePlayback()` 停止当前角色语音，避免详情长语音在窗口关闭后继续播放。
- 新用户或没有有效本地音频设置的环境默认使用 `master: 100`、`bgm: 60`、`sfx: 100`、`voice: 100`；已有 `sigrika-audio-settings` 继续覆盖默认值。角色音频和 TTS 不再因播放状态衰减 BGM，背景音乐始终保持用户设置音量；`requestBackgroundMusicDuck()` 只保留给招募演出等明确的非语音场景。全部静态角色语音源资产通过 `npm run voices:normalize` 在统一基线上应用 `1.15` 线性增益（约 `+1.214 dB`），离线校准到约 `-16.8 LUFS` 且不超过约 `-0.8 dBTP`，短于 500ms 的片段使用约 `-17.8 dBFS RMS` 回退；`npm run check:voices` 只读检查响度漂移。运行时不再按 `AudioBuffer` RMS 二次改写逐文件增益，10 到 1 秒倒计时仍绕过空灵混响并继续使用用户 voice 音量。没有角色的练习机器人通过 `botProfile.id` 保留独立语音身份；准时宝（`zhunshibao`）没有静态系统语音映射，读秒开始、剩余次数和 10 到 1 秒倒计时均走现有 `zh-CN` TTS，不能进入通用角色查找的西格莉卡回退。
- 角色技能 BGM 当前配置：主界面默认曲池包含 `home-default`（`main_bgm.ogg`）和 `home-main-bgm-1`（`main_bgm_1_once.ogg` + `main_bgm_1_loop.ogg`），达妮娅使用 canonical `denia-skill-default`（`bgm_*`），西格莉卡使用 `sigrika_intro_once.ogg` + `sigrika_loop.ogg`，爱弥斯“小爱出击”使用 `aemeath0_once.ogg` + `aemeath0_loop.ogg`，爱弥斯“远航星”使用 `lhl_intro_once.ogg` + `lhl_loop.ogg`，猪小仙使用 `matoya_*`，娜波摩使用 `busizhe_*`，千咲使用 `chisa_intro_once.ogg` + `chisa_loop.ogg`，长离使用 `changli_intro_once.ogg` + `changli_loop.ogg`，莫宁使用 `mornye_intro_once.ogg` + `mornye_loop.ogg`。旧达妮娅 slug 会在启动清理阶段迁移/删除，运行时 BGM resolver 只按 canonical `denia` 查找达妮娅技能 BGM；pending skill 与最新历史技能使用独立 `musicEffectType` 选择普通或派生槽位，派生技能的 `musicTrackId` 同时作为槽位 fallback 延续，避免演出中途回退到角色普通技能 BGM。未来爱弥斯商城技能 BGM 若替换“小爱出击”默认曲，继续写入 `musicSelections.skill.aemeath`；若替换“远航星”等派生技默认曲，则写入现有 `musicSelections.derivedSkill.aemeath[effectType]` 槽位。

- Settings > Audio persists optional per-channel mute flags under `audioSettings.muted`; clicking an audio row title toggles mute without changing the slider percentage, and moving the slider clears that channel's mute flag.
- Settings > Interface presents three player-facing theme choices in a three-column grid on desktop and mobile. Theme option labels are the direct style names without a repeated `主题：` prefix or explanatory parenthetical. Only the first option maps to the current Bright School theme; the two future theme buttons keep native button feedback and show the non-blocking `敬请期待~` toast without changing persisted theme state. On portrait mobile, the Interface tab gets its own taller `settings-tab-theme` modal state so the three theme buttons fit without an inner vertical scrollbar.

- Character detail BGM preview gives immediate loading feedback before first-use WebAudio decode, keeps the background-BGM pause request scoped to successful preview startup, and switches its play/loading/pause glyph, accessible label, state class, and `aria-pressed` together. Bright School uses a transparent hand-painted radio asset with a native 44px button aligned to its left circular control and a clipped live title over the center display; the removed Rough.js layer no longer contributes frame or underline rendering.
- 后台“音乐管理”只维护 `MUSIC_TRACKS` 静态曲目的显示名覆盖值；`MusicTrackSetting` 不改变轨道 id、类型、角色绑定或音频文件，玩家侧 `/api/music-tracks`、角色 BGM 选择、商城音乐展示和抽卡音乐奖项显示会使用合并后的曲目名称。
- 后台管理台通过 lazy `src/styles/admin.css` 与 `.admin-screen` 作用域维持浅色生产工具视觉；`src/styles/admin/polish/hud-isolation.css`、`forms-actions.css`、`tables-specials.css` 和公告管理样式会显式复位 `.primary-action`、`.secondary-action`、`.danger-action`、`.close-button`、`.modal-backdrop`、`.confirm-modal`、普通 `input`/`textarea`/`select` 和带 `setting` 片段的后台设置容器的 `clip-path`、暗色背景、霓虹 text-shadow/filter、绿色终端边线与 pseudo-element，防止 Startorch/HUD 的黑底、绿纹、斜角按钮和黑色输入框污染后台桌面端和移动端。
- 仇远（`qiuyuan`）的默认技能 BGM 是 `qiuyuan-skill-default`，使用两段式 `intro-loop` 播放：`/assets/music/qiuyuan_intro_once.ogg` 播放一次后接 `/assets/music/qiuyuan_loop.ogg` 循环；第二版技能 BGM `qiuyuan-skill-zhouwo` 显示名为“肘我”，使用单文件循环 `/assets/music/qiuyuan_zhouwo_loop.ogg`，商城商品图使用居中裁切 WebP `/assets/items/qiuyuan-zhouwo.webp`，不默认解锁但可作为商城音乐商品购买；启动内置商城 seed 会为已存在且 `imageUrl` 为空的 `qiuyuan-skill-zhouwo` 音乐商品回填该商品图，不覆盖后台已配置的非空图片，购买后会出现在仇远角色详情播放器的技能 BGM 选择中。
- 仇远（`qiuyuan`）的技能发动语音配置为两个候选音频：`/assets/voice/qiuyuan_skill_cast.ogg` 和 `/assets/voice/qiuyuan_skill_cast_1.ogg`；`skill-cast` 系统语音每次解析时随机选择一个，启动/对局预加载会展开全部候选路径。`game-start` 与 `sortie` 分别使用重新录制并统一响度校准的 `/assets/voice/qiuyuan_match_start.ogg` 和 `/assets/voice/qiuyuan_sortie.ogg`；读秒开始、剩余两次、剩余一次、10 到 1 秒倒计时及胜/负/和结果使用以仇远原声为参考、本地生成并统一校准的男声 Ogg，其中数字倒计时保持中性，已确认的角色化台词为“听风，入局。”、“还剩两次读秒”、“只剩一次读秒，抓紧脚步”、“落子声尽，胜负自明”、“一剑迟疑，满盘皆输……”与“下次再决胜负吧。”；剩余一次采用克制提醒的 D 版，整体放慢约 6%，两句间保留 600 ms 停顿。旧 Kangkang 生成器已经移除；全部事件继续通过 `CHARACTER_SYSTEM_VOICES.qiuyuan` 显式映射和预加载。
- 爱弥斯（`aemeath`）普通技能语音继续使用 `/assets/voice/aemeath_skill_cast.ogg`；远航星（`voyage-star`）技能横幅会通过 `skill-cast:voyage-star` 优先播放 `/assets/voice/aemeath_skill_cast_voyage.ogg`。
- 琳奈（`lynae`）已接入角色技能语音 `/assets/voice/lynae_skill_cast.ogg`，并配置出战、对局开始、读秒开始/剩余次数、10 到 1 秒倒计时和胜负和结果系统语音。
- 长离（`changli`）已接入角色技能语音 `/assets/voice/changli_skill_cast.ogg`，并配置出战、普通对局开始、读秒开始/剩余次数、10 到 1 秒倒计时和胜负和结果系统语音；五子棋模式开局会优先播放 `/assets/voice/changli_wuzi_match_start.ogg`。
- 千咲（`chisa`）已接入角色技能语音 `/assets/voice/chisa_skill_cast.ogg`，并配置出战、对局开始、读秒开始/剩余次数、10 到 1 秒倒计时和胜负和结果系统语音。
- 琳奈（`lynae`）的默认技能 BGM 是 `lynae-skill-default`，使用两段式 `intro-loop` 播放：`/assets/music/lynae_intro_once.ogg` 播放一次后接 `/assets/music/lynae_loop.ogg` 循环；该曲目默认解锁并跟随角色 BGM 选择/技能预览解析逻辑。

- 千咲（`chisa`）的默认技能 BGM 是 `chisa-skill-default`，使用两段式 `intro-loop` 播放：`/assets/music/chisa_intro_once.ogg` 播放一次后接 `/assets/music/chisa_loop.ogg` 循环；该曲目默认解锁并跟随角色 BGM 选择/技能预览解析逻辑。

- 长离（`changli`）的默认技能 BGM 是 `changli-skill-default`，使用两段式 `intro-loop` 播放：`/assets/music/changli_intro_once.ogg` 播放一次后接 `/assets/music/changli_loop.ogg` 循环；该曲目默认解锁并跟随角色 BGM 选择/技能预览解析逻辑。

- 莫宁（`mornye`）的默认技能 BGM 是 `mornye-skill-default`，使用两段式 `intro-loop` 播放：`/assets/music/mornye_intro_once.ogg` 播放一次后接 `/assets/music/mornye_loop.ogg` 循环；该曲目默认解锁并跟随角色 BGM 选择/技能预览解析逻辑。

## Dependency Notes

- `roughjs` is installed as a runtime drawing dependency for Bright School hand-drawn SVG/Canvas decoration, such as notebook outlines, stickers, circles, and annotation strokes. Character detail BGM previews import it for the stable campus notebook sketch layer.
- `exceljs` is installed as a browser-side admin workbook dependency for剧情教学脚本 `.xlsx` export/import. The library is isolated behind `src/admin/storyScriptWorkbook.js` so React components do not depend on workbook APIs directly.
- Tailwind CSS v4 is installed as a build-time utility CSS dependency through `@tailwindcss/vite`. The app imports `src/styles/tailwind.css` after `hud-components.css` and before the final theme layer; that entry imports Tailwind `theme.css`, project semantic tokens from `src/styles/tailwind/tokens.css`, and Tailwind `utilities.css` with the `tw:` prefix and `source("../")`, while omitting preflight/global resets so existing Bright School CSS contracts are not reset. Tailwind migration is now a staged Phase 1-7 route, with the durable full execution plan in `.trellis/tasks/07-01-tailwind-migration-roadmap/tailwind-full-migration-plan.md`: Phase 1 only records contracts and token scaffolding, Phase 2 pilots isolated admin/tooling surfaces, Phase 3 establishes UI primitives, Phases 4-7 migrate shared UI, player non-gameplay flows, Bright School tokens, and mobile safety layers in order, while Phase 8+ protected gameplay/skill work requires visual regression gates before starting. The first Phase 2 pilot is the admin-only audit table shell in `src/admin/AdminAudit.jsx`, which now consumes `AdminTableScroll` backed by `src/ui/primitives/ScrollArea.jsx` after removing the old `.audit-table-wrap` audit CSS; Phase 3 now has `ScrollArea` for overflow utilities, `AdminTableScroll` for all admin table shells, `Badge` for low-risk inline badge layout utilities, `EmptyState` for admin table empty-cell alignment/spacing through `AdminTableEmpty`, and `Button` for admin action alignment through `AdminActionButton`, while existing CSS still owns visual treatments such as colors, borders, spacing contracts, disabled states, shadows, and typography. Phase 4 has started with the narrow `ModalActionButton` wrapper in `src/modals/modalComponents.jsx`; `ConfirmModal` consumes it for danger/secondary actions, `MessageBoardModal` consumes it for the submit action, `AnnouncementModal` consumes it for simple retry/load-more secondary actions, `PersonalizationModal` consumes it for the save action, `MailboxModal` consumes it for the attachment claim action, `FriendsOverlays` consumes it for the duel-mode cancel action, and `UserProfileCard` consumes it for the profile report submit action while shared modal/action, mailbox, and friends/profile CSS still owns visual styling, list/detail layout, paper background, match-mode options, profile confirm panels, profile social actions, and mobile treatment. Room gameplay, board geometry, Pixi canvas hosts, skill-presentation marks/keyframes, player-facing button restyling, and final mobile gameplay controls remain CSS-entry owned until a later protected-surface migration has visual regression coverage.
- Phase 5 has started with the narrow `HomeActionButton` wrapper in `src/home/homeComponents.jsx`; `MatchModePicker` in `src/home/HomeScreen.jsx` uses it only for the match-mode cancel action, preserving the existing `.secondary-action` visual class while `Button` contributes the low-risk `tw:` alignment utilities. Home/modal/mobile CSS still owns match-mode layout, mode option buttons, spacing, artboard behavior, decorative imagery, colors, borders, shadows, typography, and responsive safety until a full home/lobby/commerce surface has focused desktop/mobile tests and visual checks.
- The player home Header no longer renders the lobby-wide online count. `HomeHeader` inserts the decorative `/assets/home/home-header-sigrika.gif` between the brand and action areas, with `/assets/home/home-header-sigrika-still.webp` selected for `prefers-reduced-motion`. Both resources keep the original transparent 800x800 canvas; shared Header CSS maps the stable five-frame alpha bounds `(150, 184)-(663, 704)` to a 514x521 visible layout box so positioning follows the character rather than transparent export padding. Bright School desktop keeps the small character beside the version, while portrait mobile uses a `brand mascot actions` grid with a dedicated 44px menu column and a larger mascot whose hands cross the Header bottom border. The decoration remains pointer-transparent and does not replace or overlap any action target.
- `npm test` excludes local Codex scratch folders such as `.codex-run/` in addition to worktrees, e2e tests, and stability tests, so temporary staged snapshots cannot be collected as Vitest suites during handoff.
- Impeccable design review automation is available as a project-scoped skill in `.agents/skills/impeccable`: `.impeccable/config.json` enables the detector hook, `.codex/hooks.json` preserves the Trellis prompt hook and adds Impeccable `PostToolUse`, `PRODUCT.md` records the product design register, and `.impeccable/live/config.json` points live mode at the Vite `index.html` shell. Codex 0.142 parses `.codex/hooks.json` with a strict schema, so the shared manifest keeps only the top-level `hooks` object and stores descriptive notes in system-design docs instead of the JSON file.
- Player theme metadata now has one source of truth in `src/app/visualTheme.js`: `VISUAL_THEME_OPTIONS` includes both available and future settings choices, while `VISUAL_THEMES` / `VISUAL_THEME_IDS` expose only enabled themes that can be persisted and imported. Settings renders from the full option registry, so future styles can be listed without duplicating labels in the modal. CSS refactors must preserve the root theme order `shared -> isolation -> theme-components -> bright-school -> mobile-adaptive`; board, Pixi skill overlays, point buttons, and Bright School effect files remain protected skill-presentation surfaces and should not be broad-selector cleanup targets.
- CSS round-2 inventory lives in `src/styles/cssLayerInventory.js` with tests in `src/styles/cssLayerInventory.test.js`. The inventory separates reorganizable shared domains from high-risk gameplay/room CSS, Bright School-specific overrides, final mobile safety layers, and skill-presentation protected files so later cleanup can stay scoped before visual and skill regression verification. Character detail music styling is registered as two import-only splits: shared shell/title, track-sheet, and motion files under `modals/character-music-player/`, plus Bright School shell and track-sheet skin files under `component-repairs/character-music-player/`. The closed title contains only the current music name; ordinary/derived identity stays in the open sheet tabs. Bright School keeps the closed shell transparent and renders the `920x200` radio PNG with `background-size: contain` inside `230x50` desktop and `min(210px, 58vw) x 50px` portrait owners, preserving alpha safety margins and intrinsic proportions. The title owner reserves 62px on desktop and 58px on portrait mobile so its clipping boundary ends before the painted waveform window. Its native 44px control is offset onto the painted left circle: play/pause use bright theme blue/pink, and hover/focus/active leave both the real button and inset key face transparent so the painted control is not covered by a second circle; active still uses a 2px depth shift without scaling. Character-detail nested overlays continue to disable backdrop filtering on both the interactive layer and static paint layer.
- CSS scalability cleanup records all-`src/styles` debt and expansion contracts in `CSS_DEBT_BASELINE`, `CSS_Z_INDEX_CONTRACT`, `CSS_MOTION_CONTRACT`, and `CSS_BREAKPOINT_CONTRACT`. The current baseline includes the admin story node settings window CSS, story workbook import feedback, frontend layout-polish contracts, the Bright School character-detail radio bitmap geometry/control alignment, Zahira shop owner, semantic auth header, split IRIS v2 owners, and exact-asset Semantic Ignition owner. The 2026-08-02 playback-control delta uses bright token-based play/pause glyphs, one targeted `!important` inheritance winner for the nested CSS glyph, and one consolidated transparent hover/focus/active inset rule in the existing Bright School radio owner; its net result removes 95 normalized CSS bytes without adding a CSS file, breakpoint family, motion, hardcoded color, or high-z-index debt. The preceding 2026-08-01 radio geometry delta remains recorded with its twelve targeted cascade winners. Earlier auth/IRIS deltas remain recorded explicitly. The baseline covers file count, CSS bytes, `!important`, hardcoded hex values, media-query files, reduced-motion files, and high z-index files as a non-growth guardrail rather than a visual target. New z-index values at or above `1000`, new motion-heavy families, and new media-query families must be registered or moved onto existing token/layer contracts so desktop and mobile expansion stays auditable.
- The 2026-08-26 social-card surface delta replaces the Bright School friends-row pale-blue literal with shared `var(--bright-sheet)` warm paper in the existing owner. Its 12 normalized CSS bytes add no file, `!important`, breakpoint, motion, reduced-motion, hardcoded-color, or high-z-index debt; `CSS_DEBT_BASELINE.totalBytes` is refreshed to `1450286` as the next non-growth ceiling.
- CSS round-3 and round-4 follow-ups are also encoded in `src/styles/cssLayerInventory.js`: `CSS_ROUND3_SHARED_SPLITS` records import-only shared-domain splits for `base/home-legacy-grid.css`, `base/home-stage-artboard.css`, `hud-components/pop-tech-terminal.css`, `mobile-modals/phone-house-resume.css`, `modals/character-opening.css`, `responsive/phone-portrait-room.css`, `modals/mailbox.css`, and `commerce/recruitment/board.css`; `CSS_ROUND4_REGRESSION_CHECKS` records the required static CSS, focused board/skill unit, and desktop/mobile Pixi visual stability gates. `npm run verify:battle-fixes` now includes CSS inventory/theme contracts plus BoardSkillEffects, BoardAmbientEffects, skill registry, skill SFX scheduling, and mobile point-confirmation tests.
- Full-repo CSS cleanup completion gates are tracked in `CSS_FULL_REPO_CLEANUP_VERIFICATION_GATES`: each stage runs CSS contracts, high-risk room/board/skill/mobile work runs `npm run verify:battle-fixes`, protected Pixi/skill presentation work runs `npm run verify:stability -- tests/stability/skill-effects.spec.js`, and final handoff runs `npm run check`.
- The 2026-06-27 full-repo CSS cleanup backlog has been executed on `codex/full-repo-css-cleanup`; remaining risk is broad page visual coverage because the repo has functional desktop/mobile Playwright stability checks rather than screenshot baselines for every UI surface. The desktop stability project uses the supported `1440×768` viewport instead of Playwright's undersized `1280×720` default so `DesktopViewportGate` does not replace the tested surface before assertions run. The shared profile dossier stability case opens both self resume and searched-user details, then checks 1440×768, 1600×900, 1920×1080, 360×800, 390×844, and 412×915 for tab/action contracts, all three tabs staying in one unclipped row, modal bounds, document/table overflow, card background/border/shadow winners, transparent structural rails, non-overlapping desktop visual header, static portrait semantic header, 80% contain portraits, desktop 1160px body cap, portrait 2×2 summary, and the single profile-body scroll owner.
- CSS tests that assert concrete rules behind import-only entries should expand imports with `readCssWithImports()`; raw entry reads are reserved for import-order or import-only assertions.
- Final mobile safety splits are tracked separately as `CSS_FINAL_MOBILE_SAFETY_SPLITS` because `mobile-adaptive.css` remains the post-theme override layer. `mobile-adaptive/mobile-profile-records.css` now delegates profile shell/hero, summary/results, character rows, and footer repairs to `mobile-adaptive/mobile-profile-records/` while preserving the 768px profile/resume cascade, `mobile-adaptive/phone-core.css` delegates to `mobile-adaptive/phone-core/` for match mode, shell controls, modal tabs, and scroll/detail/result safety, `mobile-adaptive/phone-gacha.css` delegates to `mobile-adaptive/phone-gacha/` for legacy gacha modal, stage, controls, and result dialog survival rules, `mobile-adaptive/home-utility-interactions.css` owns the final mobile Bright School utility-image button feedback override, Bright School final leaderboard cards delegate to `mobile-adaptive/bright-school-overrides/leaderboard-cards/`, Bright School portrait resume modal layout/settings tabs delegate to `mobile-adaptive/bright-school-portrait/resume-modal-layout/` and `mobile-adaptive/bright-school-portrait/settings-tabs/`, announcement modal base styling delegates to `modals/announcement/`, and announcement detail mobile sizing delegates to `mobile-adaptive/announcement-detail/`.
- Theme overlay splits are tracked separately as `CSS_THEME_OVERLAY_SPLITS`. `themes/shared.css` now delegates to `themes/shared/` for player theme tokens, theme settings controls, and player-surface wiring, `themes/theme-components.css` delegates to `themes/theme-components/` for outcome badges, skill chips, and replay row semantic colors, Bright School component repairs such as `foundation-home.css`, `warehouse-character.css`, and `notebook-polish.css` delegate to matching nested directories, Bright School portrait mobile `modal-shell.css` delegates to `mobile/modal-shell/` for shell surfaces and scroll/control rules, and Bright School portrait room `shell-header-menu.css`, `viewport-player-strips.css`, and `dock-actions.css` now delegate to matching directories under `themes/bright-school/mobile/room/` while preserving their positions before later Bright School repairs and final mobile safety layers.
- Gameplay room splits are tracked separately as `CSS_GAMEPLAY_ROOM_SPLITS` because these selectors affect live room controls. `room/actions-requests.css` now delegates to `room/actions-requests/` for room toggles/action bars, decision/scoring panels, timed request toasts, test tools, replay controls, and disabled action states, `mobile-room/portrait-room.css` delegates to `mobile-room/portrait-room/` for shared portrait viewport, player strip, board/dock, decision, and action-hint rules, and `room-terminal/players-timers-skills.css` delegates to `room-terminal/players-timers-skills/` for the Startorch battlefield player panel, timer, captures, skill chip, detail panel, and sheen keyframe skin while preserving their current import positions before later responsive and theme repair layers.
- Skill presentation splits are tracked separately as `CSS_SKILL_PRESENTATION_SPLITS` because these selectors own persistent board point and stone effects. `room/board/stones-skill-effects.css` now delegates to `room/board/stones-skill-effects/` for base stones, hidden/flip/double-move stone states, transient markers, protocol-ban and liberty-purge DOM marks, and related keyframes, while Bright School `quality-base/refinement-board.css` delegates to `quality-base/refinement-board/` for board point, SVG grid, row-slash, and stone-position guard rules; both preserve their import positions before later stone-effect, grid/scoring, and theme repair layers.

- The three-pass motion correction preserves visible interaction contracts while removing avoidable paint, touch-hover, and accessibility failures. Top-center toasts retain their three-second lifecycle; timed request progress uses left-origin `scaleX()`; final mobile utility-image hover is fine-pointer-only; skill-targeting keeps its color cadence without interpolating large aura shadows; chat/tutorial-record popovers stay mounted but inert while closed and use a reversible 160ms transition; skill-trait popovers enter from their measured arrow edge; and the generic mobile press layer no longer transitions filter or box-shadow. New reveal motion is limited to story reply choices, achievement toasts, the two result reward tiles, and the active settings tab panel, with 150-220ms transform/opacity timings and reduced-motion fallbacks. Result reward keyframes live in the focused `modals/result-reward-motion.css` owner; the recorded CSS delta adds one file and no `!important`, hardcoded color, or high-z-index debt.
- Desktop player windows now have a focused shell-level entry owner at `src/styles/modals/window-entry-motion.css`: ordinary windows enter over 260ms from a 16px/0.96 offset, data windows and nested details use faster 200ms variants, and mailbox/announcement master-reader panes stage at 40ms/70ms. Individual `translate`/`scale` properties preserve the animation without weakening Bright School's static `transform: none !important` anti-bleed contract. Every entry uses `backwards` fill so delayed panes keep their first frame but settled parent windows release transform components; nested fixed backdrops such as the House character detail therefore remain viewport-level instead of being clipped inside the parent window. The selector family is explicit, transform/opacity-only, and excludes story, character-opening, recruitment-cinematic, and mobile-sheet presentation; this product-directed first pass intentionally adds no reduced-motion branch.

## Achievement And Personalization Note

- 用户名注册/搜索校验允许中文、日文、韩文、半角英文、数字和下划线；按显示宽度限制为最多 8 个半角字符宽度，等价于最多 4 个中日韩字符或 8 个半角字符。注册页把用户名、密码和确认密码规范放在输入框 placeholder 中，用户开始输入后自然隐藏；失焦或提交时才在对应字段下方显示错误。登录与注册主提交按钮分别使用“开门！”和“登记入部信息”，密码可见性按钮保持 44px 点击区，但 Bright School 悬停只改变图标色，不再铺整块粉色底。 Legacy startup cleanup trims overlong existing usernames to the trailing 8 display-width units and adds a short numeric marker only when needed to preserve username uniqueness.
- 成就系统以 `Achievement`、`AchievementRewardAsset`、`UserAchievement`、`AchievementCounter` 和 `UserAchievementEquipment` 为核心模型；启动时 `ensureAchievementSchema` 会先于角色/商店种子任务运行，为旧 SQLite 自动补表，并为 `Character` 补 `source`、`cvName`、`cvUrl`、`illustName`、`illustUrl` 字段，为 `Decoration` 补 `source` 字段，为 `ShopItem` 补 `source`、`illustName`、`illustUrl` 字段。玩家侧 `/api/achievements` 返回合并后的成就列表与本次新解锁成就，`/api/me/achievement-equipment` 读写称号、徽章和用户名背景装备；成就目标本体由代码/种子维护，后台 `/api/admin/achievements` 只允许修改成就名、成就内容、奖励资产和排序，`/api/admin/achievement-reward-assets` 继续管理奖励资产并写审计日志。
- `/api/me`、`GET/PATCH /api/me/achievement-equipment` 会同时返回 `achievementEquipment` 装备 id 与 `achievementEquipmentAssets` / `equipmentAssets` 奖励资产显示数据；socket 登录用户、排行榜和社交用户列表/资料也会批量附加 `achievementEquipmentAssets`。前端通过共享 `UserIdentity` 在对弈玩家名、房间成员、排行榜、好友/黑名单、资料卡、观战列表和结算赢家名等完整用户展示点渲染称号、徽章和用户名背景；好友/黑名单列表行只展示状态、常用角色、用户名和操作入口，不再展示段位或积分，详细资料仍保留完整统计。好友列表和房间成员操作区不再展示未实现的“密谈”，保留操作在各自横向按钮区内等宽分布。
- 对局界面的房间成员列表复用 `UserIdentity` 显示普通用户名、称号和徽章，但显式关闭用户名背景/铭牌，避免桌面端固定成员列为铭牌槽预留宽度后截断合法用户名；对局双方主玩家条以及成员详细资料仍保留完整身份装饰。
- UserIdentity nameplates are rendered as fixed-ratio username badges: users without a nameplate keep natural-width text, while generic equipped nameplates use a `96px x 25.6px` base slot at `3.75:1`. The background image and horizontal safe padding scale together through scene-owned `--user-nameplate-scale`; username text uses shared `--user-nameplate-font-size: calc(15px * var(--user-nameplate-scale))` so it scales with the fixed badge while remaining independent from username length, and never emits `--user-identity-fit-font-size`. The fallback for extreme legacy names is ellipsis rather than per-name font shrinking. The nameplate background wraps only the username, stays centered, and must not return to parent-width stretching or left/center/right DOM slicing. Asset-owned exceptions may provide their own checked delivery ratio when their exact-ID CSS owner uses the matching fixed slot; PNGs should remain alpha-trimmed with `scripts/pngTrim.mjs` before final resampling.
- The application shell selects layout from current CSS viewport geometry only and never targets a device class. Full desktop layout requires at least `1440 x 768`; compact layout accepts either the rotatable short-side `320–480px` / long-side `568–1024px` range or the tall narrow portrait range with width `320–520px` and height at least `568px`, covering content areas such as `496 x 1047`. Phones, tablets, and desktop browsers with identical current dimensions therefore receive identical treatment; user agent, touch/pointer capability, Client Hints, and physical device-screen dimensions are not read. `DesktopViewportGate` reacts to resize and orientation changes and replaces route/audio/overlay rendering with the single accessible message `请用合适尺寸窗口进行游玩` whenever neither compact nor full-desktop geometry is valid; no secondary minimum-size sentence is rendered.
- Shared `UserIdentity` exposes equipped asset identity through `data-nameplate-id` and renders explicit background, pointer-transparent effect, and username layers without changing its public props or API payload. Ordinary image nameplates keep the static `96px x 25.6px` fallback. The built-in `reward-sigrika-spark-100-wins-nameplate` is an asset-owned exception in `hud-components/user-identity/semantic-ignition-nameplate.css`: its `1125 x 240` transparent raster uses a Sigrika-specific hand-painted open-starlight composition—an oversized open four-point sigil, deep-plum magical-ink username carrier, warm-gold rune curls, sparse mint-cyan star points, and a soft painted right tail—while CSS maps it to a `150px x 32px` runtime slot. A continuously visible alpha-following gold-violet rim carries the base illumination; separated sub-2px filaments move left-to-right through the open carrier, thin traces follow the upper/lower rune contours, paired lines leave the right tail, and sparse stars remain secondary accents. No motion paints a filled orb, solid sweep, mechanical ring, or full-surface haze. Continuous keyframes change only transform and opacity, the effect layer never participates in hit testing, and `prefers-reduced-motion` freezes a readable rim, sigil, filament, contour, and tail state. New bespoke nameplates add their own asset-ID owner; admin-created image-only nameplates require no code and continue using the generic fallback.
- Because Bright School and portrait/narrow-desktop safety layers load after shared HUD CSS and intentionally use broad `!important` text resets, the last-imported `mobile-adaptive/user-nameplate-final.css` owner restores only the three selected Sigrika, Danya, and Aemeath exact assets. All three keep the same scaled `150px x 32px` tag geometry, visible pointer-transparent local-effect overflow, and italic `800` UI-name treatment while preserving their own padding, dark contrast shadow, soft character-colored glow, and authored raster proportions; generic nameplates and surrounding student-ID layout remain unchanged. Legal eight-half-width or four-CJK usernames reserve `3px * --user-nameplate-scale` of paint-only inline-end space with an equal negative margin, protecting italic ink overhang without changing font size, tag size, text-safe width, flex centering, or legacy ellipsis behavior. The Semantic Ignition raster occupies Alpha bounds `x=110..1013, y=8..231`, Danya occupies `x=56..1068, y=12..227`, and Aemeath occupies `x=40..979, y=8..231`; the shared unclipped slot preserves their intended perceived height without stretching Danya's shorter painted subject.
- Repository-local Skill `.agents/skills/create-character-nameplate/` standardizes future character nameplate production in `new` and `refine` modes. It enforces text-first dossier research: supplied archive pages must be traversed through relevant body sections, tabs, accordions, anchors, pagination, and linked text entries; personality, biography, goals/conflicts, relationships, dialogue, plot development, abilities, meaningful objects/places/hobbies/foods, and achievement relevance are recorded as nine separate, unmerged rows with source locators and `found` / `absent` / `inaccessible` coverage before any visual-language card or concept may be produced. Images are secondary corroboration, and every chosen motif, palette/material decision, and motion verb must trace through textual evidence and interpretation to the visual result; inaccessible character-defining text blocks generation instead of being replaced by portrait guesses. After that gate, the workflow requires four structurally different hand-painted concepts plus an explicit human selection gate for new/major-redesign work, keeps reward/data wiring opt-in, and guides exact asset-ID CSS integration without reusing another role's motifs. Its deterministic validator reports delivered PNG dimensions, RGBA/Alpha bounds, four-edge safety margins, visible-height ratio, and declared username-safe-zone geometry; its task-local Vite preview harness renders the real `UserIdentity` across representative usernames/scales, while the capture script records `1440x900`, `1024x768`, and `375x812` in normal and reduced-motion contexts using Playwright Chromium or an installed Chrome/Edge fallback. The preview never enters production routes and does not replace final checks in real identity consumers.
- 内置成就“百次回响”使用 `mode_character_wins` 条件统计玩家使用达妮娅在 `spark`（星炬对弈）模式的胜场，达到 100 胜后解锁 `reward-denia-spark-100-wins-nameplate`，奖励资源为 `/assets/achievements/denia-spark-100-wins-nameplate.png`；`server/achievements.js` 与 `server/adminDefaultSnapshot.js` 保持相同种子定义。选定成品是 `1125 x 240` 透明手绘 RGBA：左侧为软压、非规则的偏心珠光梦境泡泡，中部为安静的蓝莓紫用户名载体，右侧以短玫红折光收束；浅钴蓝、薰衣草紫和玫粉折射表达虚空物质感，不出现黑色眼/传送门、裂口、黏液、触手、长分叉尾、角色肖像或烘焙文字。
- 达妮娅用户名背景只由精确 `data-nameplate-id="reward-denia-spark-100-wins-nameplate"` 命中。`hud-components/user-identity/denia-echo-nameplate.css` 将运行槽设为 `150px x 32px`，用户名左右安全内边距为 `40px / 25px`，维持暖白斜体 UI 文字、深紫阴影、粉紫柔光和既有省略回退；`denia-echo-nameplate-motion.css` 只漂移珠光高光、内部折射、短尾折痕和稀疏梦境光点，并通过提高这四个插画内层的最低/峰值透明度保证真实游戏中光效可见。连续关键帧仅改变 `transform` / `opacity`，不新增扫光、实心光球或无关光源；`prefers-reduced-motion` 下冻结为可读静态轮廓。
- 内置成就“飞行雪绒，出击！”使用 `mode_character_wins` 条件统计玩家使用爱弥斯在 `spark`（星炬对弈）模式的胜场，达到 100 胜后解锁 `reward-aemeath-spark-100-wins-nameplate`；`server/achievements.js` 与 `server/adminDefaultSnapshot.js` 保持相同的奖励/成就定义。选定的 v2 A 成品位于 `/assets/achievements/aemeath-spark-100-wins-nameplate.png`：`1125 x 240` 透明手绘画面用天青和粉色表达纸飞行信标，左侧是放大的折纸飞机，中部是深青安静用户名载体，周边有手绘蓝粉气流、稀疏方形数据光点和短右侧回卷；不使用机甲硬边框、径向光核、段位圆盘或脱离插画的终点徽章。
- 爱弥斯精确 ID owner 使用 `150px x 32px` 槽位和 `34px / 28px` 用户名安全内边距，冰白斜体 UI 文字配深青阴影与柔和天青辉光，并保留随 `--user-nameplate-scale` 缩放的 `-1px` 文字纵向修正。首页学生证是唯一需要整体定位修正的消费端：只对 `.home-player-plaque` 将整个 name-tag 右移 `12px * --user-nameplate-scale`，不把文字内对齐与整体位置混在同一全局平移里。动效只让三条分离纸飞机尾流、折纸纹理回声、上下轮廓细线、右侧成对尾线和少量方形像素在 `transform` / `opacity` 上流动，不出现实心光物体、全幅扫光、旋转圆环或密集扫描纹理；减弱动效时冻结为明亮可读的静态态。
- 内置成就“你给我吃了什么！？”由 `seedBuiltinAchievements` 在启动时按需创建，监听 `denia-rainbow-bean-candy` 道具使用事件；只有达妮娅接受并吃下彩虹豆豆跳跳糖后才会触发并获得 100 金币，35% 拒绝分支不会触发，不回溯统计成就上线前发生过的使用记录。
- 彩虹豆豆跳跳糖当前支持西格莉卡、达妮娅、爱弥斯和琳奈四名角色；`src/shared/rainbowBeanCandy.js` 统一登记道具 ID、支持角色与效果键，仓库目标选择和服务端效果常量共同消费这份契约，避免前端白名单漏掉已接入角色。西格莉卡合法使用必定成功，不设糖果使用失败剧情；其余三者由服务端按 35% 概率进入不消费、不生效的拒绝剧情；爱弥斯接受后获得 `aemeathRainbowMove` 临时状态，对局中她的每次普通落子会在交叉点播放约 520ms 的电子彩虹反馈：光源跟随棋子抖动后的实际视觉中心，七色细光沿棋盘横纵线向四个棋盘边缘传播，并以统一透明度遮罩从中心的高亮逐步衰减到边缘透明；沿途交叉点按距离依次点亮、亮度向外递减。明亮学园竖屏的全局 `max-width` 保护会由特效 owner 定点解除，避免光轨被裁成一格；效果不使用通用圆形彩虹环，也不改变棋子和围棋规则，并在她完成一盘有效对局后解除。琳奈接受后获得 `lynaeContraryVoice` 临时状态，只交换她的语音事件：读秒 10→1 与 1→10 成对倒置，剩余读秒 2↔1、开局↔开始读秒、出战↔技能、胜利↔失败，和棋及界面文字、计时与结算数据保持不变；完成一盘使用琳奈的有效对局后清除，结算房间通过 `completedItemEffects` 保留仅供本局胜负语音解析的完成时快照。四角色的普通糖果剧情按《彩虹豆豆跳跳糖剧情.docx》同步台词、旁白与玩家选项；达妮娅、爱弥斯、琳奈使用 `accepted-start` / `rejected-start` 双起点，西格莉卡第 1–7 次入口共用文档中的成功剧情并保留第 8 次黑化及对弈恢复的独立流程。旁白及文档标记“角色区域留空”的节点均清空 `characterId` / `speakerName`，播放器保留立绘区域的布局空间，但不渲染图片、姓名标签或占位文字；从角色对白切入旁白时同步清空。代码默认、四份部署快照及本地已发布内容保持一致，已有云端数据库仍须按剧情 key 显式同步。
- 新增内置成就“点亮语义！”使用 `mode_character_wins` 条件统计玩家使用西格莉卡在 `spark`（星炬对弈）模式的胜场，达到 100 胜后解锁 `/assets/achievements/semantic-nameplate.png` 用户名背景；`seedBuiltinAchievements` 会让管理员默认达成所有内置成就并标记奖励已发放。
- 启动初始化会在角色 seed 前运行 `cleanupLegacyDeniaCharacterData`：旧达妮娅 slug `danea`/`denea` 的用户选角和拥有权会迁移到 canonical `denia`，旧角色行会删除，引用旧 slug 的对局记录会删除，角色商品/抽卡/成就奖励目标会改写为 `denia`；公共角色列表会防御性忽略旧 slug，避免旧达妮娅再次出现在前台。
- Runtime stability note: room runtime uses lightweight `room:clock` / `room:patch` and full `room:update` paths, user/socket room indexes for resume and disconnect lookup, throttled persistence for chat/presence patches, forced persistence for key lifecycle updates, and room-code-specific persistence flush before close cleanup deletes a persisted room row so delayed upserts cannot recreate closed rooms.
- Development room test tools are non-production only. In development builds the room action area shows the test-tool group on desktop and mobile, and the server accepts these actions without requiring `ENABLE_TEST_ACTIONS`; `test-enter-byo-yomi` forces both room players into byo-yomi so countdown voice, timer UI, and clock recovery can be tested quickly. Production builds hide the controls, and the production server still rejects debug test actions even if legacy `ENABLE_TEST_ACTIONS` is set.

## Mailbox System Summary

- The 2026-08-04 mailbox visual delta keeps the existing six mailbox CSS owner files, holds every file below the 6000-byte guard, uses semantic theme tokens, removes 48 hardcoded hex matches, and records only the targeted Bright School button/modal cascade winners plus owner-local reduced-motion coverage required by the confirmed desktop/portrait redesign. It adds no CSS file, breakpoint family, or high-z-index debt; `CSS_DEBT_BASELINE` is refreshed explicitly for this bounded feature and remains the non-growth ceiling for later mailbox work.
- Mailbox is an authenticated player/admin HTTP feature. Players open it from the home top-right action area or the mobile menu, read admin-only text mail, manually claim one optional attachment, and can keep at most 20 messages.
- Admins send mailbox batches from the admin console to one user, all current users, or all current plus future users. Every new send requires a trimmed display sender of at most 40 characters; the backend validates it and snapshots it onto both `MailboxBatch` and each delivered `MailboxMessage`, including messages delivered later from future-eligible batches. Older rows with no stored sender are projected as `系统` instead of exposing the internal admin username. Item and coin claims update the existing inventory and progress systems.
- Player deletes are soft deletes on `MailboxMessage.deletedAt`; future-eligible global batches still see that delivery history, so a deleted mail is not recreated on the next mailbox summary or list refresh.
- The player mailbox keeps the same list/detail layout for empty inboxes; the left mail list shows the empty state while the detail pane remains reserved. Desktop uses a left-list/right-detail split, while mobile remains list-first and enters the selected detail with an explicit 44px back control. The unchanged player header has no subtitle or extra “全部邮件” filter/count row. Each list row is text-only and exposes title, sender, and compact time; times remain relative through six days and fall back to a local `YYYY/MM/DD` date, while unread/claimable/completed semantics stay in the accessible name rather than visible dots, icons, thumbnails, or status chips. Plain mail becomes visually completed when read; attachment mail becomes completed only after it is read and no longer claimable, so a read but unclaimed reward remains bright. Non-selected rows use separators instead of card borders, the selected row uses one restrained neutral full border, and keyboard focus remains a distinct blue outline.
- The transparent detail starts with title, sender, and full local timestamp, keeps delete as the existing icon-only secondary action beside the heading, and scrolls long Markdown-lite prose independently without a nested article frame, fill, hard shadow, runtime blur, rules, stains, or watermark. The header/main boundary uses a dashed divider; the master and reader each use the same 2px solid line-frame strength as the announcement center, while the article itself stays borderless and transparent. Incomplete rows use a slim warm-pink inset accent instead of a full mint fill, while completed rows remain dimmed. A real attachment sits independently at the lower left while the claim button remains at the lower right; claimed mail keeps a disabled neutral `已领取` action. Coins render as non-interactive icon tiles. Item attachments render a catalog/built-in icon tile, show quantities above one in a dark high-contrast top-right badge, and add a translucent dark-gray claimed overlay with a white check without hiding that badge. Clicking an item tile opens a focus-trapped, Escape-closeable detail dialog portaled to the application root; it shows only localized image/category/name/description, with no ownership, mail-quantity, claim-status, purchase/use, or internal-id rows. Desktop message switches remount the reader and stage header, body, then the attachment/action shelf from 44px to the right over 420ms with 100ms and 200ms delays; mobile keeps the shared pane transition and reduced-motion removes the staged entrance. The existing mailbox payload continues to project the safe catalog/built-in `itemDescription` through the current list response; no new endpoint, schema, multi-attachment contract, or shared shop/warehouse refactor is introduced.
- App-level overlays must keep `useOverlayState`, `App`, `useAppActions`, and `useOverlayActions` wiring in sync. `closeAllOverlays()` is reused by `match:found`, room resume, and replay entry flows, so a missing overlay setter can break matchmaking before the match-success/preload state is recorded.

## Stability Boundary Update

- 2026-06-27 stability/extensibility hardening keeps production on the current single Node.js room runtime. Multi-instance migration remains a future architecture boundary, but the code now exposes clearer seams: `server/roomQueries.js` can delegate active/watch room lists to an injected read model, `server/serverStartup.js` exports `SERVER_STARTUP_TASK_ORDER`, room-resume stability metrics include the `initial-connect` reason, character-target item ownership uses `publicUserAssets()`, skill effect image URLs live in `src/room/boardSkillEffectAssets.js`, and direct-duel incoming banner state lives in `src/app/useIncomingDuelState.js`.
- Startup preload / room resume race: `useStartupPreload()` now uses `shouldShowStartupPreload({ room: roomRef.current, matchSuccess: matchSuccessRef.current })` before setting `view="preloading"` after `/api/me`; a fast mobile `room:resume` that already restored an active room or pending match cannot be visually covered by the generic login preload route.
- Shared loading-page progress presentation stays driven by `AssetPreloadScreen`'s normalized real progress. The visible cream paper track is 20px high on desktop and 18px on phones; its full-width orange-yellow crayon fill, fixed-size scratches and paper gaps, and warm glow are revealed through `mask-size` with an equivalent `clip-path` fallback instead of progress scaling. The transparent square `public/assets/preload/orange-mascot.png` mascot remains three times the track height with `width: auto`, escapes later mobile-wide image max-width rules through component-owned `max-width: none`, and keeps its center on the reveal endpoint without flattening at 100%. Separate anchor, roll, and breath owners let meaningful progress use a 420ms ease-out position change plus clockwise 0–720-degree roll, then switch to subtle breathing after 600ms idle. The track reserves half a mascot at both sides so 0%/100% remain visible. Reduced-motion keeps the correct endpoint and revealed range while removing interpolation, roll, and breathing. Theme meter overrides no longer target `.preload-bar span`, preserving the shared paper-strip texture and mask owner in Bright School and future themes.
- Playable-ready prewarm boundary: `usePlayableReadyPreload()` schedules a home-idle client prewarm after authenticated home entry, while home match controls call `preloadPlayableReady()` on desktop pointer/focus intent and `startMatchTransition()` calls it again for the selected mode. The helper deduplicates module loading, skips Pixi for `standard` and `gomoku`, records lightweight `sigrika:playable-ready-preload` browser metrics, and is explicitly opportunistic: failures never block matchmaking or room recovery.
- 对局刷新/重连稳定性边界：`room:preload-ready` 是服务端 ack 的幂等协议，`BattleAssetPreloadScreen` 在未收到 `{ ok: true }` 前会退避重试并在 socket `connect` 后立即重发；`useRoomMemory` 会同时记住 active room 和 `match-preloading` pending room，`room:resume` 恢复到 `preloading` 玩家房间时重建 `matchSuccess` 并回到 `match-preloading`，从而重新加载本地资源并再次上报 ready。`connectGameSocket()` 先用 `autoConnect: false` 创建 Socket.IO 客户端，安装 `connect`/`room:resume` 等处理器后再主动连接，并在连接后立即 queue 一次幂等 `room:resume`，避免生产同源快速连接或移动端 polling/websocket 时序让刷新恢复只依赖单个 `connect` 回调。前端所有 full room snapshot 在进入 UI 前通过 `normalizeRoomSnapshot()` 补齐 `players`、`spectators`、`chat`、`game.points/history/captures/skillUses` 等安全默认值；`AppErrorBoundary` 包裹应用根，`view="room"` 但缺少 `room` 或 `user` 时显示恢复加载页而不是空白页面。
- Finished-room 恢复边界：`sigrika-last-room-code` 只保存未结束的玩家房间；玩家关闭有效结果或从 finished 房间退出时会持久写入 `sigrika-dismissed-result-room-code` 并清除 last-room。服务端 `room:resume` 对空 roomCode 不再自动拉起 finished room，只有显式 roomCode 才允许恢复历史结果，因此玩家离开结果页后刷新不会在房间回收前再次弹出同一结果。
- 技能演出与音频恢复边界：`BoardSkillEffects` 继续把 Pixi 作为 presentation-only 覆盖层，严格 CSP 环境下先加载 `pixi.js/unsafe-eval` 再加载 `pixi.js`；真实 `.board-effects-canvas` 是技能演出验收条件，不再渲染 DOM/CSS 替代动画。Pixi app 初始化和阻塞型 Pixi sprite 资源加载都会在技能横幅窗口内提前开始，只有两者都完成后才挂载透明 canvas 并进入可见播放，避免贴图未进 Pixi 缓存时由 `Sprite.from()` 读到空纹理或白帧；仅供结算后 DOM marker 使用的资源（如 Voyage Star crater WebP）仍属于非阻塞预热资源，不能延迟程序化 Pixi cast。协议接管和陨石擦除等临时 Pixi 残留层需要在演出结束前淡出，收尾清理必须先把 `.board-effects-canvas` 从 overlay host 摘除，再销毁 Pixi/WebGL app，避免 WebGL context teardown 在仍参与合成时露出底帧。Pixi 初始化、WebGL、贴图、renderer 或 ticker 异常只会标记 `data-effect-failed` / `data-effect-error`、清理 sound timers 和 canvas，并保持对局页面不白屏，准生产测试仍判该技能失败。本地 Vite dev server 不预优化 Pixi 入口，但会预优化 Pixi 的嵌套 CommonJS/conditional-export 子依赖以保留 ESM 互操作，避免开发期浏览器继续请求已经失效的 optimized renderer 文件，或直接加载 raw CJS 子依赖导致技能 canvas 没有挂载。BGM 加载会校验 HTTP `response.ok`、记录 decode/fetch 失败源，WebAudio 调度失败时使用 `Audio` fallback，并在 `room:update` 音频基线、socket 重连、`pageshow`、可见性恢复和首次交互后恢复当前曲目；组件级 `BackgroundMusic` 卸载时会立即停止 WebAudio/HTML fallback、清空当前曲目并废弃未完成调度，避免局部界面退出后残留 BGM。
- 生产静态边界：`deploy/nginx/sigrikago.conf` 固定正式 HTTPS 站点与证书入口，`deploy/nginx/sigrikago-routes.conf` 把 `/socket.io/`、`/api/`、`/health/*`、`/uploads/`、Vite hash 资源、普通 `/assets/**` 和 SPA shell 分开；静态文件与上传不再经过 Node。HTML/CSS/JavaScript/JSON/XML/SVG 由 Nginx gzip，Vite hash 产物使用一年 immutable；文件名稳定、部署时可能替换内容的普通图片/音频使用 `no-cache` 条件请求，避免浏览器继续显示旧按钮图或旧演出资源；`index.html` 同样使用 no-cache。SPA HTML 的 CSP 与 Helmet 同样保持 `script-src 'self'`，只通过 `worker-src 'self' blob:` 放行 Pixi 动态图片解码 Worker；`server/staticAssets.js` 在无 Nginx 的 Node 回退托管中维持同一缓存合同。`deploy/update-production.sh` 是 root 管理的单机更新编排：先通过 `set -a` 导出项目 `.env`，使检查、Prisma 和子进程与 systemd `EnvironmentFile` 使用同一配置；随后拒绝 tracked/staged 脏树和非远端祖先历史，先用私有权限做 SQLite 一致性备份，再恢复 Nginx 可读的构建权限、仅快进拉取，并以 `npm ci --include=dev` 确保生产环境仍安装 Vite 等构建期依赖，再在 `.tmp/production-update-*` 构建新前端并完成生产配置/Nginx 语法检查；然后停服执行迁移与完整非用户后台快照同步、切换完整 `dist`，最后 reload Nginx、启动单实例并在 60 秒内等待 readiness。停服后失败会尝试恢复上一份前端产物并重新启动服务，但不会强制重写 Git 历史或自动回滚数据库。
- 2核2G 部署门禁：`deploy/systemd/sigrikago.service` 使用直接 Node ExecStart、25 秒优雅停止、文件描述符上限和 1400M/1600M 内存压力/硬边界；`npm run verify:capacity -- --profile smoke|target` 通过隔离数据库和独立进程覆盖冷静态入口、空闲 Socket、并发房间、观战、action ack、20% 重连及 SIGTERM 恢复，并把客户端延迟与服务端 CPU/RSS/event-loop/持久化指标写入 `artifacts/capacity/`。
- 本地准生产稳定性验证入口是 `npm run verify:stability`：脚本先执行 Vite production build，再通过 `playwright.stability.config.js` 启动 `scripts/start-stability-server.mjs`，以 `LOCAL_PROD_STATIC=1` 让同一个 Express/Socket.IO 服务托管 `dist/` 并保留非 production 的本地测试能力；默认端口是 `4173`，可用 `STABILITY_PORT` 或 `PORT` 覆盖。稳定性服务器使用 `NODE_ENV=stability`，仅在该环境把 `/api/auth` 限流上限提高到足够覆盖桌面和移动两组注册/刷新流量，生产环境仍保持严格 auth 限流。稳定性用例覆盖生产包缓存边界、运行时资源失败不白屏、桌面/移动浏览器的对局刷新恢复、星炬与爱弥斯 `hidden-hand` / `voyage-star` 真实 Pixi canvas、finished 房间退出后刷新不重弹结果、以及对手重连不触发本机“操作频繁”toast；它减少云服务器反复部署验证，但最终上线仍需确认 Nginx/CDN 没有覆盖 Node 返回的缓存头。
- GitHub Actions 的 `CI` workflow 是远端质量门：pull request 和 `master` push 会执行 `npm ci`、`npm test`、`npm run build`、示例生产配置检查和 `npm run docs:system-design`，与本地交付门保持一致。
- App-level overlays are registered in `src/app/overlayRegistry.js`. `useOverlayState`, `modalDismissal`, `useOverlayActions`, and `App.jsx` derive overlay state, close-all behavior, and topmost-modal dismissal from that registry so new overlays do not need separate manual wiring that can break matchmaking or room recovery. `src/app/appShellProps.js` now builds the route and overlay prop contracts consumed by `<AppRoutes {...routeProps} />` and `<AppOverlays {...appOverlayProps} />`; `App.jsx` derives `show*` / `setShow*` props through `overlayPropsFromState(overlayState, overlaySetters)` instead of maintaining long JSX prop lists.
- Mailbox summary polling lives in `src/app/useMailboxSummary.js`, and recruitment ready-state polling/timing lives in `src/app/useRecruitmentReadyState.js`, keeping the app composition root focused on wiring rather than feature-specific polling loops.
- Room broadcast persistence policy is declared in `server/roomBroadcasts.js`: full updates and default patches force persistence, while clock and presence-style patch categories remain throttled/lightweight.
- Finished valid rooms do not close until `server/roomResultPersistence.js` has saved the result record; `server/roomCloseLifecycle.js` retries failed result saves before emitting `room:closed` or deleting persisted room state.
- Gameplay actions pass through the phase matrix in `server/roomActionPhaseGuards.js` before reaching move/pass/resign/skill resolution, so opening, skill preview, dead-stone marking, result review, and finished phases cannot be overwritten by generic board actions.
- Client patch-gap recovery is debounced in `src/app/socketHandlers.js`: duplicate gapped `room:patch` payloads for the same room/revision emit at most one `room:resume` per debounce window, room-code-level in-flight/cooldown state suppresses resume storms during presence reconnects, and full `room:update` / `room:resume` snapshots clear that debounce state. `server/socketGuards.js` separates recovery events (`room:resume`, `room:preload-ready`) from user actions, so excessive recovery traffic can be acked/rejected without showing the player-facing “操作频繁” toast that still protects `game:action` and chat.
- Legacy-to-structured asset sync only deletes structured rows whose `source` is `legacy`; feature-owned rows from achievements, gacha, mailbox, recruitment, or future flows stay owned by their source-specific paths and are merged for public projection.

## Story Script Summary

- 教学节点继续存放在现有 StoryScript JSON 中，并新增六个可选运行时字段：玩家落子节点的 `targetHighlightEnabled` 控制金色目标圈（缺省为 `true`），`wrongMovePointId`/`wrongMoveNextNodeId`/`applyWrongMove` 描述特殊错误落子分支及是否先真实落盘；局面节点的 `boardSetupLoadingEnabled` 控制是否经过 3 秒加载页（缺省为 `true`），`boardSetup.lastMovePointId` 只提供初始末手三角标记，不进入规则历史。错误分支与 `nextNodeId`、选项目标同属运行时可达边；普通错误仍只提示并停留，特殊分支可演示反击后用关闭加载页的 `board-setup` 立即复原。服务端归一化、后台表单/问题检查/预览路径和 v1 Excel 导入导出共同保存这些字段；旧 JSON 与缺少新增列的旧 v1 工作簿使用缺省行为。
- 教学多选题沿用剧情图本身判定答题反馈：选项目标直接回到当前题，或先进入 `nextNodeId` 回到当前题的 NPC 提示节点，视为错误答案并播放既有按钮不可用音效；同题中其余向前推进的选项视为正确答案并播放既有隐藏手暴露音效。普通剧情分支和固定单选回复不触发答题音效；带 NPC 提示节点的错误答案不切换当前问题节点，也不显示手动“继续”，而是在提示文本完整打出后原地恢复同一组选项，并保留该错误提示气泡，不重新播放原问题台词。
- Story tutorial scripts keep one script-level default `initialBoard`, but nodes may now use `type: "board-setup"` with a node-local `boardSetup` snapshot to enter or switch a full-screen local teaching battle inside a branch. This remains plain `nodes` JSON and does not add a table, DSL, or new graph model. `TutorialSessionModal` delegates board setup nodes to `TutorialBattleScreen`, which reuses `RoomBattleStage` with a local room snapshot; the admin current-step preview finds a reachable path from `startNodeId` through both `nextNodeId` and option targets before silently replaying board setup, moves, skills, and resign actions so branch previews inherit the correct board state.
- Teaching battle scripts also support in-battle dialogue flow nodes without changing the StoryScript storage model. `npc-dialogue` shows an NPC bubble in the full battle screen without making a move, while `player-choice` places reply options in the existing desktop action bar or portrait mobile “操作” dock and records the reply into the read-only battle chat log. NPC move/skill/dialogue nodes may carry `actionStartDelaySeconds`, `replyDelaySeconds`, `manualContinueEnabled`, `autoContinueEnabled`, `autoContinueDelaySeconds`, `actor`, `skillCharacterId`, and optional reply options. Battle node progression is authored as one advance mode: new admin nodes default to automatic progression and write `manualContinueEnabled: false` plus `autoContinueEnabled: true`, while manual mode writes the inverse and shows the post-node "继续" button without starting an automatic timer. The old boolean fields remain in the JSON so existing scripts round-trip through the API; runtime helpers collapse them to a single active mode. In automatic mode, blank `autoContinueDelaySeconds` means 1.5 seconds after `npc-dialogue` typewriter completion and 0 seconds for other battle nodes. The completed node then enters `nextNodeId` or reveals that node's reply options. Story and battle options may carry `transitionDelaySeconds`, shown in admin as "选择后等待", which waits after the user clicks an option before entering its target or closing the script；错误答题分支会保留当前问题节点，用目标反馈节点的文本替换气泡，文本完全显示后直接恢复该题选项。During progression waits the current surface stays mounted, choices hide after click, and duplicate controls are disabled. While choices are visible, the ordinary operation hint is hidden, the first enabled option receives focus, single choices stretch to the available width, and every choice button grows with its wrapped label. Desktop may scroll the bounded action group; portrait mobile leaves the choice group overflow-visible and delegates all vertical scrolling to the existing dock tab panel so the last option remains fully reachable. The action panel never renders free-text hints: it shows only concrete teaching buttons such as "继续", skill, counting, or resign, otherwise it remains empty while board highlights, reply options, NPC bubbles, chat, and toast feedback carry the instructional text；NPC 文本打字期间点击该功能/按钮区域会立即显示完整当前行，但不跳过后续落子、技能、复原或路由步骤。Admin preview may still expose a preview-only "立即继续" button for timer waits. `TutorialBattleScreen` keys node initialization by current node id so clearing a progression wait does not re-run the same node setup or hide newly revealed reply options. NPC actions run automatically after the configured delay;若台词必须在动作后出现，剧情数据使用无文本动作节点串联独立 `npc-dialogue`，保证棋盘结算先于气泡。Player move/skill/button nodes hard-highlight the scripted target/action, and wrong player actions show teaching toasts instead of sending room actions. `TutorialBattleScreen` now selects `DesktopRoomLayout` or `MobileRoomLayout` from the same responsive hook as real rooms, hides room utility controls and member panels, keeps only the exit/skip affordance, compacts chat messages by removing hand/time/role suffixes, and restores real room move sounds plus battle/skill BGM through `useRoomAudioEffects` and `BackgroundMusic`. Reply options no longer create a full-screen scrim or board-centered overlay; the active NPC bubble remains visible while the board stays fully readable, uses the NPC character palette as a restrained accent, and types body text progressively while showing the speaker name immediately; under Bright School, reply option hover/focus reuses the home utility-card lift, hard shadow and slight tilt via `src/styles/themes/bright-school/room/tutorial-choice-interactions.css` without changing teaching action buttons. Teaching action buttons stretch across the board action area with a light-green target affordance, mobile teaching action bars keep button-only controls filling the action dock width with only normal padding, mobile teaching button labels are centered inside the stretched button, timer panels reuse the normal `TimeBar` layout without tutorial-specific centering overrides, and no-character player panels preserve empty portrait/role/skill placeholders on desktop and mobile; desktop battle tutorials size the empty portrait frame to the same outer height as real character portrait frames, and both desktop and mobile empty portrait frames follow the player's black/white side background. A `story` node reached inside `TutorialBattleScreen` exits the battle view through the full-screen 3-second "正在收拾棋盘..." loading screen, disables the local teaching battle BGM before the transition, pauses global BGM during the transition, and resumes ordinary story playback from that node without letting the old battle BGM restart; entering or switching `board-setup` uses the shared `AssetPreloadScreen` template for the full-screen 3-second "正在激烈对局中..." loading screen. Story-to-battle, battle-to-story, and board-setup handoffs arm the loading state before the next frame is painted, so users do not see a one-frame battle shell, setup placeholder, or home screen flash between story surfaces.
- Tutorial battle reply options are button-only controls owned by the existing desktop action bar or portrait mobile “操作” dock. They must not create document-level overflow or cover the board; option labels wrap inside shrinkable spans, and each button uses content-driven height. Desktop may use one bounded vertical scrollbar on the action group; portrait mobile keeps that group overflow-visible and makes the existing `.mobile-tab-panel` the sole vertical scroll owner so the last button is fully visible at maximum scroll.

- 后台入口已从“新手引导”改名为“剧情教学”，但底层仍使用现有 `StoryScript`、`nodes`、`nextNodeId`、`options` 和可选 `initialBoard` JSON，不引入新 DSL 或新表结构。新版后台编辑体验由 `src/admin/AdminOnboardingStory.jsx` 承担，使用独立 `.admin-story-workbench-*` 样式命名空间和浅色生产工具视觉，不复用旧 `.admin-onboarding-*` 黑绿表单风格。模块包含按用途分组的脚本卡片库、自动只读流程图、当前步骤设置窗口、实时问题面板和预览区；每个脚本卡片提供单脚本 `.xlsx` 导出，当前脚本工具栏提供导入，导入只读取本系统同版本工作簿里的 `草稿-*` 工作表，校验通过后先展示变更摘要，确认后只替换当前编辑器草稿并标记未保存，不自动保存或发布。桌面工作台把脚本卡片库放在顶部横向选择带，主编辑区下方的自动流程图独占整行宽度，实时问题和预览摘要位于流程图下方的辅助区，避免脚本库、表单或预览常驻列压缩复杂分支。流程图由 `startNodeId` 出发遍历 `nextNodeId` 与剧情选项目标来判定可达节点，主线仍自上而下，选项分支按来源选项拆成横向泳道，深层分支嵌套在所属泳道内，已在主线或更早泳道出现过的共享节点不重复渲染，而是显示汇合标记。流程图区顶部保留主线/分支路径导览，新增步骤仍插入到当前选中节点之后并自动滚动高亮，结束选项与缺失目标用不同状态表达；实时问题面板按主线、分支泳道和孤立节点分组，点击流程图节点会在鼠标位置附近打开可关闭的节点设置窗口，桌面窗口挂在工作台坐标系内并随后台滚动区一起滚动，打开时会预留底部滚动空间以保证底部控件可达，窄屏则作为视口底部 sheet 显示；窗口内复用当前步骤表单，标题和操作区在窗口内部滚动时固定在顶部，并提供同一套“插入步骤”动作，流程图、路径导览、问题项和设置窗口保持同一选中节点。当前步骤预览若位于分支路径内，会先提示即将按哪条路径回放，避免误以为只预览孤立节点；只有从起点不可达的节点才进入“未连接步骤”，节点不可拖拽也不手动画线。草稿/未发布脚本可通过后台删除，已发布脚本可停用，默认 `onboarding.default` 作为系统脚本不可删除；服务端能力集中在 `server/storyScripts.js` 和 `/api/admin/story-scripts` 的 PATCH/DELETE 路由，并继续保留旧脚本可编辑兼容性。
- 剧情脚本保存仍是单次原子 PATCH，但请求体预算按路由隔离：只有 `PATCH /api/admin/story-scripts/:key` 使用 `2mb` JSON 上限，其余 API 继续使用 `64kb`；超限统一返回带 `REQUEST_BODY_TOO_LARGE` code 的中文 JSON。桌面自动流程图画布使用视口约束高度并独占纵向滚动，使原生横向滚动条始终位于画布可见底边；画布可通过键盘聚焦和滚动，`980px` 以下恢复自然高度，避免窄屏多层滚动冲突。

- Admin visual initial-board setup reuses `src/room/Board.jsx` with a temporary `createGameState()` board instead of drawing an admin-only SVG/button grid. The editor converts saved initial stones into shared board points, passes `stoneJitter={false}`, and only owns the black/white/erase tools plus save/cancel shell, so coordinate geometry, star points, stones, and click hit areas stay identical to the real battle board on desktop and mobile.

- 通用剧情播放器由 `StoryPlayerModal` 承担，`OnboardingStoryModal` 只是新手引导文案包装。播放器继续使用立绘区、打字机文字区和底部继续/选项区，桌面端默认保持 4:5:1 剧场比例，立绘、文本、选项区域与父级剧场窗口之间保留内边距 gutter，移动端保留大触控目标、同等父级内边距和父窗口内跳过确认层。节点切换时弹窗外壳、遮罩和布局区域保持稳定挂载，`StoryPlayerModal` 会在绘制前解析父层传入的新起始节点并重置打字机，立绘 `<img>` 也按有效节点和角色键控并 eager/sync 解码，避免连续点击“继续”时先闪出空剧情窗口、旧/新正文残影或旧立绘短暂停留。分支选项按钮默认使用浅绿色操作面，只有按下时才进入粉色确认态。节点级 `effect=long-text-compress-portrait` 会切换为长文本挤压立绘演出：文本区初始尺寸保持默认布局一致，随后随打字机内容增长并可把立绘区压到消失，文本区达到窗口上限后再启用自身滚动；该效果节点的打字速度为默认 1.5 倍。Bright School 移动弹窗主题有同等 specificity 的 effect 专用 `grid-template-rows` 覆盖，避免主题默认新手引导网格吞掉挤压演出。
- 剧情节点的立绘来源可包含角色目录之外的剧情专用候选，统一由 `src/shared/storyPortraits.js` 合成给后台下拉、后台预览和玩家侧 `StoryPlayerModal` 解析；这些候选只用于剧情节点 `characterId`，不改变玩家角色本体、出战角色或道具触发目标。当前内置剧情专用候选包括 `denia-rainbow-glow`，名称为“发彩虹光的达妮娅”，立绘复用 `/assets/characters/portraits/denia-candy.webp`。
- 应用级 overlay 新增 `storyPlayer`，排序高于仓库等业务弹窗；道具互动剧情会浮在仓库上方，玩家故事遮罩拥有高于普通 `.modal-backdrop` 与最终移动弹窗兜底层的专用层级。`item-character-use` 触发的脚本始终进入通用 `StoryPlayerModal`，即使开场使用 `player-choice` 节点也不能被非 `story` 节点探测误判为棋盘教学；只有教学脚本才进入 `TutorialSessionModal`。打开通用播放器会先清掉旧 `onboardingStory` 可见状态，跳过/完成/关闭和手机返回会同时关闭 `storyPlayer`、旧 `onboardingStory` 并清空当前脚本，关闭后再露出仓库。`useOnboardingStory()` 仍负责自动新手引导和手动回放，但打开的是同一个通用播放器。
- 后端新增 `StoryScript` 通用模型和 `server/storyScripts.js` 领域层。脚本包含 `key/title/triggerType/triggerParamsJson`、草稿节点 JSON、发布节点 JSON 和发布时间；发布时校验起始节点、唯一节点 id、正文非空、节点演出效果枚举、选项出现时间非负数字、选项选择后等待非负数字和至少一个结尾节点，并限制同一触发点最多一个已发布脚本。节点选项允许 `nextNodeId` 为空，表示选择该选项后关闭剧情窗口；非空选项目标仍必须指向当前脚本内存在的节点。每个选项可配置 `revealDelaySeconds`，为空时等正文展示完毕后出现，数字则从进入当前节点开始计时后单独出现；也可配置 `transitionDelaySeconds`，为空时选择后即时进入目标。对弈节点还会规范化节点推进字段 `manualContinueEnabled`、`autoContinueEnabled` 和 `autoContinueDelaySeconds`。
