Bright School is now organized as an import-only entry file plus layered files under `src/styles/themes/bright-school/`: `base.css`, `gallery-polish.css`, `surface-contracts.css`, `component-repairs.css`, and `qa-guard.css`. `surface-contracts.css` delegates explicit inherited-HUD cleanup and known surface contracts to `surface-contracts/` subfiles; it replaces the old broad fallback stack and must use real owner selectors. Routine component repair work should happen in the focused files under `component-repairs/`; systemic safety fixes should go into the focused guard file that matches the surface.
- Cross-theme semantic component states live in `src/styles/themes/theme-components.css`. It owns timer-state color contracts, result badge colors, replay outcome row colors, and skill-chip semantic states so future skins inherit game meaning before adding theme-specific polish. `src/styles/themeContract.test.js` also keeps volatile room/replay semantics in the final theme tree, including replay outcome hover/focus states, result badge colors, zero-use gray skill chips, Bright School targeting glow keyframes, and centered scoring/dead-stone marks.
- Player-facing theme expansion now has a registry contract in `src/app/visualTheme.js`: `VISUAL_THEME_IDS`, `VISUAL_THEME_CLASS_PREFIX`, `visualThemeScopeSelector()`, and `visualThemeCssImportPath()` derive the theme id list, app-shell scope, and CSS entry path from one source of truth. `src/styles/themeContract.test.js` verifies every registered theme is imported from `src/styles/themes.css`, has a scoped CSS tree, and keeps `_new-theme-template.css` aligned with the same contract, so future theme work fails fast when the JS settings list and CSS entry map drift.
- Browser smoke regression coverage now starts in Playwright. `playwright.config.js` serves the Vite app and runs `tests/e2e/ui-regressions.spec.js` through the local Chrome channel, while `.gitignore` excludes `playwright-report/` and `test-results/`. The current E2E suite verifies app boot plus the critical CSS contract strings for replay outcome rows, result badges, spent skill chips, skill targeting glow, and scoring marker repairs. `playwright.stability.config.js` is the production-like browser layer: it serves the built `dist/` through the Node server on port `4173` by default, with `STABILITY_PORT` or `PORT` available for local port conflicts; it runs desktop and mobile Chromium projects, verifies hashed/static runtime cache headers, checks that broken audio/effect assets do not blank the app, and creates a real Socket.IO room to smoke-test page reload recovery.
- Documentation text that may contain Chinese should be updated through `apply_patch` or `npm run docs:utf8 -- ...` instead of PowerShell `Set-Content` / `Out-File`. `scripts/write-utf8-doc.mjs` reads and writes UTF-8 through Node, verifies the write round trip, and fails if Unicode replacement characters are introduced; `docs/systemDesignHtml.test.js` also guards the generated Markdown/HTML pair against replacement-character encoding damage.
- Bright School theme files can accept external designer addenda as append-only CSS blocks at the end of `src/styles/themes/bright-school.css` or, for high-frequency polish, in the Bright School folder layers. These addenda must stay scoped to `.theme-bright-school` selectors so they do not affect admin views.
- Bright School inherited-HUD cleanup now lives in `src/styles/themes/bright-school/surface-contracts.css`. The main source of visual mixing is the shared HUD/base layers (`hud-components.css`, `home-terminal.css`, `room-terminal.css`, `commerce-settings.css`, and `room-terminal.css`) that define neon shadows, clipped corners, skew transforms, and pseudo-element labels before the theme layer. `hud-components.css` is an import-only compatibility entry that delegates HUD hardening, narrow HUD tweaks, pop-tech terminal polish, handbook readability, and character-chain badge rules to `src/styles/hud-components/`. Bright School clears inherited HUD artifacts with explicit `.theme-bright-school` scoped owner selectors for icon buttons, utility entries, handbook/detail rows, board/player pseudo-elements, timers, skill chips, action bars, and announcement rows.
- A Bright School component repair layer follows the explicit surface contract layer through import-only `src/styles/themes/bright-school/component-repairs.css`. It restores intentional notebook details through `component-repairs/` subfiles for foundation/home, shop, list/profile, warehouse/character surfaces, room board, chat, and final notebook polish. These files cover the shop receptionist panel and mascot overflow, paper-style scrollbars, shop item image mounts, decoration-category cleanup, warehouse target selection grid, lobby title and online tags, current-user ranking row, watch-empty states, friend-search inputs, owned-decoration spacing, character-detail contrast and portrait shadow, room-code labels, board point colors, pastel skill gradients, washi tape, binder rings, candy stones, and final lobby scrapbook fixes. Browser comment blue outlines are inspection overlays, not theme styling, and should not be treated as CSS evidence.
- Bright School's late guard layers are split under `src/styles/themes/bright-school/` while `qa-guard.css` remains import-only for compatibility. `quality-base.css` owns box model, overflow, text clarity, focus, touch targets, broad visual refinement, and reduced-motion order through `quality-base/` audit and refinement subfiles. `commerce.css` owns shop, warehouse, profile, and compact shop-detail polish through `commerce/`; `component-repairs.css` owns post-contract repair order through `component-repairs/`; while `mobile.css` owns portrait mobile ordering through `mobile/` subfiles for home shell, modal shell, commerce/warehouse, house/profile, lists/settings, import-only room, motion, and late fixes. The portrait battle-room rules under `mobile/room.css` are further split into `mobile/room/` subfiles for shell/header/menu, player strips, board stage, dock/action controls, record dialogs, touch feedback, modal sheets, and flat control cleanup. These rules remain scoped to `.theme-bright-school.theme-bright-school` and must not bleed into admin screens.
- Shop item cards stay compact in the storefront: cards show artwork, item name, purchase metadata, price, and the buy button, while longer descriptions move into `ShopItemDetailDialog`. Clicking a real shop card outside its buy button opens the nested detail modal; the buy button stops propagation and keeps the existing purchase flow. The detail modal now keeps only the description and ownership/status row, with purchase-limit and price details left on the card itself. Owned character/decoration details display `已持有`, owned status rows use the light-green Bright School treatment, owned card buttons use a light-green disabled style, sold-out buttons use gray, and Bright School decoration previews remove extra stone shadows.
- Bright School lobby polish keeps the scrapbook labels readable and the player plaque cleaner: the manual and match image buttons use larger high-contrast rounded labels, `部员手册` on the pink manual sticker and `匹配对局` on the green match sticker, so users can identify each large image button at a glance. The home title uses the thicker rounded `Arial Rounded MT Bold`/`Microsoft YaHei UI` stack for better weight, and the home player avatar mount removes the inherited paper background, border, and box shadow so only the chibi portrait remains inside the user plaque.
- `home.css` owns the lobby main-panel background asset, stage transparency, generated student-ID player plaque shell, and disabled legacy paperclip pseudo-elements. Bright School `room.css` owns player labels, room side tags, board coordinate cleanup, and battle/replay readability, while the shared `src/styles/room.css` is an import-only live-room entry whose concrete rules live in `src/styles/room/`. Bright School `modals.css` owns handbook, decoration, settings, and modal surface cleanup, while the shared `src/styles/modals.css` is an import-only modal entry whose concrete rules live in `src/styles/modals/`. `mobile.css` is an import-only entry for phone portrait, narrow tablet, modal scroll regions, and mobile room layout, with concrete rules in `mobile/`. `effects.css` owns skill targeting glow, board scoring marker repair, and related keyframes.
- Bright School handbook cleanup keeps the player manual closer to a school scrapbook: locked character slots use a gray unavailable-card treatment and no sortie flag button, deployed characters show `[出战中]` on a light-green label, the decoration reset control uses a refresh icon button with an accessible label, decoration section header/list wrappers drop inherited dashed frames/backgrounds, selected decorations use a light-green state, and stone preview images inside decoration chips remove extra frame/background/shadow.
- Bright School has focused scoped cleanup blocks for the latest handbook/settings/lobby review notes. They remove residual decoration-section wrapper frames and pseudo-element backgrounds, shrink the decoration reset icon button to match the section label height, enlarge the `[出战中]` badge, clear the settings panel's outer border while preserving inner controls, give matching portraits a warm scrapbook shadow, recolor the lobby player plaque with the sortie-character-inspired pastel palette, and remove the leaderboard helper text box. These blocks remain scoped to `.theme-bright-school.theme-bright-school`.
- Bright School also explicitly re-clears the lobby stage content and handbook decoration wrappers after the surface contract layer. The main panel itself keeps its device-specific WebP background board; only `home-main-panel.home-terminal-main::before/::after` and the inner home stage return to transparent backgrounds so cards, image buttons, and navigation sit on top of the supplied board art. `house-modal > owned-decoration-section > owned-decoration-header/list` force all dashed wrapper borders and pseudo-element decorations off while leaving the individual decoration chips styled; `match-portrait` keeps the warm scrapbook shadow and restores the original `match-hop` vertical animation, with `prefers-reduced-motion` still allowed to suppress motion.
- Bright School room/replay cleanup lives in `bright-school/room.css`. Player info username buttons are reset to text-only controls without card backgrounds or borders, room header side tags follow Go stone semantics (`black-side` uses black fill with white text, `white-side` uses white fill with dark text), and board coordinate rows/columns explicitly clear inherited paper backgrounds so coordinates sit directly on the board surround instead of on white pills.
- Mobile room headers collapse the three room utility controls (message board, settings, coordinates) into one options menu button, matching the home mobile header pattern and leaving the replay title/room badges more room on narrow screens. The move-number display toggle is no longer exposed in player-facing room headers because decorated and special-effect stones can make raw move numbers hard to read. The opened mobile menu uses a fixed inline width with icon+label rows so global 44px touch-target rules cannot compress the menu into a narrow icon column.
- Mobile room exit is also a header-level icon button on phones, placed immediately to the left of the integrated options menu. The replay action dock hides its old text exit button on mobile, and dock buttons center their icon/text content inside fixed touch targets.
- Mobile room chat removes the separate in-panel exit-room button; the chat trigger aligns to the right side of the chat panel, while desktop chat still keeps the trailing exit action. Mobile room header option menus stay on the room UI layer instead of floating above active modal overlays, while message-board close buttons keep the normal shared modal close-button stacking. Bright School mobile room controls explicitly remove box shadows, drop shadows, and text shadows from room header tags, player labels/chips, timers, capture chips, dock tabs, action/replay buttons, menu buttons, chat controls, and skill chips so the battle screen reads flatter and less visually heavy; board point buttons and stone/current-move visuals remain owned by board styles. Replay/action buttons default to a single centered icon row on mobile and only switch to icon-plus-label rows when an action label is present.
- Bright School mobile action docks keep the individual action button borders, but the integrated action-bar container itself has no outer border, outline, shadow, filter, or background treatment so the button row reads as separate touch targets instead of one framed panel.
- A final Bright School mobile guard in `mobile-adaptive.css` uses higher-specificity room selectors for controls that older Bright School layers also mark `!important`: capture/dead/overclock chips, timer fills, spent skill chips, replay controls, action buttons, chat controls, and the mobile menu. The same guard lets the chat popover escape the dock panel while open, raises the open room options menu above player strips but below modal backdrops, and keeps the message-board close button on the shared modal close-button top-right contract without the hard notebook shadow.
- Replay controls render with an explicit `action-bar replay-bar` class so the mobile guard can target replay-only chrome. On Bright School mobile, the replay control strip, replay buttons, and `replay-step-indicator` remove their panel background, border, outline, and shadow instead of inheriting the hard notebook frame used by generic action bars. Compact mobile replay counters hide the decorative replay icon and center the `current/max` hand text so the number reads balanced inside its touch cell.
- Mobile battle rooms use a fixed full-screen game-console layout on phones: the room shell stays at `100dvh`, player strips use a bounded `--mobile-room-player-strip-height`, the board stage keeps a square `aspect-ratio: 1` inside the remaining center viewport, and the bottom dock uses compact tabs plus a capped operation hint so actions, members, and chat stay reachable without covering the board. Portrait player strips keep a larger fixed avatar column, a flexible identity/capture column, and a bounded timer/skill column with visible row gaps so names, rank/rating, captures, clocks, and skill chips read as three balanced zones. Mobile identity metadata stays on one row with the larger username on the left and rank/rating tags on the right; rank/rating pills use inline-flex centering so their text remains vertically centered in tight mobile strips. Bright School mirrors the shared mobile contract with tighter 375px/393px portrait variables and flat player cards, preserving portrait/result badges, names, rank/rating, captures, timers, timer tracks, skill chips, and action labels while preventing normal play controls from overlapping board points.
- Trellis frontend quality guidelines now capture the same mobile battle layout contract in `.trellis/spec/frontend/quality-guidelines.md`: future mobile room work should assert bounded player strips, square board stages, capped docks, and Bright School parity in `src/room/RoomScreen.test.js` before shipping.
- Replay list rows use the viewed/current user's result state for final coloring across themes: user win rows are light green, user loss rows are gray, and draw rows are pale yellow. Room portrait result badges keep the winner badge red in both text and border so victory is visually distinct from loss/draw. Bright School has high-specificity theme and QA guards, so `src/styles/themes/theme-components.css` carries Bright School-scoped final selectors for replay rows and winner badges after the generic outcome rules; this prevents the theme reset from masking outcome backgrounds or forcing the `胜` text back to the default ink color.
- Mobile replay cards place the move count in the top metadata row between the date/time and result text, while the black and white player cells stay on the second row. The desktop replay table keeps its normal column layout.
- Mobile replay lists in both personal profile/history dialogs and nested house dialogs are ordinary auto-row card flows: the heading is hidden, rows use `grid-auto-rows: auto` with `align-content: start`, and the list must not inherit the leaderboard's pinned-footer `grid-template-rows: minmax(0, 1fr) auto` contract.
- Bright School lobby canvas cleanup now lives under the `bright-school/home.css` import tree and targets the exact `main.home-screen.home-terminal-screen > section.home-main-panel.home-terminal-main > section.home-grid-featured.home-stage` chain. This removes residual solid fills and pseudo-element paper backgrounds from the inner lobby stage while the parent main panel paints the supplied desktop/mobile WebP background board. The focused `home/` subfiles own the current-user student ID plaque, disabled legacy paperclip pseudo-elements, background-board material, manual label, and responsive lobby adjustments.
- Bright School portrait mobile fit is handled by the import-only `bright-school/mobile.css` entry and its `bright-school/mobile/` subfiles. This layer turns the home lobby into a single-column scroll page, removes the hanging-ID absolute positioning on phones, keeps the match and house-manual image entries inside fixed mobile-height cards, keeps all player-facing modal windows within `100dvh`, assigns internal scroll regions to shop/warehouse/house/leaderboard/friends/settings/message/watch/profile content, and preserves 44px touch targets without changing modal JSX. The house manual uses a fixed mobile grid shell (`header`, stats, internally scrolling character roster, bounded decoration picker) so roster cards and decoration chips cannot grow beneath the modal frame; stat help tooltips wrap inside the viewport instead of increasing horizontal scroll width. The same layer also owns Bright School mobile room layout: the room shell is fixed to `100dvh`, the header and player strips are compressed, the board size is calculated from remaining viewport space, and the tab dock reserves enough height for both operation hints and labeled game-action buttons instead of clipping the bottom row; mobile room function tabs use background-color changes for selected/pressed feedback and opt out of press-offset transforms because the flat mobile treatment removes button shadows.
- Bright School mobile lobby and utility windows have an additional compact pass for 393px-wide phones: the home header collapses message/settings/admin/logout actions behind a single options menu, hides the lobby debug status, and keeps the brand/online tag in a stable grid. The opened home options menu uses a fixed icon column plus `max-content` text column, with `white-space: nowrap` and `word-break: keep-all`, so the 留言/设置/退出 labels never split across lines. Resume stats stay on one line except for aggregate record summaries: profile record cards split total games onto the first line and win/loss/draw counts onto the second line, while character-record rows use separate right-aligned total/win/loss/draw/win-rate columns so repeated rows read as a table without vertical dividers. The `履历` modal embeds selected-mode character records below the recent-result marker row as `.resume-character-records`, reusing the same compact row structure instead of opening the old nested character-record dialog from the stat card; the embedded character record rows are sorted by total games descending, the section takes the remaining resume modal height on portrait phones, and the row list owns its vertical scroll container instead of letting rows continue beneath the modal frame. The manual character list uses three compact cards per row to leave vertical room for decoration display, decoration selection uses icon-sized chips, and character detail art removes mobile drop shadows. Character record lists use internally scrolling rows with compact avatar/name plus right-aligned total/win/loss/draw/win-rate columns; every stat cell keeps `white-space: nowrap`, `word-break: keep-all`, and tabular numbers so values align without vertical dividers. Leaderboard, replay, friends, and watch lists switch from wide horizontal tables to mobile cards; leaderboard cards use tighter rank/avatar/identity/score lanes, keep rating as the main right-side number, and stack a compact win-rate pill with explicit win/loss/draw chips (`胜/负/和`) in the same metrics lane, while "我的排名" mirrors the same compact row rhythm instead of becoming a sparse panel. The mobile leaderboard grid uses `minmax(0, 1fr) auto` rows so the scrollable list takes remaining space and the pinned current-user block shrinks to its label plus card instead of inheriting the old 220px desktop table row. Modal close buttons use one shared 44px size and 12px top-right inset across base, mobile, and Bright School layers. Toolbar close buttons, such as the watch-list close beside refresh, stay in the header action row as the rightmost same-size control so they do not cover sibling buttons; resume close buttons likewise stay grouped with the coin capsule, but the whole resume action group is absolutely anchored to the header's top-right corner on desktop and mobile; the resume wallet group must stay on one line and shrink its capsules instead of wrapping. The resume title reserves right-side space with a responsive padding so the fixed action group cannot overlap the heading. Bright School player plaques now keep the stats panel in a bounded, shrinkable column; mode, rank, and rating cells are centered and sized from the stats panel container so desktop and phone layouts do not clip the rank/rating text while the name takes the remaining space. The base home stage no longer forces a fixed 1180px minimum width. Between 701px and 1180px desktop width, or in low-height desktop windows, a final Bright School guard in `mobile-adaptive.css` switches the home stage to named grid areas (`player`, `manual`, `utility`, `match`) and resets the scrapbook entries to static positioning; below the narrower fallback threshold it becomes a single-column scroll layout. The Bright School phone layer now keeps friend and watch lists outside the old wide-table overflow group, while the final guard sits after the theme imports to protect the single-line stats rule, character/decoration shop cards, mobile title text, circular top-three leaderboard badges, compact pinned ranking cards, and small decoration picker chips from older desktop theme repairs. The same guard lowers the home options menu behind active modal backdrops so it cannot cover modal close buttons.
- Mobile friend rows show status, portrait, a centered username/stat text group, and the gear action in one compact row; rank and rating sit directly below the username inside that centered group. Desktop social rows keep the table-like column layout through `display: contents` on the mobile grouping wrappers.

当前音频系统主要由 `src/shared/musicLibrary.js`、`src/shared/audioScheduling.js`、`src/shared/boardAudio.js`、`src/shared/voiceEffects.js` 与 `src/audio/` 下的播放运行时组成。

- `src/audio/audioSettings.js`
  - Owns default audio settings, localStorage loading, per-channel volume calculation, and finite-number sanitization for persisted percent values.
  - Audio settings also support optional per-channel mute flags under `muted`; `audioVolume()` returns `0` when `muted.master` or the requested channel flag is true, while keeping the stored 0-100 slider percentage unchanged.
- `src/audio/backgroundDucking.js`
  - Owns explicit scoped BGM duck requests and subscribers for authored non-voice scenes such as the recruitment cinematic. Voice and TTS playback do not participate in this registry.
- `src/audio/audioRuntime.js`
  - Owns browser Web Audio constructor detection so playback submodules can no-op safely outside browser audio environments.
- `src/audio/proceduralSounds.js`
  - Owns generated short SFX such as countdown beeps and doorbell tones; `src/audio/playback.jsx` keeps compatibility re-exports for existing callers.
- `src/audio/backgroundMusic.jsx`
  - Owns the React `BackgroundMusic` component, BGM Web Audio scheduling, intro-to-loop timing, decoded BGM buffer cache, autoplay resume fallback, and reconnect recovery guard. `src/audio/playback.jsx` keeps compatibility re-exports for existing callers.
- `src/audio/effectPlayback.js`
  - Owns effect preloading/decoded cache, effect Web Audio playback, HTMLAudio fallback, no-browser no-op protection, board sound routing, and named UI interaction playback helpers. Fixed asset constants live in `src/shared/audioAssets.js`, including the dedicated home entry effects `ui_resume_open.ogg`, `ui_warehouse_open.ogg`, `ui_watch_open.ogg`, `ui_friends_open.ogg`, `ui_leaderboard_open.ogg`, and `ui_iris_database_open.ogg`; `src/audio/playback.jsx` keeps compatibility re-exports for existing callers.
  - `src/app/InteractionFeedback.jsx` installs document-level interaction feedback: enabled button-like controls play the confirm SFX, disabled/locked controls play the unavailable SFX, and the unavailable target receives a `ui-unavailable-shake` animation whose duration matches `ui_unavailable.ogg`. The unavailable animation restarts by removing the class and re-adding it on the next animation frame, avoiding synchronous layout reads on high-frequency disabled taps. Specialized interactions can set `data-ui-sound="none"` to avoid double-playing the generic confirm cue. Room board interactions, player-info panels, the room title/info stack, and replay control bars are quiet zones for generic confirm SFX so normal moves, high-frequency info taps, and replay stepping do not fire UI prompt sounds; replay disabled buttons can still fire the unavailable SFX.
- `src/audio/playback.jsx`
  - Owns voice playback, preloaded voice buffers, TTS, and compatibility re-exports for the split audio modules.

- BGM 资源配置集中在 `MUSIC_TRACKS`，按 `home`、`battle`、`skill` 三类管理。
- 用户音乐库存与选择使用 `User.ownedMusicIds` 和 `User.musicSelections` 持久化；公开用户信息会自动并入 `defaultUnlocked` 曲目。角色普通技能 BGM 继续保存在 `musicSelections.skill[characterId]`，派生技则按效果类型独立保存在 `musicSelections.derivedSkill[characterId][effectType]`。解析器按 `characterId + musicEffectType` 精确匹配槽位，不能直接拿玩法 `effectType` 当音乐槽位：普通技能预览写入 `musicEffectType: ""`，派生技能预览和最新技能历史写入对应派生效果类型。选中曲目必须属于该角色、该音乐效果类型且已拥有；无有效选择时依次回退技能定义的 `musicTrackId` 和该槽第一首可用曲目。旧用户只有 `skill` 字段时无需迁移；旧派生技能历史缺少 `musicEffectType` 时可由 `musicTrackId` 对应音轨的 `effectType` 兼容推断。
- 角色详情弹窗右上角包含独立的角色 BGM 预览播放器。普通技与每个配置了曲目的派生技显示为独立技能页签，当前技能槽位使用自己的曲目列表和选择；切换技能页签或曲目时，如果预览正在播放，新曲会自动接续播放，未播放时不会自动起播。选择通过 `/api/me/music-selection` 持久化，派生技请求附带 `effectType`，保存失败时播放器内联提示并回滚到上一次有效曲目，且可就地重试。
- Character detail BGM preview enters an immediate `loading` state before first-use WebAudio fetch/decode, keeps `aria-busy` during startup, releases the global background-music pause request on failure/track change, and switches its live play/loading/pause glyph with the accessible label, state class, and `aria-pressed`. Bright School replaces the retired Rough.js decoration with the transparent `920x200` hand-painted radio PNG, rendered with `contain`; no flat background may show through its alpha padding, and the native 44px button is aligned to the painted left circle. Its play glyph uses bright theme blue and its pause glyph uses bright theme pink, with glyph-container plus pause/loading child-span inheritance winners that prevent the later generic span-text rule from repainting any CSS shape dark ink. Hover, focus, and active keep both the real button and its inset pseudo-element background, border, and shadow transparent so no extra circular plate obscures the hand-painted key, while focus retains a visible outline and active retains only the brief depth displacement. Overflowing titles use measured one-way marquee motion with pauses; a recomputed distance at or below 1px emits no animation, preventing long-to-short title changes from leaving a `translateX(-0px)` animation. Reduced-motion users receive a non-animated horizontally scrollable title. The fixed-position sheet keeps theme ancestry, stays open after selection, limits itself to about four rows, and closes on trigger, outside press, or Escape with focus restoration.
- 角色 BGM 预览播放期间会向全局 `BackgroundMusic` 发出暂停请求；背景 BGM 会停止当前 source、保存 offset，并在预览暂停、切歌或关闭详情弹窗释放请求后按 offset 重新调度，从原进度恢复，避免双 BGM 叠播或大厅 BGM 从头重启。
- 主界面 BGM 由 `MUSIC_TRACKS` 中可用的 `home` 曲目池解析；默认池包含单文件循环 `home-default`（`/assets/music/main_bgm.ogg`）和两段式 `home-main-bgm-1`（`/assets/music/main_bgm_1_once.ogg` 播放一次后接 `/assets/music/main_bgm_1_loop.ogg` 循环）。`useBackgroundMusicTrack()` 在每次进入 `view === "home"` 时抽取一次随机值，本次停留在 home 期间保持同一曲目，离开并再次进入 home 时重新抽取；旧的 `musicSelections.home` 字段不参与首页随机解析，避免已有资料把大厅 BGM 固定到单曲。
- 对弈常规 BGM 使用 `shanjifu_intro_once.ogg` + `shanjifu_loop.ogg`。
- 角色技能 BGM 当前配置：达妮娅使用 canonical `denia-skill-default`（`bgm_*`），西格莉卡使用 `sigrika_intro_once.ogg` + `sigrika_loop.ogg`，爱弥斯“小爱出击”使用 `aemeath0_once.ogg` + `aemeath0_loop.ogg`，爱弥斯“远航星”使用 `lhl_intro_once.ogg` + `lhl_loop.ogg`，猪小仙使用 `matoya_*`，娜波摩使用 `busizhe_*`，仇远默认使用 `qiuyuan_intro_once.ogg` + `qiuyuan_loop.ogg`，仇远第二版可购买技能 BGM `qiuyuan-skill-zhouwo` 使用 `/assets/music/qiuyuan_zhouwo_loop.ogg` 单文件循环，商城商品图使用 `/assets/items/qiuyuan-zhouwo.webp`，启动内置商城 seed 会为已存在且 `imageUrl` 为空的该音乐商品回填图片且不覆盖非空后台配置，千咲使用 `chisa_intro_once.ogg` + `chisa_loop.ogg`，长离使用 `changli_intro_once.ogg` + `changli_loop.ogg`，莫宁使用 `mornye_intro_once.ogg` + `mornye_loop.ogg`。旧达妮娅 slug 会在启动清理阶段迁移/删除，运行时 BGM resolver 只按 canonical `denia` 查找达妮娅技能 BGM。
- BGM 优先级为：角色技能 BGM > 对弈常规 BGM > 主界面 BGM。
- 角色释放技能后，后续 BGM 保持为该角色专属 BGM，直到对局结束；后触发的角色技能 BGM 会覆盖先触发的角色技能 BGM。普通技能即使拥有 `flip-stone`、`liberty-purge` 等非空玩法 `effectType`，也必须读取角色普通技能槽位；只有明确带有非空 `musicEffectType` 的派生技能才读取派生槽位。
- 匹配成功倒计时、对局结果弹窗、对局房间的结束阶段会停止 BGM；如果用户从已结束棋谱回放退出到大厅，前端会清空回放房间快照，BGM resolver 以当前 `view = home` 为准恢复主界面 BGM。在线玩家从已结束房间主动退出回大厅时，前端也会把该房间号记为已处理结果，防止房间 5 分钟回收前的残留终局快照继续触发结果弹窗状态并压住大厅 BGM。
- 持久登录自动进入大厅时可能没有用户手势，浏览器会继续挂起 `AudioContext`；`BackgroundMusic` 会在 resume 后检查上下文状态，如果仍为 suspended，就注册 pointerdown / keydown / touchstart 一次性重试，保证首次点击、按键或触摸后恢复大厅 BGM。
- `BackgroundMusic` 使用 Web Audio 播放，预解码 intro/loop buffer，并在同一 `AudioContext` 时间线上调度 intro 到 loop，避免 HTMLAudio 切文件造成明显卡顿。
- 组件级 `BackgroundMusic` 卸载时必须停止 WebAudio source 与 HTMLAudio fallback、清空当前曲目并废弃未完成调度，避免教学对弈、预览层等局部界面退出后残留 BGM。
- 音量变化只调节 gain，不改变播放 identity；因此主音量或 BGM 音量调到 0 后再恢复，不会导致 BGM 从头播放。
- 新用户默认音频设置为 `master: 100`、`bgm: 60`、`sfx: 100`、`voice: 100`，保存于 `localStorage` 的 `sigrika-audio-settings`；已有持久化百分比继续覆盖这些默认值。运行时音量计算会把非法或非有限百分比值回退到默认值，避免损坏的本地设置把 `NaN` 传入 Audio/Web Audio。
- `audioRuntime.js` 通过统一 helper 暴露浏览器 `AudioContext` 构造器；倒计时 beep、门铃音和 TTS 在缺少 `window`、`AudioContext` 或 `speechSynthesis` 时静默 no-op，避免测试、预渲染或非浏览器执行路径因音频副作用抛错。

- 匹配成功后先播放原有 3 秒匹配成功倒计时弹窗，倒计时结束后才进入对局资源预加载页并停止 BGM；该页复用登录加载页样式。普通对局显示本机玩家出战角色跳动立绘、该角色的加载台词，以及 `资源加载中 0/2`、`1/2` 或 `2/2`；黑化决战不显示角色立绘、对应角色台词或同槽位缺图转圈，只保留资源状态、进度条和小橘子。客户端完成本地资源预载后发出 `room:preload-ready`。进入对局页需要同时满足匹配成功倒计时完成和双方都 ready：倒计时先结束时进入并继续停在资源加载页，资源先 ready 时继续显示倒计时并在倒计时结束后直接进入对局页；服务端 90 秒未等齐会中止匹配并由前端 toast `一方加载超时，匹配中止`。
- 对局结果弹窗出现时停止 BGM；胜方播放 `result-victory.mp3`，负方播放 `result-defeat.mp3`，和棋不播放结果音效。房间玩家还会按当前用户本局结果触发出战角色的 `result-victory`、`result-defeat` 或 `result-draw` 系统语音；琳奈的 `lynaeContraryVoice` 生效时，结算使用服务端清除前写入的 `completedItemEffects` 快照将胜利与失败语音互换，界面结果与胜负音效仍保持真实结果，和棋语音不变。无效局不弹结果弹窗，因此不触发结果音效或结果角色语音。棋谱回放模式不会弹结果弹窗，也不会触发结果音效或结果角色语音。
- 普通落子播放 `godown_clear.ogg`。
- 提子动作播放 `go_capture_clear.ogg`。
- 隐藏手暴露播放 `hidden_hand_reveal.ogg`；回放下一步遇到带有隐藏手暴露标记的历史动作时也会播放该音效。
- `boardAudio.js` 根据历史记录中的当前棋盘动作判断落子音或提子音；回放界面点击“下一手”时也会按该步历史播放对应音效，弃手不会重复播放上一手棋盘音效。
- 落子、提子、隐藏手暴露、匹配成功和结果音效均走 `sfx` 音量通道。
- ??????? `ui_confirm.ogg`???????? `ui_close_window.ogg`?????????? `ui_house_open.ogg`?????????? `ui_match_open.ogg`???????????? `ui_detail_open.ogg`???????? `ui_shop_open.ogg`???????? `recruitment-open.ogg`???????? `recruitment-success.ogg` / `recruitment-miss.ogg`????????? `ui_unavailable.ogg` ?? `sfx` ?????`.close-button` / `.inline-close` ? `data-ui-sound="close"` ???????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????????/??????????? `ui-unavailable-shake` ???????????????????????/???????????????? UI ??????????????????????????
- 星炬棋盘技能特效的程序化音效由 `src/room/boardSkillEffectSoundScheduler.js` 按 catalog cue 点调度，并由 `src/audio/skillEffectSounds.js` 负责真正发声；这些音效只读 `audioSettings.sfx`，不参与服务端结算，也不会在 reduced-motion 棋盘演出中播放爆炸/飞入类音效。`src/shared/skillPresentation.js` 会先判断该技能演出和音效层是否启用，关闭特效时不进入 SFX 调度；后台 `skillEffectsEnabled` 关闭后，服务端 pending skill preview 也只等待技能横幅时长，随后直接广播技能结算结果。`src/room/pixiPrewarm.js` 在技能可用棋盘空闲时预热 `pixi.js`，真实演出时复用同一个加载 promise，避免首个技能把模块加载成本集中到动画开始时；关闭特效或无技能棋盘不会为技能演出预热 Pixi。
- `row-slash` 使用 `skillEffectSoundCues` cue 元数据调度程序化技能音效；技能横幅结束后通过 `BoardSkillEffects` 预热并播放不依赖图片资源的 Pixi Graphics 水墨刀光，结算后的持久棋盘刀痕仍走 DOM/CSS overlay，没有独立刀光图片或音频文件依赖。`protocol-takeover` 使用程序化淡紫与冰蓝数据流 Pixi 光束演出，并通过 WebAudio 程序化播放更明显的两段式数据束启动与目标锁定 SFX，结算后的协议禁入标记继续走 DOM/CSS overlay。`double-move` 使用程序化火焰 SFX，并把凤凰与火焰透明 SVG 加入 `RUNTIME_IMAGE_ASSETS.effects` 延迟预加载，技能横幅后的全盘演出通过 Pixi 复用纹理，实际连落点位继续通过 DOM/CSS 显示持续火焰。
- 娜波摩被动灰白化层由 `BoardAmbientEffects` 根据 `game.passives.*.colorIllusion.active` 派生，使用 pointer-transparent 的 CSS 中心向外扩散波纹表现发动过程，同时 `.board-wrap.color-illusion-board-surface::before` 会把棋盘背景从中心扩散切换到 `/assets/boards/nabomo-color-illusion-board.webp`；该专用 WebP 由 `C:/codex/image/go-board-background-reference-color-vertical-2048.jpg` 转换而来，并作为效果资源延迟预加载。该被动不再使用持续 Pixi 烟雾或每帧 ticker 绘制，也不允许回退为整块矩形半透明贴层，因此不会影响落子、触控确认、数子标记或观战/回放棋盘读取。
- 读秒 10 到 1 秒倒计时通过 `playSystemVoice(countdown-N)` 播放角色语音或 TTS；`useRoomAudioEffects` 不再为最后 10 秒申请 BGM duck，单个片段也不会改变 BGM 音量。解码倒计时语音继续使用无混响的 `countdown` playback profile，并直接消费离线校准后的源资产，不做运行时逐文件 RMS 修正；超时只作为结算状态，不播放系统音频、角色语音或“还剩0次读秒”。
- `useRoomCountdownClock` 只为实况最后 10 秒生成展示层时钟，`useRoomBoardView` 将同一个 `displayRoom` 同时交给棋钟组件和语音 hook；服务端到包前后 300ms 的抖动不会重置本地一秒相位。最多预测到最近确认值减一，最低为 1；断包不会自行继续整个倒计时或结算。房间、换手、历史长度、读秒次数、计时阶段改变时重建相位，回放、历史观战、双方断线、非行棋阶段不推进；偏差超过 300ms 或时间回升时重新跟随权威快照，不修改服务器棋钟或判负规则。
- 读秒 profile 为 `{ reverb: false, trimLeadingSilence: true, maxStartDelayMs: 200 }`。解码缓存内按所有声道最早绝对幅值达到 `0.01`（-40dBFS）的位置减去 10ms 作为播放 offset，以 WeakMap 缓存，不裁原文件、不变速、不以元音峰值裁掉轻声辅音。房间开始时预热双方 10 到 1 的语音；播放与预加载共用下载/解码 promise，拒绝非成功 HTTP 响应。语音请求采用后发优先，异步解码和 context 恢复后重新检查请求有效性；读秒超过 200ms 不补播，换手/重置/暂停/卸载清理只取消自身仍有效的请求。浏览器 TTS 与无 WebAudio 的媒体 fallback 仍受平台自身起音延迟影响，不能承诺与解码缓冲相同的精度。

- 角色技能语音配置集中在 `CHARACTER_SKILL_VOICES`，派生技能等效果特定语音通过 `SYSTEM_VOICE_SKILL_EVENTS` 的模式化事件覆盖普通 `skill-cast`。
- `CHARACTER_SKILL_VOICES` 支持单个路径或候选路径数组；`resolveSystemVoice` 每次解析数组候选时随机选择一个，`loginPreloadAssets()` 与 `battlePreloadAssets()` 会展开候选数组，保证所有可能播放的语音都纳入预加载。
- 当前已配置：西格莉卡技能发动使用 `sigrika_skill_cast.ogg`，爱弥斯普通技能使用 `aemeath_skill_cast.ogg`，爱弥斯“远航星”通过 `skill-cast:voyage-star` 使用 `aemeath_skill_cast_voyage.ogg`，娜波摩使用 `nabomo_skill_cast.ogg`，猪小仙使用 `baconbits_skill_cast.ogg`，琳奈使用 `lynae_skill_cast.ogg`，长离使用 `changli_skill_cast.ogg`，千咲使用 `chisa_skill_cast.ogg`，莫宁使用 `mornye_skill_cast.ogg`。
- 仇远技能发动语音使用候选 `qiuyuan_skill_cast.ogg` 与 `qiuyuan_skill_cast_1.ogg`，每次释放技能随机播放其中一个。
- `characterVoiceMapForSkill` 可将现有技能语音桥接为角色 `systemVoices` 的 `skill-cast` 事件映射。
- 角色出战按钮会触发 `sortie` 角色语音事件；当前达妮娅、西格莉卡、猪小仙、琳奈、长离、千咲、莫宁和仇远已分别接入 `denia_sortie.ogg`、`sigrika_sortie.ogg`、`baconbits_sortie.ogg`、`lynae_sortie.ogg`、`changli_sortie.ogg`、`chisa_sortie.ogg`、`mornye_sortie.ogg` 与 `qiuyuan_sortie.ogg`。
- `SkillBanner` 出现时同步触发技能语音，同一个 banner id 只播放一次。
- 技能语音走 `voice` 音量通道。
- `playVoiceSound` 使用 Web Audio 播放链：离线响度校准 source -> dry/wet reverb mix -> voice gain -> destination；逐文件响度由源资产契约负责，运行时只应用用户 voice 音量与现有 profile。
- 同一客户端只允许一个角色语音处于活动播放状态；新的角色语音或 TTS 开始前会立即停止上一段角色语音，避免同一用户连续触发角色语音时叠音。
- 莫宁（`mornye`）已接入 `game-start`、`sortie`、`skill-cast`、`byo-yomi-start`、`byo-yomi-period-2`、`byo-yomi-period-1`、`countdown-10` 到 `countdown-1`、`result-victory`、`result-defeat`、`result-draw` 与 `house-detail` 语音；`house-detail` 使用 `/assets/voice/mornye_detail.ogg`，打开角色详情或点击详情内“描述”文本区域都会播放该语音，关闭角色详情弹窗或关闭整个部员手册会调用 `stopVoicePlayback()` 停止当前角色语音。
- 角色音频和 TTS 播放不再通知 BGM ducking 状态，也不改变背景音乐 gain；BGM 保持用户设置音量。`backgroundDucking.js` 只处理招募演出等非语音场景显式申请的 scoped request。
- `voiceEffects.js` 当前只保留轻量空灵混响与通道增益预设：`boost: 1.35`、`wet: 0.28`、`dry: 0.9`、`reverbSeconds: 1.6`、`reverbDecay: 2.2`、`preDelaySeconds: 0.035`。普通语音使用完整 dry/wet 链，连续倒计时解码语音只走干声支路，避免一秒一段的混响尾音堆叠。
- 如果 Web Audio 或资源加载失败，技能语音会回退到普通 `Audio` 播放，仍应用 1.35 倍 voice 增益上限；两条路径消费同一批已离线校准的 Ogg，因此 fallback 不再缺失独立的逐文件响度修正。
- `scripts/normalize-character-voices.mjs` 与 `scripts/voiceLoudnessNormalization.mjs` 提供可复用的 `npm run voices:normalize` 写入流程和 `npm run check:voices` 只读漂移检查。全部静态角色语音在统一基线上应用 `1.15` 线性增益（约 `+1.214 dB`）：普通片段按 BS.1770/EBU R128 integrated loudness 校准到约 `-16.8 LUFS`，最终 Ogg True Peak 必须不超过约 `-0.8 dBTP`；短于 500ms 或 integrated loudness 不可测的片段改用约 `-17.8 dBFS RMS`。处理链保留声道数和采样率，使用高质量 Vorbis 编码，在系统临时目录完成全部输出、最终 Ogg 复测和路径验证，只有整批无失败时才覆盖仓库资产。普通写入会跳过已处于容差内的文件；全局基线变化时必须运行 `npm run voices:normalize -- --force`，避免新旧容差区重叠导致少数旧文件未重写。

- 系统语音事件集中在 `src/shared/systemVoices.js`，通过 `resolveSystemVoice` 解析。
- 琳奈的 `lynaeContraryVoice` 由 resolver 在选取音频前做固定事件重映射：`countdown-N` 映射为 `countdown-(11-N)`，`byo-yomi-period-2` ↔ `byo-yomi-period-1`、`game-start` ↔ `byo-yomi-start`、`sortie` ↔ `skill-cast`、`result-victory` ↔ `result-defeat`；`result-draw`、`house-detail` 与超时静音保持原样。房间语音角色、技能横幅、棋舍出战和结果弹窗都必须显式携带对应的 `itemEffects`，不能修改静态角色目录或真实事件数据。
- 当前未映射角色事件默认走 TTS 文本；如果角色配置了 `systemVoices[event]`，则优先播放对应音频。仇远的 `game-start` 与 `sortie` 已分别替换为重新录制并统一响度校准的 `qiuyuan_match_start.ogg` 和 `qiuyuan_sortie.ogg`；读秒开始、剩余两次、剩余一次、`countdown-10` 到 `countdown-1`、`result-victory`、`result-defeat` 与 `result-draw` 使用以用户提供的仇远原声为参考、本地生成并统一校准的男声 Ogg。数字倒计时保持中性；已确认的角色化台词为“听风，入局。”、“还剩两次读秒”、“只剩一次读秒，抓紧脚步”、“落子声尽，胜负自明”、“一剑迟疑，满盘皆输……”与“下次再决胜负吧。”。剩余一次采用克制提醒的 D 版，整体放慢约 6%，两句间保留 600 ms 停顿。旧 `Microsoft Kangkang` 生成脚本已经移除，全部事件仍通过显式映射优先于浏览器 TTS 播放。
- 已预留事件：`game-start`、`game-start:gomoku`、`skill-cast`、`sortie`、`byo-yomi-start`、`byo-yomi-periods`、`byo-yomi-period-2`、`byo-yomi-period-1`、`byo-yomi-countdown`、`countdown-N`、`timeout`、`result-victory`、`result-defeat`、`result-draw`、`house-detail`。
- 对局正式开始时，服务端写入 kind 为 `game-start` 的系统消息，前端据此播放“对局开始”语音。
- `game-start:gomoku` 是 `game-start` 的模式化角色语音变体；当前长离在五子棋模式开局时优先播放 `changli_wuzi_match_start.ogg`，其它模式继续播放 `changli_match_start.ogg`；长离倒计时已覆盖 10 到 1 秒。
- 进入读秒和剩余读秒次数已经接入事件化 resolver；剩余 2/1 次读秒使用显式 `byo-yomi-period-2`、`byo-yomi-period-1` 事件，10 到 1 秒倒计时使用 `countdown-10` 到 `countdown-1` 角色语音事件。`timeout` 不再解析为角色音频或 TTS 文本。

- BGM 和效果音位于 `public/assets/music/`。
- 技能语音位于 `public/assets/voice/`。
- 转码用临时工具目录 `.tmp/` 已加入 `.gitignore`，不应提交。

- Runtime audio playback now lives across focused `src/audio/` modules: audio settings, BGM ducking, browser audio runtime detection, procedural generated sounds, background music scheduling, and effect playback are split out, while `playback.jsx` keeps voice/TTS helpers plus compatibility re-exports. Future cleanup can continue splitting voice and audio-cache concerns if the feature set grows.
- Voice playback uses a shared module-level Web Audio context when available; regular voice playback, cached playback, and preload decoding all reuse that context. Future tuning may still need more explicit lifecycle cleanup and browser autoplay handling.
- Audio settings, background music ducking, offline voice loudness helpers, and no-browser audio API guards are covered by deterministic helper tests, but not by a browser-level audio graph test.
- Character voice loudness is an authored-asset/build-tool contract rather than a runtime `AudioBuffer` correction. `check:voices` must pass after adding or replacing any `public/assets/voice/*.ogg`; runtime Web Audio and ordinary `Audio` fallback intentionally avoid independent per-file normalization so their relative source loudness cannot diverge.
- Voice reverb now uses a deterministic generated impulse. If authored reverb tails become important, replace it with a static impulse asset.
- BGM assets are committed directly under `public/assets/music/` and increase repository size. If the soundtrack grows, consider Git LFS, an asset CDN, or a manifest-driven asset pipeline.
- Music ownership, purchase availability, and player selection are represented in configuration shape but not yet backed by persisted music inventory/settings UI.
- Character skill voice configuration is static in `CHARACTER_SKILL_VOICES`; future admin-configurable voices would need schema/API/upload support and cache invalidation rules.
- No browser-level automated test currently verifies actual audio playback, Web Audio graph routing, or autoplay fallback behavior; existing coverage focuses on deterministic helper logic.
- Countdown character voice assets are opportunistically preloaded and decoded into a module-level Web Audio buffer cache while the active player is in byo-yomi. Cached playback reuses the same dry/wet reverb voice chain as regular voice playback, and failures fall back to the existing voice path without blocking timers.

## Music Display Name Management

- Current effective preload contract: login preload blocks home entry only on the selected character portrait, home-shell images, interaction audio, and currently reachable home BGM. Other owned character portraits, shop/recruitment/inventory/equipment/decorations, battle/skill tracks, result sounds, and character voices remain in the same manifest but load in the bounded background queue after home entry. Unpurchased music products still do not preload full audio. Battle preload is scoped to the current room and current user's music selections: it loads both player portraits, only the selected battle track, one resolved track per relevant base/derived skill slot, required voice candidates, and mode-specific effect images. No-skill modes such as `standard` and `gomoku` skip skill BGM, skill voices, and skill effect images. A refreshed or reconnected player that resumes a room still in `GAME_PHASES.preloading` must return to the battle preload route and emit `room:preload-ready` after local assets finish, instead of entering active room state from that snapshot; this report is retried until the server acknowledges it.
- Client-side playable-ready prewarm sits before the current effective preload contract. It can warm module/Pixi caches while the user is on the home or match-selection surfaces, but the app still shows `match-preloading` and waits for the same room-scoped battle asset manifest plus server ready acknowledgements before exposing the interactive room.
- Current effective cache contract: production gives hashed Vite build outputs `Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable`; named `/assets/**` runtime resources and the SPA shell use `no-cache`. Nginx and Express fallback templates share these values, so stable-name WebP/SVG/BGM/voice replacements revalidate through ETag/Last-Modified instead of serving a stale visual for another release window. Hash or version filenames remain recommended for truly immutable high-traffic resources.
- Local stability verification uses `LOCAL_PROD_STATIC=1` to exercise this same cache contract against the production build without setting `NODE_ENV=production`. This keeps local socket/auth/test-action behavior usable while still catching stale-cache regressions before a cloud deploy. `npm run verify:stability` now also covers desktop/mobile room recovery, real Pixi `.board-effects-canvas` playback for Sigrika and Aemeath `hidden-hand` / `voyage-star`, finished-room exit plus reload without reopening the result modal, and opponent reconnect without showing the local player a rate-limit toast.
- Startup preload may show the generic login `preloading` route only while no room or pending match has already been restored by Socket.IO. If `roomRef.current` or `matchSuccessRef.current` exists after `/api/me`, startup asset loading continues without hiding the recovered battle route.

- `MUSIC_TRACKS` remains the authority for playable BGM ids, types, character bindings, default unlock flags, purchasability, playback modes, and audio source paths. Home BGM tracks share the same static catalog: adding a new default-unlocked or owned home track automatically makes it eligible for home-entry randomization and login preload.
- `MusicTrackSetting` stores only per-track `displayName` overrides keyed by static track id. Blank or missing settings fall back to the static `track.name`.
- Server startup runs `ensureMusicTrackSettingsSchema()` so older SQLite development databases can read and write music display-name settings without a manual migration.
- Authenticated players read the merged catalog from `GET /api/music-tracks`; admin users read and update the same catalog through `GET /api/admin/music-tracks` and `PATCH /api/admin/music-tracks/:id`.
- The admin music tab edits display names only. It cannot create tracks, upload or replace audio files, disable tracks, or change track type/character binding.
- Frontend music consumers should pass the merged track map into existing music-library resolver functions instead of duplicating selection logic. Character BGM preview/select controls, background music resolution, shop music card names, and gacha music prize options/display names should all use the merged catalog.

## Login Asset Preloading

- Frontend deployment helpers live in `src/shared/preloadAssets.js`.
- Socket.IO now connects to `window.location.origin`, so the deployed site can run behind `https://sigrika.fun` without a hard-coded localhost socket endpoint.
- Vite development proxy forwards `/socket.io` websocket traffic to the local backend, keeping the same-origin socket path usable in development and production.
- After a valid token is confirmed, the app enters a `preloading` view before the home screen.
- Fresh login enters `preloading` before the home screen, preventing the home screen from flashing before assets begin loading. Stored-token startup is intentionally disabled so refresh and browser restart return to the login screen.
- While `AuthScreen` is visible, `src/app/authPortraitPrewarm.js` starts a non-blocking two-worker warmup of the base portraits eligible for random loading-screen presentation. Sigrika and Denia are first in the ordered batch; remaining eligible built-in portraits follow at the same bounded concurrency. Repeated login mounts share one active batch, individual failures stay out of the ready set and may retry on a later mount, and neither the submit control, auth request, nor `onAuth` callback awaits the warmup. Costume portraits, candy-effect portraits, audio, shop media, and battle effects remain outside this pre-authentication batch.
- `preloadImageAssets()` reports a source through `onLoaded` only after its image load and browser decode complete. `AssetPreloadScreen` subscribes to the resulting decoded-source ready store: whenever at least one matching source exists, no-fixed-character startup/route loaders choose their first character and 10-second rotations only from that ready subset; when the subset is empty they retain the existing safe candidate fallback. Random loaders deliberately render the warmed base portrait rather than substituting an equipped costume or item-effect portrait that was not part of the pre-authentication batch. Fixed-character battle and tutorial loaders keep their existing user/costume snapshot resolution.
- The preload step fetches non-replay runtime assets after login and blocks home entry on the current user's accessible asset manifest. That manifest includes current-user-visible character portraits, home entry/background imagery, shop product display images, owned inventory/recruitment item imagery, equipped achievement asset images, owned/default-unlocked BGM sources, relevant owned/default character skill BGM, character skill voices, system voices, and common board/UI effect sounds. Unpurchased music products only contribute shop display imagery, not full BGM audio, because the player cannot preview unowned music. Replay lists and replay details remain lazy data requests so opening the app does not prefetch historical game records.

## Aemeath Voyage Star Resources

- `voyage-star` is a full-board Pixi skill effect backed by `/assets/effects/voyage-star-crater.webp`, a transparent WebP converted from the supplied heart/star artwork. `src/room/boardSkillEffectRegistry.js` draws the white/ice-blue knight greatsword, target omen, impact glow, visual-only quake layer, yellow-white soft-edged explosion cover, and removal particles from pending preview geometry while leaving the crater artwork to the resolved board overlay. The greatsword drops from above with a short trail and stays visible through impact; the impact window adds concentric earthquake ellipses, 2-3 decaying shake pulses on the Pixi layer only, and deterministic dust sparks without moving the DOM board or hit areas. The explosion cover layer is added after the other Pixi layers so it can fully mask the impact frame; it expands from the skill center with a solid warm-white core whose radius is at least 2.2 board cells, covering the roughly four-cell crater footprint, then blends outward through low-alpha white and warm-yellow discs whose feathered outer edge fades to zero alpha. The separate impact glow fades before full-cover, then the renderer adds a fully opaque white full-board cover with no circular rim, holds that cover for resolution, and dissolves it with eased opacity plus outward yellow-white rings, radial streaks, and spark fragments instead of snapping directly back to the board; the sword alpha is forced to zero once that cover reaches every board corner. The Pixi cast starts from procedural Graphics and never draws a temporary crater sprite or fallback shape; the crater WebP is loaded through the renderer asset list only as monitored prewarm for the resolved DOM crater, so slow image decode cannot delay the real Pixi canvas or the full-board cast startup. `skillPreviewResolutionDelay({ effectType: "voyage-star" })` resolves at `2936ms`, which is the 2s banner plus 52% of the 1800ms board-effect window, placing the authoritative crater and stone removals under the full-cover explosion light. `BoardSkillEffects` keeps an already-started Pixi cast alive after that early resolved snapshot clears `pendingSkill`, so the opaque cover hides the resolved board until its dissolve phase instead of letting the crater show through the whiteout. The persistent DOM crater is a board-level `.voyage-star-crater-mark` overlay positioned by board-coordinate CSS variables from the source id, sized to 3.5 board cells, and animated with tight pink-purple/cyan drop-shadow glow that follows the WebP alpha shape instead of a round haze; the erased points remain stored on the points as `voyage-star-crater-point` or `voyage-star-erased-point`. This one-owner split keeps the crater from appearing inside the cover, avoids a sudden post-effect crater pop, and avoids a second flash when the resolved room state arrives.
- `aemeath-skill-default` is the selectable base Aemeath “小爱出击” BGM and uses `/assets/music/aemeath0_once.ogg` followed by `/assets/music/aemeath0_loop.ogg`. `aemeath-voyage-star-default` belongs to the independent `voyage-star` derived slot and uses `/assets/music/lhl_intro_once.ogg` followed by `/assets/music/lhl_loop.ogg`; Voyage Star pending previews and resolved skill history carry `musicEffectType: "voyage-star"`, so `findSkillTrack()` reads `musicSelections.derivedSkill.aemeath["voyage-star"]` before falling back to that configured track. Ordinary Aemeath previews carry an empty music effect type and continue to read `musicSelections.skill.aemeath`. `battlePreloadAssets()` preloads derived skill tracks by `effectType`, and old Voyage Star history without `musicEffectType` remains compatible because its `musicTrackId` resolves to track metadata with `effectType: "voyage-star"`. Future purchasable Aemeath tracks use the character-level slot only when they replace “小爱出击”; tracks replacing Voyage Star or another derived skill use the existing effect-scoped nested selection.
- The Voyage Star track intentionally keeps Aemeath's previous `lhl_*` intro/loop files, while the base Aemeath track now uses the supplied `aemeath0_*` assets.
- Preload failures are non-blocking per resource: failed or hanging asset loaders are skipped after a bounded per-task timeout so users are not trapped on the loading screen if a single asset stalls during reconnect, server restart, or cache recovery. Skipped login and battle assets are retried in the background after the target screen is entered with a lower concurrency cap.
- The preload screen uses a character portrait hop plus progress bar, with a short minimum display duration to avoid a visual flash on cached loads. `AssetPreloadScreen` reads `siteSettings.characterLoadingLines` as `characterId=line` rows for the main loading line, uses a decoded-ready random base-catalog character for post-login preload when available, and uses the current player character for battle preload; the spinner remains only as an image-missing fallback. It also reads `siteSettings.preloadTips`, keeps each parsed non-empty line unchanged in configuration/runtime data, and prefixes the selected rendered line with exactly one `Tip：`; on the post-login preload view it rotates the random character portrait, character loading line, and tip together every 10 seconds while the view remains open, while fixed-character battle preload keeps the player character stable and only rotates tips. `showTips={false}` is reserved for flows that must reuse the same loading template but suppress random hints, such as tutorial-battle entry and exit transitions with fixed copy. The shared square orange progress mascot keeps `width: auto`, fixes its height to three times the track, and clears the mobile-wide image `max-width` cap so reaching the 100% endpoint cannot flatten it. The loading panel itself stays borderless and transparent in both the base layer and the final Bright School safety layer, uses full-viewport centering, and lets the character line plus tip text wrap inside the shared preload width so mobile and desktop keep the same no-frame loading treatment without adding a solid middle panel background.
- Loading-character presentation excludes `baconbits` at the shared `AssetPreloadScreen` boundary. Both random catalog selection and explicit fixed-character inputs fall back to another portrait-bearing character, and configured Baconbits loading copy is ignored; the underlying character portrait remains part of gameplay asset preload when a room actually contains Baconbits.

### Tutorial handoff progress and human portrait

Unassigned human tutorial players reuse `SIGRIKA_CORRUPTED_PLAYER_PORTRAIT_ASSET` as display-only art. Their character remains null, preserving the tutorial's no-skill contract; assigned actors, NPCs and practice bots retain their existing resolution. Tutorial portrait preloading includes this shared asset.

The tutorial route Suspense fallback uses zero progress instead of the completed global startup asset progress. `TutorialBattleLoading` remains keyed by loading ID, and timed progress also records its owning ID so a new segment renders zero before effects reset the timer. This prevents an initial 100% flash in story/battle handoffs while preserving forward progress timing and cleanup.

### Window content loading

Player windows use `WindowLoadingState` for pending content, sharing the empty-state paper drawing and LXGW typography. A gently moving pencil distinguishes loading from empty results; reduced-motion disables it. Compact inline feedback preserves existing content during profile mode changes and replay pagination. Request lifecycles, errors, and full-screen resource progress are unchanged.

---

# UI 主题、移动端与交互体验

竖屏对弈新增棋钟深度后，最终 `mobile-room-shadow-gutters.css` 必须覆盖 `mobile-player-slot` 的旧 `overflow: hidden !important`，普通房间改为 `overflow: visible`；动作区锚定的视口在全部竖屏移动断点统一采用 `0 5px 6px 0` 留白，现有行间距容纳 3px 阴影／位移。验证必须包含真实 `RoomScreen → mobile-room-viewport → mobile-player-slot → PlayerInfo` 容器链、黑白换手与短屏，不能只在宽松容器中单测 PlayerInfo。

`RoomHeader` 的 `.room-code-label` 以当前模式短名替换“房间号”：吃子挑战优先显示“吃子赛”，其他 `matchSource=practice` 显示“人机”，其余读取棋种 `shortTitle`。保留房间代码和特殊剧情 `ERROR`；`.move-count` 只使用当前手数，不再混入吃子赛名称、手数上限和提子数。普通 Bright School 对弈由最终样式 `mobile-adaptive/battle-paper-panels.css` 与 `battle-paper-panels-mobile.css` 拆分身份、棋钟和技能面板。`PlayerInfo` 的 `player-clock-panel` 包含原计时器和统计，其他主题使用 `display: contents` 保留原布局；黑化剧情不启用新外观。棋钟常态使用 `--bright-sheet` 米色，`.active-turn` 使用既有黄色 `#fff0a6`、3px 棕色硬阴影和零位移，其他棋钟下沉 3px 且无阴影。立绘沿用装扮解析与队际阵容，普通单角色清除旧裁切轮廓并越过浅色背板；姓名牌、观战视角和技能浮层交互保持原语义。竖屏信息卡按用户要求恢复为大改前的两行布局（portrait/meta/time 与 portrait/captures/skill）；`battle-paper-panels.css` 仅作用于非 mobile-room-screen，手机的 `player-clock-panel` 使用 display: contents，因此沿用原 timer、统计和技能样式。保留 94px 信息条、66px 出框立绘，独立卡片底板从左侧 48px 开始，约 18px 边缘藏在立绘下；底板使用米色／回合黄色及整卡阴影，文字从立绘列之外开始，不被覆盖。底板伪元素显式覆盖旧主题的 content/display/background 重置，立绘使用更高局部层级。视口仍为四行 max-content、8px 间距，棋盘使用屏宽和 dvh／安全区共同定尺寸，大屏优先一屏，短屏允许滚动。桌面分层布局保持不变。

常规玩家 `ActionBar` 固定呈现弃手、数子、技能、和棋、认输五个按钮。五子棋的弃手／数子、无技能模式的技能、禁和棋模式的和棋保留原生 `disabled` 灰色占位；无技能时仅显示“技能”。观战回放、死子确认和教学专用操作区沿用各自布局。`room-people-table` 的滚动区域内保留上／右／下／左 `2px 6px 6px 2px` 内边距，覆盖成员按钮悬停上移及右下硬阴影，仍保留列表纵向滚动。

## 玩家窗口左侧书签选项卡

`ResumeModal`、`SettingsModal`、`AchievementModal`、`LeaderboardModal`、`WatchModal`、`FriendsModal`和 `AnnouncementModal` 显式声明 `window-bookmark-host`，对应分类/模式控件使用 `WindowBookmarkTabs`；`UserProfileCard` 仅在首页好友入口启用 `titleStickers` 时接入，房间内保持原布局。组件保留原按钮 ID、`aria-controls`、回调、选中状态与加载禁用规则，普通校园主题通过 portal 将书签列放到最近的已启用窗口外层。监听 `.app-shell` 主题类变化，黑化或主题切换时恢复原布局；不得穿过未启用的嵌套对话框挂载到祖先窗口。当前商城为双店铺切换，旧 `ShopTabs` 和 `GachaModal` 没有现行入口，不为样式推广重新启用。

书签以简短不规则 SVG 纸边与淡色底呈现，外轮廓使用 2.5px 描边和 `vector-effect: non-scaling-stroke`，避免手机窄幅缩放把侧边线压细；内部铅笔线保持纤细。书签内部仅呈现文字，SVG/图片图标与装饰点隐藏；标签及嵌套文字显式使用 400 字重并避免合成加粗。文字引用 `--font-window-title` 对应的 `LXGWMarkerGothic-Regular.ttf`，不将文字烘焙进图片。书签列右沿对齐窗口外边框，尾端不覆盖窗口边线；未选中项尾端使用窄幅渐变暗部表现插入纸页的层次。选中项向外伸出，桌面悬停小幅平移，减少动态效果偏好关闭过渡。书签保留 tab 语义，纵列使用上下方向键及 Home/End，加载期间不会触发模式切换；未接入时保留原有横向键盘操作。

`mobile-adaptive/window-bookmarks.css` 汇总外层几何、纸签样式、内容滚动适配和移动端规则，在最终黑化规则前加载。backdrop 使用 `minmax(0, 1fr)` 列并预留书签与阴影空间，避免窗口原有视口宽度撑大隐式 grid 列；书签列独立滚动，正文不会带走书签。宽度不超过 1100px 或高度不超过 700px 时正文整体滚动，窄桌面的最近十盘移到四项统计下方；手机履历使用内容自适应高度，保留原安全视口最大高度并顶部对齐，少量记录不再留下整屏空纸；58px 宽书签列使用 72px 最小标签高度和 8px 间隔，竖排文字居中。手机正文可纵向滚动到最后一条角色战绩，320–360px 竖屏采用图标式成就/个性化入口并缩小身份头像，回放按钮换行保留 44px 点击区域。桌面、800×600 窄桌面与 320×568、390×844 竖屏均验证边界、模式切换和内容可达性。

`window-bookmark-content.css` 移除设置/成就/公告中原选项卡占用的网格行，好友工具栏保留搜索行，招募道具选择保留在窗口内操作栏，不接入书签；图标使用书签内专门尺寸，观战书签移除房间数，仅保留模式名称，书签内部隐藏图标及未读装饰点，仅保留文字；公告列表的未读状态继续显示。560px 以下的排行榜把积分与战绩移到姓名下方，避免书签预留空间挤没姓名列；360px 以下的详细资料社交操作分成两行，保持 44px 点击区。空窗口留出标签所需最低高度（340px，上限受安全视口约束）；书签文字禁止换行：桌面单行横排、手机单列竖排；标签列独立滚动。好友与黑名单列表采用 `align-content: start`，单行不会随容器剩余空间拉高。贴纸标题头统一保留 52px 最小高度、12px 底部内边距和 10px 下外边距，给虚线两侧的控件阴影与正文留出间隔；履历沿用专属操作行。`WindowBookmarkWindows.dom.test.jsx` 对接入窗口验证挂载、选择回调，以及房间资料与招募道具选择的 opt-out；独立懒加载边界继续由 `AppOverlays.dom.test.jsx` 回归保护。

## 首页挂扣学生证

首页身份入口使用 `student-id-hanging.png/webp` 透明竖版卡面，保留金属挂钩、卡套和几何学院风。`PlayerPlaque` 通过共享 `characterPortraitImageProps` 解析当前出战角色、服装和特殊状态立绘，在右侧窄照片框叠加，用户名仅位于照片下方右侧留白；首页不再渲染模式段位、积分、共鸣链或成就铭牌。其他资料页的成就装备展示不变。点击、键盘操作、专属履历音效与剧情禁用行为沿用现有入口。

最终样式由 `mobile-adaptive/home-student-id.css` 管理。卡面、挂钩、铆钉和边缘统一为铅笔描线与水粉手绘质感，保留几何学院图案。`PlayerPlaque` 是木板 `.home-main-panel` 的直接子元素，挂点位于原左侧位置上方的木板上沿；绝对定位必须设置 `grid-area: auto`，防止继承旧 `player` 命名区域后落入父网格右侧隐式区域而缩小。宽度由木板上的 `--home-hanging-id-width` 按桌面/手机视口自适应控制。手册在右侧稍低位置，内容网格预留证件垂挂高度，下方入口不被覆盖。背景与文字按同一证件比例定位；长用户名可换行，完整值保存在 title，照片不横向铺满卡面。图片透明区不增加实色外框；减少动态效果时停用摆动。


移动端对弈的技能/数值点击说明挂载到 `.app-shell` 浮层，避开玩家卡片与棋盘容器裁切，并保留主题和浮层顺序；竖屏对弈视口与操作滚动区预留右下阴影空间。引导误点提示统一为“请在黄圈位置落子”。房间页不显示关闭倒计时，也不再运行显示用秒级计时器；服务端 5 分钟关闭逻辑保持不变。

普通校园主题对弈模式卡片只显示单行模式标题与匹配人数，背景英文完整显示，背景模式图标隐藏。规则不在按钮内展示：仅在宽度超过 768px 且具备精细指针悬停能力时，于鼠标旁显示规则浮窗，跟随移动并避让视口边缘；移出、点击、滚动、改变窗口大小或 Escape 关闭，移动端在卡片右上角提供独立的 22px 小 ⓘ（图标 16px），绝对定位、不改变卡片布局；点击切换规则浮窗，点击空白处关闭，不触发匹配。`mobile-adaptive/match-mode-title-layout.css` 负责最终布局，模式选择与陪练入口行为保持原样。
本文记录 Bright School 主题、CSS 分层、移动端适配、弹窗布局、反馈消息和 UI 交互体验。新增主题、移动布局、弹窗视觉合同或用户反馈界面时优先更新本分篇。

## 当前结论

- 普通校园主题首页及固定功能子窗口通过 `WindowTitleSticker` 使用素净纸签标题；透明图片中的文字由项目霞鹭字体直接排版。纸签左侧越界 20px（手机 18px）、上方露出 52% 并固定倾斜 -2°，逻辑尺寸为 168×76、220×80、272×84，手机缩至 80%；浅色横线纸面、白色裁切边、小折角和投影提供贴纸层次。各窗口共用扣除 76px 与安全区的高度预算，为越界图片与阴影保留空间。普通标题栏采用 8px 顶部内边距、44px 最小行高和 4px 底部内边距，关闭、刷新、回退等标题栏图标按钮统一为 36px 方形，同排文字按钮统一为 36px 高，手机信息中心操作列同步为 36px；绝对定位关闭按钮距顶部 6px、右侧 16px；部员手册虚线与正文整体上提 20px，履历标题贴纸脱离文档流，定位容器保持窗口全宽，避免图片的百分比最大宽度基于缩窄标题容器再次压缩贴纸，操作区垂直居中，成就、个性化、金币和关闭依次排列并统一 44px 高，页头最小高度 56px，关闭按钮保持 44px 方形；自己的履历竖屏角色表采用 44%/12%/8%/8%/8%/20% 六列比例和 8px 卡片横向内边距，表头与数据列对齐；回放改为总对局卡片右侧图标按钮，最近十盘卡片去掉小标题，桌面端撑满所在行并与左侧统计卡片组等高，竖屏保持紧凑单行高度；胜负标记整体水平居中，不足十盘和暂无状态同样居中，角色战绩去掉标题并使用 20px 高的对齐表头，他人资料保持原布局；外壳顶部保留 8px 安全留白，移除原手机端 28px 标题占位。`window-sticker-resume-header.css` 负责该局部布局。`window-title-stickers.css` 负责非滚动外壳定位、安全区与标题回退，`window-title-sticker-content.css` 保留内部滚动、按钮阴影内边距与嵌套遮罩定位。标题不增加副标题或说明；商店及子窗口、IRIS、动态名称、匹配状态与剧情不参与。共用资料卡和回放通过 `titleStickers` 显式启用，对局入口默认关闭；黑化主题保持原字体标题。主页和社交挑战的“选择对弈模式”使用同一资源。

- 玩家侧目前只有 Bright School 一个默认主题，但主题注册、作用域和 CSS 入口仍保留扩展口。
- `src/styles/base.css`、`room.css`、`modals.css`、`commerce-settings.css`、`responsive.css`、`mobile-room.css`、`hud-components.css`、`mobile-adaptive.css` 和后台/剧情教学路由 CSS 都保持 import-only 入口加领域子目录的结构；低频路由 CSS 由 lazy owner 组件导入，最终 `mobile-adaptive.css` 仍在主题之后作为手机、窄桌面和后台全屏安全层。
- Bright School 根入口当前顺序是 `base.css`、`gallery-polish.css`、`surface-contracts.css`、`component-repairs.css`、`qa-guard.css`。`surface-contracts.css` 是显式表面合同层，委托到 `surface-contracts/` 下的 root/surface/controls/forms/badges、known HUD reset、home/profile/commerce/room cleanup、final explicit surfaces、announcement controls 和 typography 分篇；它替代旧广域兜底清理结构，不再允许 broad substring selector、空兼容文件或主题级 `*` 阴影清理。
- Bright School 的高频视觉工作仍进入 owning domain：大厅和主面板在 `home/`，商业在 `commerce/`，弹窗/设置/结果/房间浮层在 `modals/`，技能/棋盘动效在 `effects/`，对局可读性在 `room/`，移动端在 `mobile/`，意图明确的纸张/手账细节恢复在 `component-repairs/`，质量和移动兜底由 `qa-guard.css` 后置导入。
- Bright School 的 `.app-shell.player-theme-enabled.theme-bright-school .modal-backdrop` 由 `surface-contracts/root-shell.css` 显式覆盖为暗色静态遮罩，并同时声明 WebKit 前缀和标准 `backdrop-filter: none !important`；标准声明保持在前缀之后，避免生产 Lightning CSS 压缩只保留前缀而让 Chrome 回退到基础层 `blur(9px)`。`npm run check:built-css` 在 Vite build 后直接检查 `dist` 中的最终压缩 CSS，且已进入 `npm run check` 和正式更新脚本。
- 低频路由 CSS 不再要求进入全局根样式：`src/styles/admin.css` 是后台管理 domain entry，但由 lazy `src/admin/AdminConsole.jsx` 导入；`src/styles/room/tutorial-battle-screen.css` 是剧情教学对弈专属 entry，由 lazy `src/tutorial/TutorialBattleScreen.jsx` 导入，包含桌面和移动教学战斗空角色位、目标/行动、独立 `target-ring.css` 目标光圈和 loading motion 规则。`src/styles/mobile-adaptive/admin-fullscreen.css` 仍保留在全局最终安全层中，因为它需要在主题和 HUD 之后清理 `.admin-screen` 的全屏背景继承。
- 后台管理是浅色生产工具界面，即使复用 `.primary-action`、`.secondary-action`、`.danger-action`、`.close-button`、`.modal-backdrop`、`.confirm-modal`、普通 `input`/`textarea`/`select` 或带 `setting` 片段的容器，也必须由 `src/styles/admin/polish.css` 与公告管理样式在 `.admin-screen` 内显式复位 `clip-path`、暗色/霓虹背景、绿色终端边线、text-shadow/filter 和终端 pseudo-element；该合同同时覆盖桌面端和移动端，避免 Startorch/HUD 的黑底、绿纹、斜角控件和黑色输入框污染后台。
- 移动端布局由共享移动层、Bright School 移动层和最终 `mobile-adaptive.css` 安全层共同约束。设置弹窗在最终手机层使用标题、tab、内部面板三行网格，外壳不滚动，当前 `settings-panel` 自己滚动并 containment；关闭按钮、首页图标按钮和手机菜单按钮保持显式 accessible label 与 44px 触控目标。
- Bright School 竖屏共享弹窗以遮罩的 padding box 作为唯一尺寸边界。`themes/bright-school/mobile/modal-shell/shell-surfaces.css` 将普通与嵌套遮罩统一为 `inset: 0`、`width/height: auto`，左上保留各 `10px` 基础间距，右侧保留 `12px`、底部保留 `14px`，四边再叠加对应 `safe-area-inset-*`；子窗口使用内容区 `width/max-width: 100%`，最大高度按上、下共 `24px` 及纵向 safe area 扣减。`mobile-adaptive/modal-shadow-gutters.css` 位于商店、邮箱、服装详情等特性终局样式之后，重复这一最终边界合同，禁止后置 `100vw` 计算重新吞掉商店 `9px × 11px` 硬阴影槽。弹窗自身仍保持原有 `overflow` owner，列表/设置面板等内部滚动区继续独立滚动；本合同不处理 IRIS 首页入口位置。
- 滚动条按界面所有者隔离：`hud-hardening/tokens-shell-scrollbars.css` 的青色终端滚动条只匹配非 Bright School 的 `.app-shell.player-theme-enabled` 及其文档根，不允许裸 `*` 规则污染后台或纸张主题；Bright School 的 `component-repairs/foundation-home/root-scrollbars.css` 同时覆盖 `html`、`body` 与主题壳为粉蓝纸张滚动条；lazy 后台入口的 `admin/scrollbars.css` 使用中性灰蓝轨道和滑块。文档根滚动条占宽时，移动弹窗仍依赖实际包含块而非视口单位，因此不得产生横向滚动。
- 首页学生证头像的 DOM 固定为 `.plaque-avatar > .plaque-avatar-mask > img`，连携徽章是 `.plaque-avatar-mask` 的同级元素。外层头像容器允许投影越界，内层继承圆角并负责 `overflow: hidden` 裁图，`drop-shadow` 由内层合成结果生成；移动与窄桌面只调整内层 padding，不能把 `overflow: hidden` 放回外层导致投影或徽章被裁。后台仍由 `.admin-sidebar` 与 `.admin-main` 分别滚动；`.admin-screen` 在右、下各增加 `16px` 专用阴影留白，并从两面板最大高度中扣除该值，使 `--admin-shadow-sm` 的柔光不被根 `overflow: hidden` 截断。
- 视觉合同应靠 CSS contract tests 锁定，避免后续主题覆盖把语义状态、关闭按钮、移动列表等打散。
- 邮箱与公告沿用 `InformationCenterLayout` 的桌面双栏与竖屏列表/正文切换，共享 18px 桌面/16px 移动 header 控件到虚线的净空、14px 桌面栏间距、2px 语义外框、透明正文 article、无硬阴影的列表节奏和中性选中框；公告仍保留分类 tabs、置顶/未读标签和未读点，邮箱列表则只显示标题、发件人与紧凑时间，不渲染可见未读点、附件缩略图或状态 chip。邮箱纯文本已读、以及附件已读且不可再领取的行使用 muted ink 与较低字重，所有未读或仍待领取的行保持正常纸面，只用 `--theme-accent` 的纤细暖粉内侧线区分，不再铺浅绿色底。桌面切换邮件时，以标题、正文、附件/操作区的顺序从右侧 44px 滑入，单段 420ms，后两段分别延迟 100ms 与 200ms，使动效明确贯穿到底部按钮；竖屏不复用该动效，reduced-motion 直接静态显示。附件图块与右下领取按钮分离，Bright School 的全局 sticker-button 规则不得重新给附件图块加 3px 边框和硬阴影；右上数量角标用深色边框面和显式浅色文字 winner，不能被后置弹窗文字规则染成黑字。已领取附件图块在图像上叠半透明暗灰遮罩，再在遮罩之上绘制白色勾；Bright School 必须同时锁定标记容器与 Lucide SVG 本身的白色 `color`/`stroke`，数量角标保持最高层。道具详情通过 portal 挂到 `.app-shell`，位于邮箱遮罩之上，桌面限制为 620px 双栏，竖屏为单栏且按真实视口/遮罩内容区居中；内容只保留道具类别、图片、名称和说明，不再显示持有量、邮件数量或领取状态，内部继续使用 44px 关闭目标、焦点陷阱、Escape 单层关闭和焦点恢复。金币图块不可交互，道具图块才打开详情。
- 衣柜服装详情保留共享 `.shop-detail-art` 图像框，但由 `.character-costume-detail-art` 在基础层和 Bright School 最终主题层共同清除立绘的 `drop-shadow`；该覆盖不作用于商店商品详情立绘，也不移除图像框或弹窗阴影。
- Bright School 下扎希拉与娜波摩的台词气泡使用两张 ImageGen 完整透明 WebP 框图：扎希拉是淡莓白手切纸片并从右下指向立绘，娜波摩是暖金票券纸片并从左下指向立绘。纸纹、手绘轮廓、硬影和尾巴全部位于图片画布内部，CSS 清除旧边框、阴影、圆角与三角伪元素，只以 `100% 100%` 背景承载框图并把实时 DOM 台词放在安全内边距中，因此多层商店 `overflow: hidden` 不会单独裁掉尾巴或阴影。竖屏最终层只调整框图宽度、位置、字号与内边距，两套气泡的文字左右安全区统一为 `22px`，保持文本、切换木牌和看板娘互不遮挡；其中残星会气泡重复 Bright School 完整主题作用域，以右侧 `54%` 的竖屏布局胜过桌面的 `80%` 重要规则，避免遮住娜波摩面部。
- 商店双页的切换路牌在桌面和竖屏都使用镜像底角锚点：扎希拉页位于左下，残星会页位于右下；桌面统一使用 `14px` 边距，最终移动层统一使用 `8px` 边距，并显式清除顶部与相反方向的 inset。
- 商店页头余额与履历金币共用 `.shop-wallet` 的黄金币渐变、深色描边、硬阴影和 `CircleDollarSign` 图标；商店 owner 只压缩竖屏尺寸，不再声明会覆盖共享渐变的纯色背景。
- IRIS Database 是首页自有的视口固定入口而不是舞台网格项。`src/home/IrisDatabase.jsx` 持有本地打开状态并复用共享 `ModalDialog`；入口和弹窗完整使用数据流原型 v2 的深蓝/绿色、切角、断线节点、点阵数据字与扫描语法，入口与窗口的立绘槽都不渲染 `<img>` 或请求候选人物资源。`src/styles/base/foundation.css` 将内置 `public/assets/fonts/DottedSongtiCircleRegular.otf` 注册为 IRIS 专用 `Dotted Songti Circle` 字体：入口整体只用该字体；窗口使用 WuWa 拉丁/数字字形在前、`Dotted Songti Circle` 字体在后的组合栈，因此英文和数字走 WuWa，中文与符号走`Dotted Songti Circle` 字体；友情链接索引列表在自身 owner 中覆盖为全 `Dotted Songti Circle` 字体，Bright School 的最终字体胜出规则独立放在 `themes/bright-school/modals/iris-database-typography.css`。桌面窗口采用左侧空立绘数据扇区/右侧编号链接记录，竖屏移动端采用上方立绘扇区/下方记录列表；不再由 Bright School 把右栏覆盖成米白纸面，也不使用圆角外壳、圆角资料行或围棋棋子列表。问候语绝对定位在立绘扇区顶部 8%（竖屏 13%），立绘扫描框从 20%（竖屏 34%）开始，二者测量不重叠；通讯条始终完整显示 `irisGreeting` 台词池中本次打开时随机选中的一条，不再自动收起、响应点击或显示收起态。资料列表自身使用 `overflow-y: auto`、`overscroll-behavior: contain` 和稳定滚动条槽，条目增加时不撑高窗口。问候语池由共享规范化器限制为最多 12 条、每条 80 字，并兼容旧数据库中的单条纯文本；链接限制为最多 30 条安全 HTTP(S) 地址，并在后台“看板娘管理”页的 IRIS 分区一起编辑。编辑页加载完成前不显示可编辑草稿，加载完成后仅 token 改变会重新取数，父层重渲染与字段换焦不会覆盖未保存输入。入口使用 safe-area 右偏移、保持现有宽度并以 1.56 宽高比把原高度减半，同时维持 44px 以上触控面，低于弹窗遮罩与手机菜单层；样式按 entry、shell、greeting、portrait、archive、records 与 Bright School winner 分开持有，扫描和边缘流动具备 reduced-motion 停止态。
- 商店窗口在同一 `ModalDialog` 内挂载扎希拉商铺与残星会服装店，两页用 240ms transform 平移切换并在 reduced-motion 下取消过渡；每页保留独立目录、批次、1 秒刷新冷却和错误重试状态。窗口自身是固定裁切容器而非滚动 owner，因此 `window-redesign.css` 显式使用 `overflow: hidden` 与 `scrollbar-gutter: auto`，抵消通用 modal 的双侧稳定滚动条槽，使 header、200% 双页滑轨及两家商店背景在桌面和竖屏都直接贴合边框内缘。Bright School 竖屏首页的通用 `max-width` 保护只作用于 `.home-screen` 后代，不能压缩商店的 200% 双页滑轨。扎希拉 `data-store="zahira"` 页头使用简单的蓝灰—灰紫低细节配色条，不裁切或模拟主体桌面场景；页头只渲染一份当前激活目录的实时金币余额，顺序固定为刷新、标题、金币、关闭，不再在两家接待区复制钱包。手机最终层使用 `44px minmax(0, 1fr) max-content 44px` 四列网格，把通用模态框关闭按钮恢复为静态网格项，使关闭和刷新两个 44px 控件垂直居中，并让金币紧邻关闭按钮左侧。服装店使用版本化深红粗蜡笔舞台图，页头标题固定为不换行且完整显示的“残星会cosplay部”；`data-store="costume"` 的页头复用桌面舞台图顶部帷幕，并以深红、黑梅低对比色块延续粗蜡笔舞台，暖象牙标题与现有浅色纸面按钮在桌面和竖屏共用同一语义 owner。两页切换按钮共同使用 `.shop-switch-button`，文案只保留实时 DOM“残星会”与“扎希拉商店”；路标改用 ImageGen 生成的 `1420x430` 透明无损贴纸 WebP，扁平箭头板、极短支撑脚、暖白模切边、酒红粗轮廓、少量蜡笔纹和两颗简化钉点全部在图内，不再绘制写实木纹、绳结、裂口或立体倒角，右向原图只在 `::before` 图片层通过 `scaleX()` 镜像成左向，按钮文字不会反转。对应透明 PNG 作为源图保留，WebP 通过 `SHOP_DIRECTION_SIGN_IMAGE` 进入商店关键图片预加载；旧 CSS 渐变板面、clip-path 外形和铆钉伪元素不再渲染。桌面与竖屏触控面都至少 48px 高；hover/focus 保留轻微上移、旋转和提亮，但不再应用 drop-shadow，避免移动阴影与背景产生错位，active 仅轻压，reduced-motion 下不位移。桌面图按左侧约 34% 娜波摩接待、右侧约 65% 最多五件服装的低细节舞台构图，竖屏独立图按上方约 57% 商品舞台、下方约 43% 看板娘/气泡接待区构图。两张图都使用与扎希拉背景一致的蜡笔颗粒、纸张纹理、歪斜轮廓和简化色块，并以居中 `cover` 覆盖 `.costume-store-panel`；最终移动层通过 `--costume-shop-mobile-background-image` 直接选择竖图，不运行时裁切横图，也不叠加旧幕布伪元素。两端的服装商品舞台都复用扎希拉商店的实际容器测量与 `layoutShopCards` 数量感知排布，按当前 1–5 件商品动态选择单排或平衡双排、逐行居中且不保留空槽；每批服装复用 6–9px 浮动值与 4.2–6 秒周期，桌面按 2 倍呈现 12–18px 总行程，竖屏按 1.5 倍呈现 9–13.5px 总行程，悬停/聚焦/按下时暂停，减少动态效果时停止连续动画。五张商品图使用裁到 alpha 内容边界的矩形 WebP，`.costume-shop-art` 随实际立绘尺寸收缩，售价绝对定位到该锚点右下角并与可见立绘少量重叠；该视觉层在不改变共享槽位、点击框或排布算法的前提下，桌面缩放为 1.14、竖屏缩放为 1.18，立绘与售价共同位于旋转/浮动包装内并始终同步变换。桌面悬停保留原位移动效但卡片背景保持透明，只在立绘后方显示局部暖金舞台光。旧钱包 WebP 保留为历史资产但不再运行引用或预加载；未拥有对应角色的服装卡只灰化立绘，仍可打开详情，购买按钮不可用。
- 部员手册角色详情的衣服图标始终可点，桌面位于立绘区右上角、移动端移到左上角以避开关闭按钮。服装选择通过 portal 挂到主题应用根并使用独立顶层遮罩，不受部员手册父窗口的布局与裁剪影响；窗口只显示“{角色名}的衣柜”标题，不显示副标题、“只有默认服装”等解释性文案；列表固定“默认服装”第一、当前非默认服装第二、其他已拥有服装随后、未拥有服装最后。卡片主体打开详情且必须保持透明、无边框和无额外阴影，让外层卡片承担与部员手册角色卡片一致的纸卡轮廓；独立衣服按钮即时装扮。当前服装仅以外层浅绿色卡片背景表达，并通过 `aria-current` 保留语义，不显示“装扮中”文案。服装详情在桌面与移动端都直接复用扎希拉商品详情模板：立绘位于 `.shop-detail-art` 图像框，右侧/下方文案区依次显示“{角色名}服装”分类胶囊、服装名、左对齐 illust 和独立说明框；不渲染持有状态区域，竖屏继续沿用商品详情的一列响应式布局。非默认服装的衣柜缩略图与全局实际装扮立绘读取同一套逐服装缩放/偏移校准；校准使用独立 CSS transform 属性与既有交互动效合成，不改变卡片布局盒，也不作用于商店商品图和详情大图。未拥有服装与未拥有角色的装扮按钮使用原生 disabled，不增加额外提示；默认服装不灰化。移动端规则由 `modals/character-opening/costume-wardrobe-mobile.css` 单独拥有服装列表，服装网格为两列、内部滚动，卡内立绘与装扮按钮纵向排列并水平居中；服装详情使用应用根下的第二个固定定位顶层遮罩，与衣柜窗口互为同级，不能作为衣柜内部的相对定位子层。
- Board move preview and mobile touch-confirmation markers must stay centered by the `.point` absolute coordinate contract: each point receives `--board-point-center-x/y`, sits on the same intersection as SVG grid lines, and centers its preview pseudo-element internally. The shared `.board .point` rule repeats `transform: translate(-50%, -50%) !important` so later generic button hover/active transforms cannot shift board points into grid cells. ChangLi's extra-turn placements reuse ordinary move placement hints, so they must stay on the same intersections as stones and SVG grid lines. Mobile touch confirmation must render only the inner `.touch-confirm-marker`; `.point.touch-confirming::before` stays hidden, and confirmation feedback may brighten the point but must not drop the centering transform. If a mobile press state scales a point, it must compose with `translate(-50%, -50%)`, and reduced-motion states must keep the same translate so stones do not animate from grid cells back to intersections after placement.
- Tailwind CSS is available only as a low-intrusion utility layer through `src/styles/tailwind.css`, imported from `src/styles.css` after `hud-components.css` and immediately before `src/styles/themes.css`. It uses the Tailwind v4 Vite plugin, imports `tailwindcss/theme.css`, `src/styles/tailwind/tokens.css`, and `tailwindcss/utilities.css` with `prefix(tw)` on Tailwind imports and `source("../")` on the utilities import, and deliberately omits preflight so Bright School, board, modal, and mobile safety layers keep their existing element and component contracts. Tailwind is a staged long-term target with its full execution plan persisted at `.trellis/tasks/07-01-tailwind-migration-roadmap/tailwind-full-migration-plan.md`: phase 1 records contracts and the `@theme inline` semantic token scaffold without migrating JSX, phase 2 pilots `tw:` utilities in new or isolated admin/tooling surfaces, phase 3 creates UI primitives, phase 4 migrates shared modal/list/card/form internals, phase 5 migrates home/lobby/commerce non-gameplay flows, phase 6 tokenises Bright School, and phase 7 reduces final mobile safety layers only after component ownership and visual checks exist. The current Phase 2 pilot is limited to admin table shells: `AdminAudit` first proved the `tw:max-w-full`/`tw:overflow-x-auto` utility path, and Phase 3 now routes all admin `.admin-table-wrap` shells through `AdminTableScroll` backed by `src/ui/primitives/ScrollArea.jsx` while existing CSS keeps visual shell rules. Phase 3 also includes `src/ui/primitives/Badge.jsx` for low-risk inline status badge layout utilities, `src/ui/primitives/EmptyState.jsx` for admin table empty-cell alignment/spacing through `AdminTableEmpty`, and `src/ui/primitives/Button.jsx` for admin action alignment through `AdminActionButton`, with visual styling still owned by existing CSS. Phase 4 has started only with `src/modals/modalComponents.jsx` `ModalActionButton`, used by `ConfirmModal`, `MessageBoardModal`, simple `AnnouncementModal` retry/load-more actions, the `PersonalizationModal` save action, the `MailboxModal` attachment claim action, the `FriendsOverlays` duel-mode cancel action, and the `UserProfileCard` profile report submit action to route modal action alignment through `Button` while preserving existing `.danger-action`, `.secondary-action`, and `.primary-action` visual contracts. Current room gameplay, board geometry, skill presentation, Pixi canvas hosts, player-facing button restyling, mailbox list rows/delete controls, friends match-mode option buttons, profile confirm panels, profile social action buttons, Bright School final mobile gameplay safety, and mobile gameplay controls stay CSS-entry owned.
- Current Phase 5 has started only with `src/home/homeComponents.jsx` `HomeActionButton`, used by the home match-mode picker cancel action to route alignment through `Button` while preserving the existing `.secondary-action` visual contract. The match-mode option buttons, modal spacing contracts, home artboard, utility entries, player plaque art, decorative imagery, and final mobile safety rules remain CSS-owned until a full home/lobby/commerce surface has focused desktop/mobile tests and visual checks.
- Current Phase 6 has started only as a no-visual-change token contract: `src/styles/tailwind/tokens.css` maps Bright School paper, clean surface, ink, border, accent, and paper-shadow variables into Tailwind semantic tokens, while Bright School value ownership remains in `surface-contracts/final-root-surfaces.css` and `quality-base/refinement-foundation.css`. Current Phase 7 has started only as `cssLayerInventory` registration for the `mobile-adaptive.css` final guard reduction candidate; the final mobile layer remains after `themes.css` until replacement component ownership is proven on phone portrait, small landscape, narrow desktop, and regular desktop.
- Accent typography is semantic and opt-in. `src/styles/base/foundation.css` owns `"Sigrika Accent Latin"` plus `--font-display-accent`, `--font-numeric-accent`, `.text-display-accent`, `.text-clock-value`, and `.text-rating-value`; the font is not a global UI replacement. Chess clock digits use `.text-clock-value`, player-visible rating values use `.text-rating-value`, and only player-side atmosphere labels such as the login `SigrikaGo` subtitle use `.text-display-accent`. Home student-ID plaque rank digits are a narrow owner-CSS exception: `.plaque-mode-rank` uses `--font-numeric-accent` directly for the displayed number only, without applying `.text-rating-value` to non-rating data. These accent classes use `text-transform: uppercase` so lowercase Latin source text visually renders as uppercase art lettering without mutating the underlying string. Usernames/UserIdentity, admin, body copy, chat/announcements, form inputs, coins, prices, stock, probabilities, game-count/record numbers, room codes, move counts, dates, timestamps, and leaderboard rank positions keep the normal UI font. Because Bright School has late broad readability guards with `!important`, `src/styles/mobile-adaptive/semantic-accent-typography.css` is the final post-theme guard that reapplies the semantic font tokens and uppercase transform, restores clock state colors and timer track variables, clears accidental background/border/shadow from `.text-clock-value`, keeps main-time clock digits at dark `#1c171a` instead of gray while preserving byo-yomi warning colors, deliberately avoids changing `.digital-timer` card background or border styling, and applies a mobile-only compact clock sizing override so player-strip timer tracks stay inside their container after the art font loads.
- Window-title typography is a separate semantic contract. `src/styles/base/foundation.css` registers `public/assets/fonts/LXGWMarkerGothic-Regular.ttf` as `"Sigrika Window Title"` and exposes `--font-window-title`; `.text-window-title` applies it to the home main title, while dialog/backdrop-scoped `h2` selectors cover player-facing window titles without changing body copy, controls, subtitles, or numeric text. The final semantic typography guard repeats the title family with `!important` so Bright School's broad Microsoft YaHei reset cannot overwrite it on desktop or mobile. Warehouse and leaderboard headers use the shared paper-title treatment in ordinary Bright School; other themes retain plain headings. Neither adds an icon or subtitle. The friends window owns a separate `.friends-modal-header` titled “社交系统” above its tabs/search toolbar; portrait layouts keep header, toolbar, and scrollable list as three grid rows so the new title does not displace the list scroll owner. Bright School keeps the `.friends-tabs` wrapper transparent, borderless, and shadowless while retaining each tab button's own surface and selected-state feedback.

- Startorch home terminal styles are now an import-only lobby skin entry. `src/styles/home-terminal.css` owns import order and delegates concrete rules to `src/styles/home-terminal/shell-background.css`, `top-strip.css`, `layout-player.css`, `entries.css`, `utility-footer-motion.css`, and `mobile.css`; `src/styles/styleContract.test.js` guards that directory and import map.
- Shared mobile home layout styles are now import-only as well. `src/styles/mobile-home.css` delegates the base 900px mobile/tablet rules, the 620px narrow-phone fallback, and the 900px landscape fallback to `src/styles/mobile-home/`, with `src/styles/styleContract.test.js` guarding the import order.
- Bright School portrait mobile home shell is now a nested import-only entry: `src/styles/themes/bright-school/mobile/home-shell.css` owns import order, while `src/styles/themes/bright-school/mobile/home-shell/` contains shell-base, top-strip-menu, main-stage, player-plaque, and entries-utility-footer so future mobile home, menu, plaque, and footer rules stay in focused files.
- 首页 Header 不再显示或保留在线人数占位。`HomeHeader` 在品牌区与操作区之间渲染无障碍隐藏、不可拖拽、不可点击的 `<picture class="home-header-mascot">`：普通模式加载 `/assets/home/home-header-sigrika.gif`，`prefers-reduced-motion` 加载透明静态首帧 `/assets/home/home-header-sigrika-still.webp`。两者保留 800x800 透明画布；五个 GIF 帧的非零 alpha 边界均为 `(150,184)-(663,704)`，每帧延时由 100ms 调整为 GIF 可表达的 170ms，使播放速度降低约 40% 且不重编码画面；共享 `top-strip.css` 通过 514x521 可见盒、`155.642%` 原图宽度、`-29.183%` 横向补偿和 `11.875%` 底部透明区补偿精确锚定角色。Bright School 桌面端使用 `82-94px` 可见宽度紧跟版本号，并以 `34-37px` 下沉量让身体接触线靠近、但不覆盖 Header 黑边；竖屏移动端使用 `brand mascot actions` 单行三列、`72-92px` 可见宽度、`28-34px` 下沉量、12px 列间距和独立 44px 菜单列，衣服下沿停在黑边上方，双手继续越界，看板娘与折叠菜单保持明确间隔。移动端隐藏整个桌面 `.topbar-actions` 容器，只保留折叠菜单；最终 `mobile-adaptive/bright-school-overrides/home-header-menu.css` 重申这一结构，并把展开面板强制设为 `left: auto; right: 0` 与紧凑 148px 安全宽度，防止后置通用 44px 触控规则把面板压窄或向屏幕外展开。
- Bright School phone portrait home must remain usable in portrait. The portrait home shell displays the main stage as a single-column flow (`player`, `match`, `manual`, `utility`) and keeps the GPT Image 2 utility dock as a 2x3 image-only button grid with native `button` semantics and `aria-label`s; it must not hide the stage behind an orientation guard.
