The utility grid decouples art size from layout density: two centered 46% tracks and three `max(52px, 18vw)` rows move the button centers closer together, while the mobile motion wrapper expands to 132% to preserve the previous visible art scale. The grid keeps a compact `2px` gap and the art's right/bottom transparent shadow bleed, so all six entrances read as one group without shrinking their hit areas or clipping shadows. The phone-only match and member-handbook image tracks stay compressed so the utility dock remains inside the mobile main-panel frame, but the alpha-trimmed match entry owns the larger track (`clamp(176px, 52vw, 224px)`) and the handbook entry owns the smaller secondary track (`clamp(148px, 42vw, 188px)`) so matching cannot appear smaller than `部员手册`; each utility button keeps a 52px-plus touch target.
- Bright School 首页主面板背景由设备图源驱动：桌面主面板使用 `/assets/home/home-main-panel-desktop.webp`，移动/竖屏使用 `/assets/home/home-main-panel-mobile.webp`。图源挂在 `.home-main-panel.home-terminal-main` 上并以 `background-size: 100% 100%` 跟随面板宽高伸缩；`mobile-adaptive/home-narrow-desktop/` 的 1024-1180px 与 701-1023px 兜底仍保留横版图的木框/纸面安全内边距，手机 portrait 的 `mobile/home-shell/main-stage.css` 负责切换竖版背景和安全内边距，内部 `.home-stage` 继续透明。
- Bright School desktop student ID card styling is now a nested import-only entry: `src/styles/themes/bright-school/home/student-id-card.css` owns import order, while `src/styles/themes/bright-school/home/student-id-card/` contains card-shell-avatar, identity-name, user-identity-tag, and mode-stats rules.
- Bright School commerce shop polish is a nested import-only entry through `src/styles/themes/bright-school/commerce/shop.css`; `window-redesign.css` owns the paper shop window, semantic header, product-stage cards, wallet, dialogue, and portrait composition, while `card-badges.css` owns the late-theme category and quantity corner badges without growing the main owner past the CSS size contract. Desktop reserves the upper-left stage for at most five cards in balanced 2+3 / 2+2 / 2+1 rows, with seeded jitter confined to 28px-safe cells; lower-left remains the wallet and lower-right remains Zahira. Dialogue is bottom-anchored above the rendered portrait instead of sticking to the header, so its tail stays visually connected without covering cards or art. Card rotation is fixed per batch while the transform-only vertical float pauses on hover, focus, press, and purchase and is removed by reduced-motion.
- Bright School commerce warehouse/profile polish is also a nested import-only entry: `src/styles/themes/bright-school/commerce/warehouse-profile.css` owns import order while `src/styles/themes/bright-school/commerce/warehouse-profile/` contains typography-numbers, profile-stats, warehouse-header, warehouse-empty, warehouse-item-card, and warehouse-item-mobile rules.
- Bright School hard-shadow cards inside scrollable or clipped modal regions reserve right/bottom bleed on their content owners rather than weakening the shadows. The house manual character list, leaderboard list and pinned current row, warehouse grid, shop layout/sidebar lane, and friends list each keep explicit desktop/mobile padding or scroll-padding so right and lower shadows are not cut at the modal edge.
- Startorch HUD hardening is also a nested import-only entry: `src/styles/hud-components/hud-hardening.css` owns import order while `src/styles/hud-components/hud-hardening/` contains tokens-shell-scrollbars, inputs-settings-auth, inventory-state-tags, home-hologram-entries, character-deploy-detail, shop-pagination-owned, warehouse-surfaces, and friend-actions rules.
- Shared UserIdentity cosmetics are also nested import-only through `src/styles/hud-components/user-identity.css`, delegating fixed-ratio nameplate primitives, surface-specific alignment hooks, and phone scale hooks to `src/styles/hud-components/user-identity/` while keeping the shared HUD cascade order stable.
- Shared mobile battle shell/dock styling is also a nested import-only entry: `src/styles/mobile-room/base-shell-dock.css` owns import order while `src/styles/mobile-room/base-shell-dock/` contains shell-header-menu, flat-control-reset, viewport-dock-shell, player-timer-strip, board-viewport, dock-tabs-actions, and decision-chat-panel rules.
- Startorch terminal modal system styling is also a nested import-only entry: `src/styles/modals/terminal-system.css` owns import order while `src/styles/modals/terminal-system/` contains tokens-backdrop, modal-chrome, close-header-actions, terminal-buttons, replay-profile-surfaces, result-modal, and outcomes-resume-actions rules.
- Bright School portrait mobile house/profile styling is also a nested import-only entry: `src/styles/themes/bright-school/mobile/house-profile.css` owns import order while `src/styles/themes/bright-school/mobile/house-profile/` contains shell-profile-stats, character-grid-cards, owned-decorations, and character-detail-music rules.
- 部员手册角色详情的共享 BGM 控件保持原生播放键、只显示当前曲名的单行标题和浮动选曲纸页；普通技/派生技身份只在选曲纸页页签中出现。Bright School 闭合控件改用 `/assets/characters/bright-school-radio-player.png` 透明手绘收音机，Rough.js 装饰层已移除；桌面以 `230x50`、竖屏以 `min(210px, 58vw) x 50px` 等比 `contain` 显示，背景保持透明，避免 alpha 留白露出淡蓝色胶囊。44px 原生按钮按图内左圆圈偏移，内缩圆形键面默认透明、hover/focus 绿色、按下红色；播放/加载/暂停图形和 `aria-pressed` 仍由实时 DOM/CSS 状态控制。标题在桌面右侧保留 62px、竖屏保留 58px，裁切边界停在波形窗之前。曲名只有实测溢出且距离大于 1px 时才单向滚动，长标题切换短标题不会残留 0px 动画；选曲页内只有当前 hover/focus 的溢出行滚动。共享和主题播放器 CSS 继续使用现有 import-only 拆分，竖屏仍将播放器内联放在标题旁并保持 44px 触控目标。
- Bright School portrait mobile commerce and warehouse layout remains a nested import-only entry, but the shop's final post-theme shell contract lives in `src/styles/mobile-adaptive/shop-window-redesign.css`, `shop-window-card-layout.css` owns final card-size isolation and internal density, `shop-window-compact.css` owns the under-620px reception-area compression, and `src/styles/themes/bright-school/commerce/shop/background-crayon.css` owns the themed background layers without expanding the structural window file. The shell owner explicitly clears shared modal padding/gap and gives the header/body the full paper width instead of using negative-margin expansion. The shop body owns a restrained crayon campus-store background: a pale-blue display wall, mint reception wall, and warm-brown counter are rendered as static CSS layers with low-frequency translucent strokes, paper-fiber highlights, and irregular clipped edges instead of flat bands, repeated stripe textures, runtime SVG/noise filters, animation, or a new raster dependency. Desktop keeps the display boundary around the 68% horizontal product lane; final mobile CSS changes the display-wall clip polygon to a slightly uneven 56% horizontal edge matching the product-stage height. The card owner beats the portrait-wide `max-width: 100%` guard, legacy shop-card minimum heights, centered shrink-to-content grid columns, item-price right alignment, and inherited action sizing with `.shop-window`-scoped rules, so scale is applied once, the rendered card stays within its algorithmic slot, every purchase action fills the same content width, and the price element itself is centered. The product stage never scrolls; five cards use centered 2+3, four use 2+2, and three use 2+1. Horizontal geometry keeps 4–5px gaps, while the vertical layout reserves rotation, float, and hard-shadow bleed so the visible clearance remains similar and the bottom purchase controls are not clipped; enlarged product art, compact name/price rows, the bottom purchase action, and both corner badges still scale as one unit with no fixed minimum. The shared 4–6px float parameter is doubled only for desktop into an 8–12px total travel; mobile resets it to 4–6px and reduced-motion still disables continuous floating. Dialogue and wallet sit below the products, the wallet stays clear of Zahira's visible hair, and the compact owner scales the reception assets apart on short screens; refresh/close retain 44px controls and safe-area clearance.
- Bright School portrait mobile list/settings/social layout is also a nested import-only entry: `src/styles/themes/bright-school/mobile/lists-settings.css` owns import order while `src/styles/themes/bright-school/mobile/lists-settings/` contains list-scroll-widths, watch-rows, friends-rows, table-shells, leaderboard-cards, replay-cards, and toolbars-card-borders rules. Friend user rows inherit the shared `var(--bright-sheet)` warm paper surface used by dossier cards on desktop and portrait mobile; ordinary rows do not use a pale-blue status fill.
- Bright School profile color ownership follows the shared dossier structure. `.profile-resume-view`, record-panel/overview rails, the secondary-action rail, and the mode-tab wrapper stay transparent; the identity hero and character section are large `#fffaf0` hard-shadow cards, the four summary items are independent medium cards, recent results is its own medium card, and each semantic character row is one short-shadow card mixed with about 7% catalog `palette`. Achievement, coin-wallet, personalization, and replay retain their pink, gold-gradient, blue, and light-green semantics, while social controls remain like, friend, blacklist, report with 18px icons, a numeric like count, and a light blood-red report surface. Profile identity and record portraits ignore scene costume transforms and use centered 80% `contain`; record masks remain transparent/borderless at 68×56px desktop and 46×46px portrait-mobile. Username/nameplate scale is `1.4` desktop and `1.288` mobile, exactly 70% of the previous enlargement, and equipped tags are pinned to their shared width/height variables so the 3.75:1 asset slot is not squeezed. The visible `.profile-character-table-head` is used on desktop and portrait: “角色战绩” occupies the first column with the shared window-title font at `1.5rem`, while the five compact metric labels share its bottom baseline. Portrait metric labels mirror their data cells' centered alignment and `2px` inline padding; the first heading cell mirrors the identity cell's left alignment and `4px / 5px` padding. The transparent non-scrolling row reserves no visible scrollbar gutter. The semantic `thead` remains visually collapsed for accessibility, while `.profile-character-table-scroll` is the only character-body vertical owner, hides native scrollbar chrome without disabling wheel/touch/keyboard input, and never scrolls horizontally. Its hidden-scrollbar declarations are repeated after the broad Bright School root scrollbar owner and again in the final portrait layer so the global important paint cannot return. Portrait keeps the 48% identity column and 60px native single-line rows; the non-scrolling `.profile-record-panel` fixes summary/recent data above the record card and gives the inner row scroller at least one complete row at 360×800. Rank/rating help stays above following cards and fully inside the dossier without scrolling the panel. Watch/profile tabs, recent replay placement, standard close controls, late QA import order, and retired-owner exclusions retain their existing contracts.
- Bright School portrait mobile modal shell is also a nested import-only entry: `src/styles/themes/bright-school/mobile/modal-shell.css` owns import order while `src/styles/themes/bright-school/mobile/modal-shell/` contains shell-surfaces and scroll-controls rules, keeping the generic modal sheet contract separate from shared scroll regions, close controls, and touch-target overrides.

## Modal Layering

- Shared `.modal-backdrop` stacks above room `--room-floating-z` surfaces so request and confirmation modals dim skill chips, chat controls, and room member popovers together.
- Mobile home and room floating controls use explicit local z-index tokens instead of ad hoc fixed layer numbers. Bright School portrait home sets `--home-floating-z: 120` on `.home-top-strip` and applies it to `.home-mobile-menu` plus `.home-mobile-menu-panel`, keeping the folded action menu above the home main panel. Mobile battle rooms set `--room-floating-z: 120` in base and final mobile layers; opened room menus, chat widgets, chat popovers, and `.mobile-room-dock:has(.chat-widget.open)` use that token while preserving modal-backdrop overrides that intentionally lower room controls under active modals.
- Player story playback uses a dedicated top modal layer on `.onboarding-story-backdrop`, above ordinary `.modal-backdrop` sheets and the final mobile modal safeguard, so item-triggered stories remain clickable while the warehouse or other business modal stays underneath. The story backdrop and the local `.tutorial-session-backdrop` both use the Bright School deep-plum static fill and explicitly disable standard/WebKit live backdrop filtering, preserving desktop/mobile hierarchy without making Chrome resample the full viewport during typewriter, hover, or board updates. Story dismissal is centralized: opening the generic player clears the legacy onboarding-story flag, and closing/skipping/back dismissal clears both story overlay flags plus the active script. Admin embedded story previews reset that backdrop to normal document stacking with `z-index: auto`.
- Mobile room portraits do not render character-chain badges; duplicate-chain data remains in user payloads but is hidden on player-facing portrait surfaces.
- Battle result modals are player-centered. `src/modals/gameLifecycle/ResultModal.jsx` keeps winner color only for result semantics, but the portrait, candy item effects, chain badge, and `UserIdentity` come from the current user's room player when available. The modal emits `result-outcome-win`, `result-outcome-loss`, `result-outcome-draw`, or `result-outcome-spectator`: win shows `赢了耶！` with the existing portrait hop, loss shows `输掉了...` with a grayscale frozen portrait plus `/assets/effects/result-defeat-rain.gif`, and draw/observer states keep the panel compact without victory motion.
- Desktop, Startorch terminal, Bright School, and final mobile result styles must keep the outcome label first and keep loss rain sized from `--result-defeat-weather-width/top`. Result reward tiles are intentionally filled rather than transparent: `result-reward-negative` uses a pale red surface, `result-reward-nonnegative` uses pale green, and `result-reward-coins` uses pale gold, with terminal/Bright School/mobile overrides preserving those semantic fills. The inner `.text-rating-value` in result rewards keeps only the semantic numeric font and must not draw its own background, border, or shadow. Mobile result sheets collapse to one column, cap the portrait through `--result-portrait-size`, and center the confirm action without reintroducing a winner-only layout.

## Feedback Messages

- Player feedback is persisted in the `FeedbackMessage` Prisma model, backed by the `202605250001_add_feedback_message` migration.
- Logged-in users submit feedback through `POST /api/feedback`; the server stores `userId`, current `username`, normalized `content`, and `createdAt`.
- Feedback content strips control characters, trims whitespace, rejects empty submissions, and caps content at 400 characters.
- The home and room message-board modal calls the feedback API. On success it closes the modal and shows a green top toast: `感谢您的反馈！`.
- The message-board textarea uses the same 400-character limit, displays the remaining character count below the input, and defaults to a taller writing area on desktop while mobile clamps the height to the viewport.
- Admins can view the latest 100 feedback messages through `GET /api/admin/feedback`.
- The admin console includes a `留言反馈` tab that displays submit time, username, and full feedback content with wrapping for longer text.

## Mobile Responsive Adaptation

- `src/styles/mobile-adaptive.css` is imported at the end of `src/styles/themes.css`, preserving the existing `styles.css` theme-layer contract while making this the final 320px-768px mobile safety layer. The entry is import-only and delegates concrete rules to `src/styles/mobile-adaptive/`, while keeping existing visual themes and component markup intact and enforcing no page-level horizontal overflow, 44px minimum touch targets for buttons/tabs/icon controls, 16px mobile form inputs, safe-area padding, and media elements constrained to their containers. The focused `src/styles/mobile-adaptive/home-utility-interactions.css` guard owns the phone/tablet Bright School home utility-image hover/focus/active exception so the final layer can beat both theme hover rules and generic mobile press scaling. The portrait mobile battle-room sub-entry `src/styles/mobile-adaptive/mobile-room-portrait.css` is nested import-only and delegates shell/header/menu, neutral chrome reset, viewport shell, player strips, board viewport, dock panels, and action/decision controls to `src/styles/mobile-adaptive/mobile-room-portrait/`. The announcement detail sub-entry `src/styles/mobile-adaptive/announcement-detail.css` is also nested import-only and exists specifically to keep the embedded detail layer the same size as its parent announcement sheet after generic nested-modal and Bright School mobile overrides.
- The shared commerce sub-entry `src/styles/commerce/shop-settings.css` is import-only and delegates shop shell/tabs, settings panel, shop cards, detail/pagination, compact shop media, compact modal safety, and phone layouts to `src/styles/commerce/shop-settings/`. This keeps shop, settings, leaderboard/friends mobile guards, and warehouse/house phone fixes in named files while preserving the existing `commerce-settings.css` import position. Shop card purchase actions must keep unavailable states as native disabled controls and render sold out, already owned, not purchasable, pending purchase, and insufficient-coin buttons with one gray inactive treatment across base, Bright School, and final mobile layers. 商品详情的可选 `illust：{name}` 标签在桌面标题行跟随商品名，移动端堆叠到商品名下方；链接态必须继承颜色、无下划线、无边框、无背景、单行省略，并通过 Bright School 和最终手机层重复 `min-height: 0`，避免全局链接/按钮触控规则撑高标题。
- The shared gacha modal sub-entry `src/styles/commerce/gacha.css` is also import-only and delegates modal tabs, featured stack, machine stage, featured prize, control panel, list/result dialogs, and animations to `src/styles/commerce/gacha/`, preserving the existing commerce layer order while keeping result grid and animation rules in named files.
- The shared recruitment modal sub-entry follows the same import-only commerce style contract through `src/styles/commerce/recruitment.css` and `src/styles/commerce/recruitment/`, with mobile safety rules in `src/styles/mobile-adaptive/phone-recruitment.css`. `modal-shell.css` owns only the ordinary modal grid and heading, while `cinematic.css` exclusively owns the application lock, dimmer, sprite-atlas frames, flight path, glow, and full-screen flash. The modal is intentionally arranged as three stable rows: header information, a bulletin-board main panel for selected/pending/ready/result states, and one-line action controls for recruitment item selection and confirmation on both desktop and mobile. The bulletin-board main panel uses `/assets/recruitment/notice-board-flat-candidate.webp`, and Bright School recruitment overrides must preserve `--recruitment-board-background-image` instead of replacing the second row with a flat color. The image uses proportional `cover` sizing with centered positioning so the second row crops from the vertical center instead of stretching the artwork. The empty state may use the letter-paper surface and success may layer celebration art, while ordinary no-response results remain image-free beyond the consumed recruitment-item icon and result copy; the retired envelope sticker is neither rendered nor preloaded. Modal text and controls stay DOM/CSS-owned for readability and responsive safety. The compact header uses the single title `部员招募栏`; pending recruitment hides the helper label and renders only the remaining time digits as CRT-style green glowing tabular text without a screen background or border; mobile item selection can drop item names and keep only icons plus quantities when the row is too narrow, selected-item confidence copy uses a red secondary cue, and unavailable recruitment confirmation must stay a gray native disabled button in base, mobile, and Bright School layers.
- The Aemeath ticket adds a third center item column only while owned. Its button uses five authored colors around a light, stable, dark-text inner surface, with slow irregular background-position drift and a static readable reduced-motion fallback; normal item ordering remains poster, memorial ticket, radio. Starting it mounts a body portal above all registered modal layers, blocks pointer/touch/keyboard and app-level dismissal, and still runs the full authored presentation under `prefers-reduced-motion` because the sequence conveys the required state transition without strobing. The page begins gradually darkening with the first `999:00` frame; at 3.2 seconds the eight-frame Pink Cyber Angel right-flight loop enters from outside the left edge on a deterministic irregular vertical path, settles over the countdown at 5.2 seconds, switches to a four-frame wave, and glows at 5.8 seconds. The flash uses the same measured target coordinates as the settled sprite, first appearing as a local orb at the character rather than at viewport center, then reaching a fully opaque cover for the 6.25-second sprite/countdown swap. White and dim layers fade away by 7.05 seconds and input unlocks; local completion is remembered separately from interruption, while a receipt-anchored display deadline preserves roughly four visible seconds on portrait mobile even after a slow start response and transitions directly to ready at zero without a refetch wait. The server `readyAt` remains the claim gate. Desktop and portrait mobile remeasure viewport/countdown coordinates on resize, and only page hide/offline/unmount removes the body lock before requesting server interruption.
- The shared warehouse/toast sub-entry `src/styles/commerce/warehouse-toast.css` is import-only and delegates warehouse list rows, character-target item dialogs, toast stack/keyframes, and 560px phone layouts to `src/styles/commerce/warehouse-toast/`, preserving the existing commerce layer order while keeping desktop and mobile warehouse fixes in named files.
- The shared social/profile/leaderboard sub-entry `src/styles/commerce/social-profile.css` is also import-only and delegates modal shells, friends toolbar/search, friends list/status, friend actions/notices, duel request banner, leaderboard table, and owned decoration section rules to `src/styles/commerce/social-profile/`, preserving the existing commerce layer order while keeping social and leaderboard contracts in named files.
- General CSS architecture guard coverage lives in `src/styles/styleContract.test.js`: any CSS file with `@import` must stay import-only, and new or expanded 6000-byte CSS files fail the contract unless the known-debt baseline is intentionally updated during a split plan.
- Full-repo CSS cleanup completion gates live in `CSS_FULL_REPO_CLEANUP_VERIFICATION_GATES`: every CSS stage runs the static CSS contracts, room/board/skill/mobile or broad CSS changes run `npm run verify:battle-fixes`, protected Pixi/skill-presentation changes run `npm run verify:stability -- tests/stability/skill-effects.spec.js`, and final handoff runs `npm run check`.
- The Startorch commerce/social polish sub-entry `src/styles/commerce/terminal-polish.css` is also import-only and delegates terminal headers/profile stats, character cards, shop shell/tabs, item cards/empty states, list/status rows, settings/warehouse target surfaces, and compact phone fixes to `src/styles/commerce/terminal-polish/`, preserving the existing commerce layer order.
- `src/styles/mobile-modals.css` is now an import-only mobile modal safety entry after `room-terminal.css` and before HUD compatibility styles. It delegates backdrop/base mobile constraints, 760px compact modal shells, 560px watch/replay/shop/leaderboard/friends/house-resume phone layouts, and reduced-motion safety to `src/styles/mobile-modals/`, while modal feature tests read the import tree so the split does not weaken the existing layout contracts.
- Bright School's final mobile guard entry `src/styles/mobile-adaptive/bright-school-overrides.css` is also import-only. Its concrete rules are split under `src/styles/mobile-adaptive/bright-school-overrides/` by home/auth header, replay dialog, profile/house records, shop cards, leaderboard cards, and deployed-character state, preserving the original 760px portrait cascade order without keeping one large late override file. The profile/house records sub-entry `src/styles/mobile-adaptive/bright-school-overrides/profile-house-records.css` is nested import-only as well, delegating house/profile stat fixes, profile resume stats, character record dialog rules, and embedded resume character records to `src/styles/mobile-adaptive/bright-school-overrides/profile-house-records/`.
- The Startorch live-room terminal skin keeps `src/styles/room-terminal.css` as the root import point after `mobile-room.css`, but concrete rules now live under `src/styles/room-terminal/` for shell/theme variables, header tags, player/timer/skill panels, board/actions, shared panels/chat/replay, portrait mobile, and landscape mobile. Room tests read the import tree so the skin contract follows the split files instead of depending on one monolithic CSS source.
- Shared room player/timer/skill styling is also nested import-only: `src/styles/room/players-timers-skills.css` delegates side layout, player cards, capture tooltips, timers, skill chips, mobile tap tooltips, and color badges to `src/styles/room/players-timers-skills/`.
- The mobile room layout remains driven by `MobileRoomLayout`, but portrait and landscape breakpoints now prioritize the board as the primary region. Portrait uses a single-column stack with compact opponent/self player strips, a board size calculated from remaining `100dvh` space, and a bounded bottom tab dock. Landscape uses a three-column board-centered grid with player strips as side rails and the dock below, so the board stays visually dominant on phones and narrow tablets. Short landscape player rails hide portrait art, rank/rating tags, capture counters, and skill chips, keeping only the player identity and timer inside the side cards instead of clipping overflow content.
- Ordinary live rooms, spectator views, and record replays do not compose `ChatBox`: there is no chat button, mobile chat tab, readable chat panel, input, or client `chat:send` binding. Server chat events and stored room chat data remain temporarily compatible. `RoomBattleStage` keeps `showTutorialLog=false` by default; `TutorialBattleScreen` is the only production opt-in and renders the existing compact message renderer as readonly `剧情记录` with no send callback. On mobile, the tutorial-record entry fills the dock track beside `操作`; opening it does not replace the short dock panel. `ChatBox` measures the clicked trigger, portals the record into the nearest `.app-shell`, and opens a viewport-clamped floating panel upward from that button. The trigger track and the popup's essential display/fixed-position geometry are component-owned, and the popup is explicitly excluded from generic compact-room chat hiding, so later theme or responsive rules cannot shrink the entry or hide the record. Its log owns a flexible middle row so the available height is used for readable history instead of two or three compressed lines. Desktop operation hints now live below the self player panel in the former chat-control slot instead of under the opponent/member column. Portrait and landscape room shells return to fixed `100dvh`, clipped page overflow, and compact action/member dock ceilings because ordinary rooms no longer need the expanded five-line chat budget. `RoomPeopleList` portals member actions plus profile/confirmation follow-ups into the nearest `.app-shell`, preserving Bright School theme ownership and the shared room floating z-index while escaping dock overflow clipping. `roomViewStatusFor()` still supplies replay control mode, following-live state, and viewpoint, but `RoomHeader` no longer renders the redundant mode/viewpoint subtitle. Player cards remain structural `aside` elements, portrait buttons own viewpoint switching, and mobile battle `UserIdentity` rules override ellipsis only in this surface: legal names remain one line, legacy names wrap completely, and equipped title/badge/nameplate visuals stay present.
- Tutorial-battle exit confirmation uses `结束教学`. When a concrete yellow target ring is visible, wrong move/skill point input always reports `请落子或选择黄圈位置`; hidden-target exercises preserve their authored wrong-point copy. If a hidden-target move enters an NPC wrong-answer branch, the runtime waits until the typewriter has completed, keeps the full line visible for another 1.5 seconds, then advances automatically even when legacy node data says immediate or manual continuation.
- `MobileRoomLayout` is now a shell only. `RoomBattleStage` owns the rendered mobile battle grid, player strips, board slot, and bottom tab dock, so the layout no longer tries to extract composite React children before they render. The mobile action dock keeps player actions in one five-button touch row with 44px-plus targets, short visible text labels, and a bounded operation-hint region, while replay/spectator history uses a separate seven-slot icon row for first, minus-five, previous, centered icon-free move count, next, plus-five, and last. The move count stretches to the same 46px row height for vertical centering; five-move jumps clamp to either endpoint, and the last icon preserves an accessible “回到实时” label without visible copy. The low-frequency member panel stays behind its tab and the optional tutorial record is explicitly gated. Landscape phone layout keeps a larger board-centered grid instead of compressing the board into a miniature preview; in this mode the action tab suppresses the operation hint, uses a 44px no-padding single-row dock, and shifts the dock upward into the board gap so the action controls stay inside the short viewport.
- Modal and sheet behavior is normalized for mobile: shop, house, warehouse, leaderboard, friends, settings, message-board, profile, result, replay, and detail dialogs fit within `100dvh`, keep their outer frame inside the viewport, and assign scrolling to internal content regions. Leaderboard, friend rows, watch rows, and replay tables use mobile cards where compact reading matters; only views that truly need table comparison keep local horizontal scrolling instead of causing page overflow. Bright School player-facing internal scroll owners hide scrollbar chrome with `scrollbar-width: none` / WebKit scrollbar suppression while keeping `overflow-y: auto`, touch momentum, and containment. The final `mobile-adaptive.css` safety layer explicitly keeps friend and watch lists on card-width rows with `overflow-x: hidden`, so later theme repairs cannot accidentally restore the old wide-table scroll behavior.
- Desktop watch-mode tabs keep their three equal columns and use an explicit inline-flex content owner, so the mode subtitle and room count preserve a real `1em` gap instead of relying on an inactive `gap` declaration. The room count uses an equal `1.75em` width and height, centered content, zero padding, and a 50% radius so single- and double-digit values remain circular rather than pill-shaped.
- `src/styles/mobile-adaptive/mobile-window-headers.css` is the final Bright School mobile owner for the dashed utility-window title family: Watch, warehouse, leaderboard, friends, and every resume/achievement/personalization surface that reuses `.house-header`. At widths up to 768px it restores the shared `clamp(20px, 6vw, 28px)` title scale, centers the header on the 44px control row, and reserves 12px before the 2px dashed divider. Watch refresh/close controls stay static inside their action group, so the older 560px responsive rule cannot shrink “对局列表” to 18px or top-align it against the buttons. Information center, shop, recruitment, settings, and IRIS retain their dedicated header owners because their grid, divider, or terminal geometry differs and already provides its own control clearance.
- Mobile interaction feel is owned by the final mobile CSS layers instead of JSX-specific handlers. `src/styles/mobile-adaptive.css` defines shared tap tokens, removes mobile tap highlights, gives buttons/cards/rows a short 120ms press response, keeps internal scroll containers smooth and contained, and adds lightweight backdrop/sheet entrance animations with a `prefers-reduced-motion` fallback. Theme and HUD motion should animate transform, opacity, color, or shadow only; progress/timer bars must not declare `transition: width`, and spring/bounce cubic-bezier curves are avoided in favor of smooth deceleration. Mobile and Bright School overrides must preserve the existing `TimeBar` grid, digit, and track contract instead of adding tutorial-specific centering overrides. 黑化决战棋钟的最终 owner 是 `mobile-adaptive/sigrika-corruption/room-timers.css`：桌面标题使用 `--sigrika-duel-muted`、数字使用 `--sigrika-duel-text`，不得回落到 Bright School 的深棕文字；竖屏显式恢复通用层隐藏的 `.timer-label`，并以 `label digits track` 三列单行布局，让“主时间/读秒”左对齐、具体时间紧随其后、进度轨通过 `minmax(0, 1fr)` 填满剩余水平空间。
- 黑化决战移动端的最终文字 owner 是 `room-mobile-readability.css`：回放手数、折叠菜单文字、系统记录数量角标、小号统计标题、观战视角标记、结果徽标与点按说明浮层必须直接引用黑化语义 token，不能只依赖父级 `color`。特殊房系统记录在竖屏以 Portal 挂到 `.app-shell`，因此 `ChatBox` 必须附加 `.sigrika-duel-system-log`，由 `room-system-log.css` 独立覆盖普通纸张外壳、标题/正文、技能记录、关闭按钮、滚动条和只读输入；只把规则写在 `.sigrika-candy-duel-room` 后代下不会生效。
- The normal/corrupted Sigrika switch uses one app-shell-owned full-screen transition rather than exposing a one-frame theme swap. `mobile-adaptive/sigrika-corruption/transition.css` owns the fixed pointer-blocking veil, directional fault slices, token-derived materials, filter exclusion, and named `100300` layer; `transition-motion.css` owns only the cover/reveal keyframes and its veil-only reduced-motion fallback. Entry visually swallows the normal paper interface, while exit restores it through a lighter paper-toned reveal. Portrait devices keep the same timing but use compositor-friendly transform and opacity only; full-screen `backdrop-filter`, layout animation, and scroll-dependent geometry are forbidden.
- The shared preload stage uses the same 16px orange-gradient track and 48px aspect-preserving endpoint mascot on desktop and mobile. Fill growth uses `scaleX()` rather than a width transition; clipped dots and a travelling highlight create visible motion inside the loaded area, and a blurred warm glow scales from the same origin without drawing an outer capsule edge. The small mascot remains upright while its anchor follows the real endpoint with an ease-out transition, and the track reserves enough horizontal room to keep it inside narrow screens. When progress is unchanged or advances by at most one point the mascot floats up to four pixels; meaningful movement switches to a restrained two-degree glide until 600ms of inactivity. Bright School may retain its paper track and dark outline, but its late meter rules must resolve `--preload-progress-fill` to the owner layered gradient instead of reusing the timer's pink fill. Reduced-motion removes interpolation, travelling highlight, glide, and idle float without moving the mascot or static texture away from the current progress endpoint.
- Bright School's portrait mobile layer is an import-only entry that mirrors the same interaction contract with high-specificity theme rules: home entries, utility buttons, modal controls, shop/warehouse/house cards, table rows, and room dock tabs all get touch-friendly press feedback; board points keep `touch-action: none` so taps place/confirm stones without page drag, while previewable and touch-confirming points get subtle brightness or composed scale feedback without replacing `translate(-50%, -50%)`. Board point buttons are exempt from ordinary button chrome in both shared board CSS and the Bright School refinement-board layer: they stay transparent with no appearance, border, shadow, background image, or inherited 44px min-size, so theme button rules cannot paint a white button sheet above the SVG grid. The `.board-lines` SVG grid is likewise protected from broad media resets by full-size, no max-size, visible-stroke guard rules.
- Bright School star points must render their black dot through `.point.star::after`, not `.point::before`; `.point::before` is the shared move-hover and skill-target preview layer, so desktop star-point hover must still show the ordinary move hint.
- Default black/white stone styles must not use `border` or `inset` shadows to form an outline ring; Bright School default stone overrides must stay ringless. Semantic skill states such as hidden-hand, flipped, and double-move may still use glow rings or state halos.
- Player theme extension is registry-driven. `src/app/visualTheme.js` owns `VISUAL_THEME_OPTIONS` for the Settings > Interface list, including disabled future options, and `VISUAL_THEMES` / `VISUAL_THEME_IDS` for only enabled themes. Future ids such as `club-standard` and `motari-luxury` can appear as disabled settings choices, but `sanitizeVisualTheme()` still resolves them to `bright-school` until their CSS entries are imported, scoped, and visually verified.
- CSS theme refactors must preserve the current cascade boundary: `src/styles/themes.css` imports shared theme contracts, Bright School, then the final `mobile-adaptive.css` safety layer. Board and skill presentation selectors are protected during style cleanup: `.board .point`, `.board-lines`, `.board-effects-canvas`, `.board-wrap` skill state classes, and `src/styles/themes/bright-school/effects/` must not be normalized with broad button/media/pseudo-element cleanup because character skill timing and Pixi/DOM layering depend on those contracts.
- Round-2 CSS cleanup planning is tracked in `src/styles/cssLayerInventory.js` and enforced by `src/styles/cssLayerInventory.test.js`. It classifies styles into reorganizable shared domains, high-risk gameplay/room surfaces, Bright School-specific overrides, final mobile safety layers, and skill-presentation protected surfaces. Round-3 low-risk cleanup must stay inside the reorganizable shared domain list unless the work explicitly enters the board/skill visual regression path.
- The current CSS scalability baseline is also tracked in `cssLayerInventory`: `CSS_DEBT_BASELINE` guards all `src/styles` debt metrics, including the hidden player-window scrollbar, mobile music shop card, left-aligned replay-time, story-player padding, mobile announcement badge, Bright School mobile utility-image feedback containment, the semantic auth header, responsive mascot-panel composition and extended layered paper-rule shelf, the final `bright-school-overrides/auth-login-lockup.css` owner split, and the split IRIS v2 dataflow owners. The 2026-07-31 auth delta tightens the baseline to the current checkout metrics and adds no file, breakpoint, motion, hardcoded-color, or `!important` debt; the IRIS delta records its bounded file/byte/theme/media/reduced-motion increases so every shell, portrait, archive, record, and theme winner remains below the per-file limit. `CSS_Z_INDEX_CONTRACT` registers legacy/system overlays above `1000`, `CSS_MOTION_CONTRACT` records token and reduced-motion expectations, and `CSS_BREAKPOINT_CONTRACT` registers existing desktop, mobile, portrait, landscape, narrow-desktop, pointer, and reduced-motion media-query families. These are non-growth contracts for future UI expansion, not visual redesign instructions.
- The 2026-08-10 corrupted-duel occupied state stays in the existing match owner files and adds 1681 CSS bytes only. Its shallow-yellow surface is `--bright-cream` with existing ink/border/paper fallbacks, while the exact “西格莉卡？正在对局中。。。” label remains the non-color state cue. Existing Bright School late winners now read owner-local semantic variables so the delta adds no CSS file, literal color debt, breakpoint, motion family, important-count growth, or high-z-index declaration; `CSS_DEBT_BASELINE.totalBytes` is refreshed to 1418907 as the new non-growth ceiling.
- The Zhunshibao difficulty picker keeps its shared modal layout, portrait-phone fitting, and Bright School winner rules in three focused `practice-difficulty.css` owners. The inventory records their bounded feature delta, while the pre-existing match-mode and final-control files stay below the 6000-byte guard rather than gaining an oversized-file exemption.
- Round-3 shared-domain cleanup split `base/home-legacy-grid.css`, `base/home-stage-artboard.css`, `hud-components/pop-tech-terminal.css`, `mobile-modals/phone-house-resume.css`, `modals/character-opening.css`, `responsive/phone-portrait-room.css`, `modals/mailbox.css`, and `commerce/recruitment/board.css` into nested import-only directories while preserving their existing cascade positions. Round-4 regression gates are recorded as `CSS_ROUND4_REGRESSION_CHECKS`: static CSS contracts, `npm run verify:battle-fixes` for focused board/skill/mobile units, and `npm run verify:stability -- tests/stability/skill-effects.spec.js` for desktop and mobile Pixi skill visual stability.
- Final mobile safety cleanup uses `CSS_FINAL_MOBILE_SAFETY_SPLITS` instead of the shared-domain list. `mobile-adaptive/mobile-profile-records.css` now delegates to `mobile-adaptive/mobile-profile-records/` for profile shell/hero, character record rows, and footer/resume stat repairs, `mobile-adaptive/phone-core.css` delegates to `mobile-adaptive/phone-core/` for match mode, shell controls, modal tabs, and scroll/detail/result safety, `mobile-adaptive/phone-gacha.css` delegates to `mobile-adaptive/phone-gacha/` for legacy gacha modal, stage, controls, and result dialog survival rules, `mobile-adaptive/home-utility-interactions.css` owns the final mobile Bright School utility-image button feedback override, Bright School final leaderboard cards delegate to `mobile-adaptive/bright-school-overrides/leaderboard-cards/`, Bright School portrait resume modal layout/settings tabs delegate to `mobile-adaptive/bright-school-portrait/resume-modal-layout/` and `mobile-adaptive/bright-school-portrait/settings-tabs/`, and announcement detail mobile sizing delegates to `mobile-adaptive/announcement-detail/` while keeping the final 768px and 760px portrait cascade after themes.
- Theme overlay cleanup uses `CSS_THEME_OVERLAY_SPLITS`. `themes/shared.css` now delegates to `themes/shared/` for player theme tokens, theme settings controls, and player-surface wiring, `themes/theme-components.css` delegates to `themes/theme-components/` for outcome badges, skill chips, and replay row semantic colors, Bright School component repairs such as `foundation-home.css`, `warehouse-character.css`, and `notebook-polish.css` delegate to matching nested directories, and Bright School portrait room `shell-header-menu.css`, `viewport-player-strips.css`, and `dock-actions.css` now delegate to matching directories under `themes/bright-school/mobile/room/` while preserving the `themes.css` order before later Bright School repairs and final mobile safety layers.
- Gameplay room cleanup uses `CSS_GAMEPLAY_ROOM_SPLITS` instead of the shared-domain list. `room/actions-requests.css` now delegates to `room/actions-requests/` for room toggles/action bars, decision/scoring panels, timed request toasts, test tools, replay controls, and disabled action states, `mobile-room/portrait-room.css` delegates to `mobile-room/portrait-room/` for shared portrait viewport, player strip, board/dock, decision, and action-hint rules, and `room-terminal/players-timers-skills.css` delegates to `room-terminal/players-timers-skills/` for the Startorch battlefield player panel, timer, captures, skill chip, detail panel, and sheen keyframe skin while preserving their current import positions before later responsive and theme repair layers.
- Skill presentation cleanup uses `CSS_SKILL_PRESENTATION_SPLITS` instead of the gameplay room list. `room/board/stones-skill-effects.css` now delegates to `room/board/stones-skill-effects/` for base stones, hidden/flip/double-move stone states, transient markers, protocol-ban and liberty-purge DOM marks, and related keyframes, while Bright School `quality-base/refinement-board.css` delegates to `quality-base/refinement-board/` for board point, SVG grid, row-slash, and stone-position guard rules; both preserve their import positions before later stone-effect, grid/scoring, and theme repair layers.
- Commerce and settings controls use mobile grid tabs/pagination with wrapping labels instead of squeezed inline buttons. Shop mobile layout collapses to a top summary strip plus a two-column item grid, with one-column fallback below 360px; card purchase actions retain full-width 44px touch targets.
- Bright School's final mobile layer keeps login and home titles on one line by scaling type within bounded widths instead of truncating the main title. The login brand and login/register segmented control do not add tinted background panels on phones or desktop, so the paper grid remains the dominant surface; the outer login card keeps the same lower-right hard shadow depth as Bright School buttons. The visible login mascot is part of the same `.auth-composition` as that card, so responsive centering and scrolling account for the complete silhouette.
- `AuthScreen` keeps a login-only `/assets/login-sigrika-mascot.webp` rather than reading the shared 西格莉卡 character portrait. Its PNG/WebP pair uses a transparent 640x640 canvas after removing the source image's bottom export line and uniformly fitting the artwork. Exactly 8 transparent raster rows remain below visible alpha; this resolves to roughly the 3px structural-line thickness at the desktop mascot size, so the image touches the supporting rule's top edge without hiding the rule or leaving the earlier gap. The panel sits inside `.auth-composition`; shared custom properties own panel width/padding, horizontal overhang, top reserve, mascot size and divider anchors. The semantic `.auth-panel-header` retains the `.text-window-title` club title, while the auth repair owner extends its dark / `--bright-blue` two-layer, pointer-transparent paper rule left beyond the panel and bottom-aligns the mascot to that shelf. Desktop reserves 118px left and 117px above the 440px card for a 252px mascot. The final `bright-school-overrides/auth-login-lockup.css` owner now uses the existing 900px mobile-layout family rather than portrait-only 760px matching: it sets the left reserve to zero, constrains the composition to the 440px panel width, uses `clamp(148px, 42vw, 164px)` art, shifts the image only 8px into the safe clipped edge, and adjusts title padding so the role and title do not collide across phones, common landscape widths and 768px previews. Login and registration share this stable composition; registration adds only two short label notes, and field errors render only after blur or submit. Password toggles keep a 44px target, selected modes use `aria-pressed`, and the Bright School `.auth-password-toggle:disabled` owner preserves `translateY(-50%)` after the generic pending-button rule so both password controls remain vertically centered during registration submission; reduced-motion removes nonessential transition/press movement. Initial checks at 1280x720, 390x844 and 320x568 show no horizontal overflow; the narrow registration form remains reachable through the auth screen's vertical scrolling, while final visual acceptance remains user-owned.
- The title column also renders the root package version as `v{version}` below the club title. It reuses the `SigrikaGo` subtitle's `.text-display-accent` class so both secondary labels stay the same size and font, clears the title's default lower margin, then applies the same 4px gap used above the title. `.auth-version` right-aligns and shifts 8px beyond the title edge for the requested stagger; the existing 900px responsive title column remains the positioning owner.
- Bright School home image entries use the final import-only `src/styles/mobile-adaptive/home-narrow-desktop.css` safety layer even on desktop breakpoints. Concrete rules live under `src/styles/mobile-adaptive/home-narrow-desktop/` by common region reset, 1181px-1500px wide stage, 1024px-1180px compact stage, 701px-1023px micro-stage scroll, and short-height stack concerns. The member handbook, match entry, and Bright School utility entries are image-only buttons: visible `data-hud`, paper labels, fixed text badges, card bottom label areas, radial glows, blurred halo shadows, and old utility card border/backgrounds are not allowed. Their buttons keep accessible `aria-label`s, the `<img>` nodes are decorative and constrained with `box-sizing: border-box`, `height/max-height: 100%`, and `object-fit: contain`, and the final Bright School entry layers own non-text hover/focus/active feedback. Desktop handbook/match hover/focus rotates the visible entry art clockwise; the six utility buttons use deterministic clockwise/counterclockwise resting tilts on `.utility-entry-art`, then continue in their original direction to tone-scoped hover/focus angles around `3.8deg` to `4.4deg` with shared `scale(1.015)` visual-size compensation. Utility button containers remain untransformed and the desktop 3x2 grid uses fixed rows, so transformed overflow cannot change the main-panel/background height or hit boxes; later narrow-desktop resets must preserve this image-layer ownership instead of restoring container motion or icon-plus-title columns. Touch/mobile cannot depend on hover, must keep at least 44px target size, and must not transform, border, or pad the `.utility-image-entry` button container on hover, focus, or active; mobile feedback stays on the decorative `.utility-entry-art` through filter, shadow, and transform-only lift/press motion so the hand-drawn button frame does not stretch or shift. `prefers-reduced-motion` cancels transform movement while preserving focus visibility.
- Bright School 桌面首页采用四段响应式契约：`>=1181px` 是 wide desktop，主舞台无横向滚动并让匹配入口成为一级视觉主角；桌面主舞台统一使用两大列，左列从上到下堆叠玩家铭牌、部员手册和 3x2 “社团工具箱”，右列只放跨三行的匹配图片按钮。`1181px-1500px` 额外压缩列间距、降低舞台顶部留白、放大右侧匹配入口，并给玩家铭牌保留可读列宽，避免 1280x768 等中等桌面裁切段位面板；`1024px-1180px` 是 compact desktop，使用 `player/manual/utility/match` 命名网格，不允许页面级横向滚动，并保持左操作栈加右匹配主列；`701px-1023px` 是 micro desktop，`.home-main-panel` 提供受控横向滚动，内部 `.home-stage` 固定 `960px` 最小舞台宽度，继续保留左操作栈和右匹配入口的层级；`<=700px` 继续走移动首页布局。主面板本体使用可拉伸的背景板图片，桌面/窄桌面用横版图，移动端用竖版图，窄桌面和低高度兜底不得把面板内边距压回旧平面卡片数值，内部 `.home-stage` 不再绘制自己的板面背景。核心可点击入口必须在正常 grid flow 内；Bright School 玩家铭牌内部保持头像、用户名、段位面板三列固定结构，用户名只能在中间列内缩放和收敛，不能跨列遮挡段位面板；铭牌外壳背景使用 `public/assets/home/student-id-nameplate.webp` 手绘学生证图，PNG 同目录保留为源图，背景按 border-box 铺满整张卡片且不再叠旧粉绿渐变，图片只提供外框和三块安全区，头像、用户名和模式图标/段位由 DOM/CSS 渲染，按钮本体、头像容器和段位面板不再绘制旧背景、边框或 `box-shadow` 外框；`.plaque-stats` 由 `--home-student-id-stats-*` 变量定位到右侧纸页安全区中心，默认桌面中心为 `88% / 52%`、宽高为 `clamp(104px, 24%, 126px) / 70px`，最终 portrait 与窄桌面安全层使用同一右页中心并收束为 `clamp(82px, 24%, 96px) / 60px`；铭牌 grid 第三列直接引用 `var(--home-student-id-stats-width)`，不再用比绝对定位统计层更宽的固定/minmax 列挤压中间用户名列。三模式列只显示收束后的图标和 WuWa 数字，完整 rank 文本保留在 `aria-label`，段数字为红色、级数字为深紫色；装备用户名背景时，首页铭牌 `.user-identity-name` 必须使用共享 `--user-nameplate-font-size`，避免继承外层大字号后只显示半个英文用户名；铭牌按钮本体只使用与部员手册、匹配入口图一致的近距深色 `drop-shadow` 和 hover/focus 小角度旋转，头像与段位内层仍透明且无边框阴影，头像容器通过共享的画框中心和尺寸变量锚定到生成图左侧立绘画框，容器内部立绘继续用 grid/contain 水平垂直居中；旧纸夹伪元素保持关闭且后置 modal/mobile 安全层不得重置铭牌背景；最终窄桌面与 portrait 安全层重复透明外壳、无旧边框、头像图水平垂直居中和段位统计定位合同。低高度桌面允许纵向滚动，footer 只在宽桌面且高度充足时固定在视口右下角。Bright School 工具区每个按钮使用 `public/assets/home/home-utility-*.webp` 手绘横向标签图作为唯一可见内容，DOM 图标和主标题仅作为非 Bright School fallback/可访问语义存在；桌面、compact/micro desktop 与 portrait 手机都不得恢复旧的图标列、文字列、圆角纸片背景或硬边框。工具图与部员手册、匹配入口共用近距深色 `drop-shadow`；桌面六张工具图用固定的正负小倾角形成随手贴放效果，按钮容器保持 `transform: none`，hover/focus 只让 `.utility-entry-art` 沿各自原方向继续旋转到约 `3.8deg-4.4deg`，并统一轻微放大至 `1.015`，避免反向倾角跨过零度产生视觉缩小；active 也只压低图片层。桌面网格固定两行高度并把图宽提高到 `128%`、gap 收紧到 `clamp(4px, 0.55vw, 8px)`，避免悬停改变背景板高度；移动端继续使用既有 `121%` 图片基线与最终交互覆盖，不修改 2x3 布局和触控尺寸。portrait 手机主舞台 gap 保持 8px，入口图必须先裁掉透明空边，匹配行高大于手册行高并把下方工具区保持在主面板内；招募 ready alert 只给图片增加轻微粉色光晕，不重画旧背景。匹配入口下方不展示模式票据；`lobbyStats.matchmakingCounts` 继续用于点击打开的对弈模式选择卡片，规则文案固定为“路数·时间”与“贴目/规则”两行，计数卡片固定贴到对弈按钮最右侧并以图标+人数横排呈现，按钮中央通过 `MatchModeWatermark` 显示图标加 WuWa 英文标签且不参与点击，桌面透明度为 50%，移动端透明度由最终 `mobile-adaptive` 安全层降为 20%；Bright School 竖屏设置弹窗的标题行高和 tabs 三列布局由最终 portrait 安全层控制，防止标题被裁切或 tab 换行。
- 招募工具图当前以 `C:/codex/image/main/Recruit_btn1.png` 为源，仓库内保留同尺寸 `public/assets/home/home-utility-recruitment.png`，运行期使用保留透明通道且满足 PNG 四分之一体积门槛的高质量 `public/assets/home/home-utility-recruitment.webp`；1500x600 画布与透明边距继续服从上述桌面/移动图片层合同。
- Bright School portrait home/profile/shop fixes that must survive theme overrides live in the final mobile safety layer. The home player plaque still reserves the `UserIdentity` column and protects equipped nameplates. Profile/resume windows keep a compact single-row title/action header, shrinkable coin capsule, and reachable 44px controls; the shared dossier keeps portrait and identity side by side, moves context actions to a full-width second hero row, keeps `.profile-record-panel` non-scrolling, changes the four independent statistic cards to a compact 2×2 grid, keeps the ten recent-result markers on one row, and assigns the only profile-body scrollbar to `.profile-character-table-scroll` inside the role-record card. The transparent mode wrapper uses bounded margins and auto width so all three independent tabs remain fully visible on one row. Bright School shop wallet display continues to show only the coin capsule while blue-gem balances remain hidden from shop and resume surfaces.
- Bright School home player plaque stats are centered inside the right-side generated stats panel on desktop and mobile: the stats panel declares an inline-size container, uses `--home-student-id-stats-*` center/size variables for positioning, and its grid span covers the whole plaque before absolute positioning so `left` is not calculated from only the third grid column. It renders three shrinkable centered columns for larger mode icon plus number-only rank while omitting rating points from the plaque. Dan numbers are red, kyu numbers are deep purple, and the visible digits use the WuWa numeric font through owner CSS.
- Bright School mobile player plaque mode stats use a 20% smaller mobile-only size contract than desktop: `mobile/home-shell/player-plaque.css`, `home/mobile-compact.css`, and the final `mobile-adaptive/bright-school-portrait/home-player-plaque.css` set `.plaque-mode-icon` to `clamp(19.2px, 17.6cqw, 24px)` and `.plaque-mode-rank` to `clamp(14.4px, 13.6cqw, 19.2px)`. Desktop and narrow-desktop rules keep `clamp(24px, 22cqw, 30px)` / `clamp(18px, 17cqw, 24px)` so the mobile adjustment does not shrink medium desktop plaques.
- The plaque stats layer is visually a right-page panel but must use the full `.home-player-plaque.tactical-id-card` grid span as its absolute positioning basis. Do not anchor it to the third grid column, because percentage-based `left` values would then push the third mode column past the generated shell edge.
- Resume and user-profile records share the fixed accessible columns 角色、对局、胜、负、和、胜率. Desktop and portrait render `.profile-character-table-head` as a transparent non-scrolling grid above the row scroller while retaining a visually collapsed semantic `thead`; its visible first cell is “角色战绩”, reuses the dossier window's `.text-window-title` typography at `1.5rem`, and bottom-aligns with the five compact metric labels. The header and row scroller use a shared `4px` grid gap without changing the existing `7–8px` spacing between record cards. Every record remains one native six-column single-line row. On portrait, metric headers and body metrics share centered alignment and `2px` inline padding, while the first header and identity cells share left alignment and `4px / 5px` padding. Only the labelled, focusable row wrapper scrolls vertically; late theme and final-mobile winners hide its native scrollbar chrome across Firefox/WebKit/legacy Edge while wheel, touch and keyboard scrolling remain available, the upper statistics/recent cards stay fixed, and no layer scrolls horizontally. Both replay actions stay beside “最近十盘”; blacklist is a top identity action immediately before report. Disabled replay, relationship, liked-today, and pending states preserve native disabled semantics and the shared inactive treatment.
- User profile hero cards are left-aligned on desktop. Desktop uses portrait, identity, and context actions in three columns; portrait keeps portrait and username/nameplate in the first row and places context actions in a full-width second row. Social actions are like, friend, blacklist, report; only the like count is visible beside its icon, while the other three controls are icon-only with accessible names. Self resume replaces those controls with one full-width “个性化” action. Disabled states remain native gray controls across base and Bright School layers.
- Friend-list action rows and room member username popovers keep unavailable social actions as native `disabled` controls with gray inactive styling across the base, Startorch HUD, and Bright School theme layers; the not-yet-open direct-message entry stays disabled in both surfaces.
- Mobile leaderboard rows in the final Bright School layer remove extra left list inset, shrink the rank badge column, enlarge character portraits, and center the username/nameplate plus rank stack so the player rank sits directly below the username on the same vertical center axis. Compact leaderboard `UserIdentity` names also expose a fit font-size token, letting legacy/generated usernames shrink to remain readable before the name would clip. Current-user leaderboard rows, including the pinned "my ranking" row, use a light green surface on desktop and mobile to distinguish player-owned leaderboard information from ordinary entries without changing the section title treatment; the rank-position number itself remains transparent so emphasis stays on the row surface rather than a second colored badge.
- Selected tab-like controls and selected option buttons keep one pressed-state language on desktop and mobile. Active, `aria-selected`, and `aria-pressed` controls use a shorter shadow, slight downward transform, and inset shadow so the selected state looks physically pressed into the paper surface rather than only color-highlighted.
- Achievement and personalization modal headers use a shared two-column title/close grid on desktop and mobile. Bright School's final portrait layer repeats the static close-button placement so nested modal close controls stay on the title row instead of drifting onto the divider.
- Desktop resume modal headers use a four-column title, shortcut, wallet, and close layout. The wallet group and close button both sit in right-aligned static grid columns so currency capsules cannot overlap the close control.
- Desktop resume modal bodies use a fixed-height internal grid instead of letting the whole modal scroll; the embedded character-record section owns the vertical scrollbar, while `.profile-grid.top-stats-bar` remains overflow-visible so rating/rank help tooltips are not clipped by the stat region. Desktop user profile detail cards keep the same pattern by bounding `.profile-character-list`, while character detail headings reserve close-button space so the compact character BGM player and top-right close control do not overlap.
- Bright School character detail BGM controls use the hand-painted radio PNG on desktop and a separate portrait size contract, with transform-only hover/press feedback. Character headings keep the name and optional `CV：{cvName}` in the left column: desktop keeps the CV label on the same baseline, while mobile stacks it below the name. CV links inherit color, omit underline/color shifts, and retain focus-visible outline. Both the Bright School mobile layer and final `mobile-adaptive/bright-school-portrait` guard repeat the `minmax(180px, 210px)` player column, `min(210px, 58vw)` width, 50px height, transparent background, painted-circle button offset, and central-title safe margins so the waveform window remains visible.
- Personalization equipment categories use compact style-picker entry buttons instead of listing every decoration inline. The nested picker shows default plus unlocked options, auto-closes on selection, and updates the try-on preview immediately; mobile uses a single-column internal scroll list while desktop uses an adaptive grid.
- The home footer is rendered from configurable `footerText` site settings. Desktop treats it as a viewport-fixed HUD sibling after `main.home-screen`, so it remains in the lower-right browser corner while the lobby stage scrolls; links use the safe Markdown form `[label](https://example.com)`. The mobile home footer remains part of the normal document flow rather than a fixed overlay, keeping copyright/link text below the content stack and preventing it from covering navigation cards or modal content.
- Self resume and social detail now use the same `HouseReplayDialog`. Its `.standalone-replay-backdrop` is portaled to the themed `.app-shell`, remains above the ordinary profile backdrop, and is dismissed independently without unmounting the dossier. The fixed title/close shell contains `.replay-dialog-list-scroll` as the sole desktop/mobile replay-history scroll and pagination owner; the nested `.replay-table` remains overflow-visible so row gestures reach that wrapper. Replay rows remain card-like with the time cell left-aligned on desktop and mobile, and the retired profile-only replay dialog and scroll classes no longer create a second cascade.
- House replay cards on narrow phones use a bounded three-row grid with fixed 22px player portraits and single-line player names, so date/result/player/move-count content stays inside each colored row at 375px width.
- Mobile shop cards now share one compact internal contract across character, item, decoration, and music categories: preview, name, stock/price, and purchase button stay inside the card with the action pinned to the bottom; disabled purchase buttons remain gray and non-pressing even when Bright School portrait overrides restyle card actions.
- Key responsive breakpoints are `768px` for the general mobile adaptation layer, `768px + portrait` for phone/narrow tablet vertical layouts, `768px + landscape` for short wide devices, and `360px` for the smallest phone fallback. Existing project breakpoints at `900px`, `760px`, `620px`, and `560px` remain in place; the final mobile layer only resolves conflicts and enforces mobile usability guarantees.

- Motion corrections keep lifecycle and state information intact while reducing paint and touch-hover failures. Top-center `.toast` notices retain their three-second lifetime under `prefers-reduced-motion` and switch to an opacity-only final fade instead of being forced to their transparent endpoint after 1ms; achievement notices alone get a 220ms emphasized entrance. Timed room-request progress passes its remaining duration through `--room-request-toast-duration` and shrinks with left-origin `scaleX()` rather than width. Final mobile Bright School utility-image hover lift/filter rules are gated by `(hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)`, while the generic phone press layer transitions only transform and state colors rather than filter or box-shadow. Skill-targeting board, action, and chip states keep their existing color cadence without animated large shadows. Chat and tutorial-record popovers stay mounted but closed with `inert`, `aria-hidden`, hidden visibility and disabled pointer events, then reverse through a 160ms opacity/transform transition; skill-trait popovers use their measured arrow position and above/below placement as the transform origin and entry direction.
- Purposeful reveal motion is limited to four low-frequency hierarchy changes: story reply buttons enter over 180ms with 40ms staggering capped at the fourth item; result rating and coin tiles enter over 200ms with only the second delayed 40ms; settings switches animate only the newly mounted tab panel over 150ms; and achievement toasts use a distinct 220ms entrance while retaining the shared 400ms exit and three-second lifecycle. All use transform/opacity only. Reduced motion removes translation and stagger, leaving an 80ms opacity-only reveal or the existing blanket near-instant presentation. Result reward motion is split into `src/styles/modals/result-reward-motion.css` so the main result surface remains below the concrete-file size guard.
- Desktop modal shells add a deliberately more perceptible first-open pass in `src/styles/modals/window-entry-motion.css`. Ordinary player windows use 260ms vertical/scale motion, leaderboard/friends/watch/message-board data windows and nested detail dialogs use 200ms restrained variants, and information-center panes enter directionally after the shell. Individual `translate`/`scale` properties keep the keyframes active without removing Bright School's static transform anti-bleed winner. Entry animations use `backwards` fill so delayed panes keep their initial frame while settled parent windows release transform components; nested fixed backdrops such as the House character detail therefore stay viewport-relative instead of being clipped by the parent. It applies only above 768px and leaves the existing portrait mobile bottom-sheet motion, story stage, character-opening, and recruitment cinematic unchanged. This product-directed owner intentionally has no reduced-motion branch.

## Achievement And Personalization UI

- 成就与个性化窗口沿用 Bright School / 纸张 scrapbook 视觉：`house-modal` 外壳、8px 控件圆角、纸张边框、柔和浅色背景和现有 modal close-button 契约，不引入新的品牌视觉。
- 移动端 `UserIdentity` 装饰有 768px 专用保护层：已装备的徽章、称号和用户名背景会同步缩小，用户名背景保持左对齐文字，房间玩家条和成员列表会进一步限制名牌宽度，避免手机端出现横向溢出。
- UserIdentity nameplates use the shared fixed-ratio badge contract across desktop and mobile: users without a nameplate keep natural-width text, equipped nameplates use a `3.75:1` background slot, and equipped username text uses shared `--user-nameplate-font-size: calc(15px * var(--user-nameplate-scale))` so text, background, and safe padding scale together. Scene CSS may change `--user-nameplate-scale`, but must not define per-surface arbitrary nameplate font sizes or stretch the tag to the parent width. Nameplate PNG assets should still be alpha-trimmed with `scripts/pngTrim.mjs` and delivered or resampled at `3.75:1`.
- 资料卡头部继续通过 `UserIdentity` 展示已装备的用户名背景，但资料卡局部会清除身份容器、主行和 name-tag 外层的纯色背板、虚线框、描边和阴影；头像在资料卡内也不加额外边框或阴影。移动端点赞、加好友、加入黑名单和举报按钮保持至少 44px 高；除点赞数外只显示图标，举报继续使用独立危险语义。对局回放位于“最近十盘”标题右侧并打开共享顶层弹窗；禁用回放和不可用关系按钮统一使用弱化文字与 not-allowed 光标。加入黑名单先打开独立确认框。举报输入弹窗使用固定视口居中定位，并在短视口内自己滚动，失败信息留在弹窗内且不清除输入内容；最终 `mobile-adaptive/profile-report-dialog.css` 安全层会用高优先级宽度和定位覆盖 Bright School 对 `.room-floating-modal` 的宽面板规则。
- 成就筛选 tab 使用三等分按钮，桌面端列表保持成就名、内容和奖励三列对齐；`unachieved` 行使用灰色背景，`achieved` 行使用浅黄色背景。达成时间通过点击已达成行时从点击位置附近弹出的固定定位时间浮窗展示，不再占用列表列宽，也不参与成就窗口内部排版；浮窗会按桌面或移动视口边界收束。560px 以下隐藏表头并将每条成就变成单列卡片，避免奖励内容挤压。
- 个性化窗口桌面端使用预览区加三列装备面板，称号、徽章、用户名背景分别独立选择；预览区通过 `UserIdentity` 展示保存前试穿效果。装备按钮状态遵循成就 tab 的粉红选中视觉：粉红表示当前已保存并生效，浅绿色表示正在试穿但尚未保存。560px 以下改为单列面板，按钮保持 42px 以上高度和内部省略号，保证移动端可点选。
- 部员手册的出战状态分两层表达：角色卡本体使用浅绿色表示当前出战角色，卡内出战按钮复用 tab/装备按钮的粉红选中视觉；桌面层保留 pressed depth，竖屏最终层则把按钮收为 30px、绝对定位到卡片右下角并使用 1px 轻阴影，避免操作遮挡已放大到 72px 且水平居中的直属角色立绘。该直属图片选择器不能命中嵌套道具图标，后者继续保持 24px；桌面和移动最终层都显式覆盖出战状态，避免 disabled 或通用按钮样式把当前出战按钮洗回灰色。
- `toast.achievement` 使用金橙色高亮渐变和更强阴影，作为成就达成反馈；普通成功/错误 toast 样式不变。
- 新邮件一次性提醒使用独立的 `.toast.mail` 浅绿色语义面，不改变普通成功/错误 toast。邮箱附件领取后，禁用动作按钮在默认、hover、focus 和 active 下都保持中性灰色，Bright School 的后置粉色按钮规则不得覆盖该状态；招募起始道具数量为零时按钮文案为“数量不足”。普通招募等待界面的“神奇小钟表”使用 44px 触控按钮和右上数量角标，数量为零时仍可见但以中性灰禁用；成功后 3 秒内由旋转闹钟、时间波纹和仅影响 transform/filter/opacity 的数字快进动画取代按钮，随后保持完整的 `00:03`、`00:02`、`00:01`。特殊招募与剩余不超过完整 6 秒展示窗的普通招募不渲染该按钮，竖屏倒计时行继续在弹窗内容宽度内收束。

## Mailbox UI And Mobile

- The home mailbox entry uses a `lucide-react` mail icon in the existing top-right action cluster on desktop and the compact mobile menu on phones. The badge is the unread plus claimable count from the summary API.
- `src/styles/modals/information-center.css` defines the shared desktop list/reader grid for announcement and mailbox (`288–312px` master column plus a flexible reader) and the common `.information-center-prose` measure, heading, list, divider, link and focus treatment. Both domains share dashed header clearance, a 14px pane gap, 2px master/reader line frames, transparent detail articles, separator-led lists and low-shadow neutral selection; `src/styles/modals/mailbox.css` additionally owns mailbox completion states and the compact real-attachment footer shelf, while announcement CSS retains kind tabs and announcement-only pinned/unread semantics. The player header has no subtitle; mailbox detail starts with title, then one sender/time metadata row. Text-only mail omits the former “纯文本邮件” placeholder, while real attachments use a catalog image or semantic icon followed by localized name, quantity, claim state and the adjacent claim action. Neither detail article has an image, fill, nested border, runtime blur, or hard shadow. Desktop opens the newest message after loading; mobile remains list-first and no longer renders a horizontal card strip above the reader.
- Desktop announcement, changelog, and mailbox information centers open the newest first row after loading; an unread default row follows the same read mutation path as a clicked row. Phones remain list-first, keep their existing domain empty copy, and mutate read state only after an explicit row selection.
- The mailbox modal is included in the shared terminal and Bright School modal shell layers so desktop renders as a centered sheet instead of a full-width strip, while mobile keeps a top sheet with safe-area padding.
- `src/styles/admin/mailbox.css` defines the admin send form and recent send history as a two-column admin workspace on desktop and a single-column flow below 900px.
- The mailbox styles are imported through the existing import-only domain entrypoints: `src/styles/modals.css`, `src/styles/admin.css`, and `src/styles/home-terminal/top-strip.css`.

## Announcement UI And Mobile

- The home announcement entry uses a `lucide-react` newspaper icon in the same top-right action cluster as mailbox/settings on desktop and in the compact mobile menu on phones. A red dot indicates any unread announcement or changelog; tab-level and row-level dots inside the modal use the same semantic unread cue.
- Bright School home image entries and image-only utility buttons keep their WebP artwork shadows outside the layout box. Mobile and desktop owner CSS must leave `.home-image-entry`, `.match-image-entry`, `.house-manual-entry`, `.home-utility-grid`, and `.utility-image-entry` overflow-visible, while `.utility-entry-art` keeps right/bottom transparent bleed so `drop-shadow` is not clipped by the image box or final mobile guard.
- `src/styles/modals/announcement.css` remains an import-only player announcement entry delegating to `src/styles/modals/announcement/`. It owns compact segmented tabs, semantic list rows, side-marker unread state, empty/error states and the formal-publication detail surface; the top bar now reads only “公告”. Detail hierarchy uses a quiet category line, fixed-size article title, publisher timestamps and a solid divider. `MarkdownLiteContent` safely maps `##`/`###` to in-article headings, numeric and bullet lists to semantic lists, `>` blocks to restrained key-point callouts, `---`/`***` to dividers, and keeps bold/http(s) links without accepting raw HTML. Shared information-center CSS owns the `68ch` prose measure plus outer sheet and responsive navigation. Bright School repairs remain explicit in `final-announcement-controls.css`; `src/styles/mobile-adaptive/information-center.css` preserves safe-area sizing, centered 44px close/back controls, zero horizontal overflow, and the synchronized 260ms list-to-detail transition. The mobile header reserves the back-control column in both states so navigation never shifts the title or close control; reduced-motion disables the transition and detail entrance animation.
- `src/styles/admin/announcements.css` defines the admin management page as list + editor workspace on desktop and a single-column mobile flow. Desktop shows edit and Markdown-lite preview side by side; mobile uses an edit/preview segmented switch. Soft-delete uses a normal confirmation dialog that shows the entry title and warns there is no restore entry.
- Announcement styles are imported through the existing import-only domain entrypoints: `src/styles/modals.css`, `src/styles/admin.css`, and `src/styles/home-terminal/top-strip.css`.

## Story Player UI And Mobile

- Mobile tutorial NPC dialogue keeps its desktop overlay role but uses a translucent surface at `max-width: 900px`: the bubble softens its background, border, and shadow while portraits and text remain fully opaque. Reply choices no longer occupy the board overlay; they reuse the mobile room's existing “操作” dock with translucent button surfaces and opaque labels. Neither surface uses `backdrop-filter`, so stones, grid lines, and target rings remain visually available.
- Tutorial story and battle portraits referenced by the available script nodes are preloaded. When a new NPC battle node starts, `TutorialBattleScreen` atomically replaces the active bubble's speaker, resolved portrait URL, palette, and text instead of waiting for the previous portrait's exit animation; exit animation remains available when a bubble is actually hidden. This keeps the visible portrait synchronized with the line whose typewriter is running.

- `src/modals/StoryPlayerModal.jsx` is the reusable player story surface for onboarding and item-character interaction scenes. `src/styles/modals/onboarding-story.css` remains the import-only player story modal entry split into `onboarding-story/` shell, portrait/text, actions/skip-confirm, and mobile files. It defines the modal as a Bright School vertical theater sheet: a centered paper portrait area at the top with plain centered speaker-name text, no separate title/subtitle header, the largest area reserved for dialogue text, and bottom controls for next, finish, or branch options. Desktop defaults to a 4:5:1 grid ratio for portrait, text, and controls, with a parent-window padding gutter so the portrait, dialogue, and option controls do not touch the theater frame. The right-top pure-icon fast-forward button owns the skip-confirmation entry and replaces the ordinary close button. The skip confirmation is an absolute child overlay of the story modal, centered inside the parent window with width and height capped to that parent instead of inheriting generic full-screen nested-modal rules. The default text area owns its own scroll behavior so long lines do not resize the stage.
- Story portrait options are not limited to playable/admin character rows. `src/shared/storyPortraits.js` merges the normal character catalog with story-only portraits before admin node selection and player-side rendering; story-only ids such as `denia-rainbow-glow` may appear in node `characterId` and render in `StoryPlayerModal`, while item trigger target selects stay restricted to real characters.
- The typewriter state is visible through the dialogue text region and a small caret treatment. While typing, clicking anywhere inside the story panel fills the current node immediately without advancing it; clicking the outer backdrop remains the separate skip-confirmation action. Branch option buttons normally remain hidden until the current node text is fully visible and use a light-green default surface, switching to the existing pink confirmation color only while pressed. Individual options may set `revealDelaySeconds`; those options appear after their own non-negative delay from node entry, and all remaining options appear when the text completes. Story options may also set `transitionDelaySeconds`; after click, the options hide, the footer shows "继续中...", and the modal waits before entering the selected target or closing. Admin preview may skip that wait with "立即继续", while player playback cannot skip individual waits. Node handoffs reset the typewriter before the browser paints and keep the backdrop/modal shell stable, including the unified tutorial path where `TutorialSessionModal` passes only the current story node as a temporary playback window. Portraits referenced by the available script nodes are preloaded; the visible image is keyed by character and portrait URL instead of node id so adjacent lines by the same character keep one stable image instance while a speaker change swaps to the new preloaded asset.
- Story nodes may set `effect=long-text-compress-portrait`. That mode keeps the default story text region's initial size, then lets the text row grow with typed content, squeeze the portrait region down to zero if needed, and only enable text-region scrolling after the text reaches the modal's viewport-bounded height. The effect also runs the typewriter at 1.5x the default speed. Bright School's portrait mobile modal shell must keep a matching effect-specific `grid-template-rows` override after its default onboarding story modal grid so the themed phone layout does not revert to non-compressing rows.
- Story options with an empty `nextNodeId` are intentional close-window actions. Admin publish validation allows those empty targets while still requiring option labels and rejecting non-empty targets that do not exist in the current script. Player rendering continues to route those empty targets through `StoryPlayerModal` close behavior.
- Unified tutorial playback is coordinated by `src/tutorial/TutorialSessionModal.jsx` and `src/tutorial/TutorialBattleScreen.jsx`. Story nodes are still delegated to `StoryPlayerModal`; when playback reaches a `board-setup` node, the app enters the independent `tutorial-battle` view, while the admin editor opens the same experience in a full-screen preview layer. `TutorialBattleScreen` reuses the real `RoomBattleStage` layout with both player panels, the shared board, action bar, skill banner, and local room snapshot; it chooses `DesktopRoomLayout` or `MobileRoomLayout` through `useMobileRoomLayout()` so mobile tutorials call the same mobile battle shell as normal rooms. The local runtime in `src/tutorial/tutorialGameState.js` creates or switches the board from script `initialBoard`/node `boardSetup`, hard-gates scripted player points, applies NPC moves and resign through shared game rules, and handles skill tutorial nodes as a staged flow: click skill, select target or confirm, show `SkillBanner` plus `game.pendingSkill` board presentation, then reveal the resolved state before advancing. In-battle `npc-dialogue` nodes show NPC dialogue in a top-left overlay without a move; `player-choice` nodes place reply options in the existing operation area—below the board in the desktop action bar and inside the portrait mobile “操作” dock—so choices never cover or dim the board. NPC move/skill/dialogue bubbles slide in/out with reduced-motion support, remain visible while reply options are shown, use the active NPC palette as a restrained surface accent, type body text progressively while the speaker name stays immediate, and are replaced when the next NPC node starts. Battle node progression is authored as a single advance mode while still storing the legacy `manualContinueEnabled`/`autoContinueEnabled` booleans for script compatibility.
