New admin nodes default to automatic progression: blank `autoContinueDelaySeconds` means 1.5 seconds after NPC dialogue typewriter completion and 0 seconds for other battle nodes; manual mode writes the inverse booleans and shows a post-node "继续" button without starting a timer. Option clicks use `transitionDelaySeconds` as the wait before entering the option target. A multi-option answer that routes directly back to its question, or through one NPC feedback node whose `nextNodeId` returns to that question, is treated as wrong and reuses the UI-unavailable SFX; its advancing sibling is treated as correct and reuses the hidden-hand-reveal SFX. Ordinary multi-branch dialogue and single fixed replies stay silent, while wrong-answer NPC feedback uses manual continuation before retrying. Waiting keeps the current desktop or mobile battle surface mounted, disables duplicate action/choice input, and only admin preview exposes "立即继续" for timer waits. The action bar never renders free-text hints: it shows only concrete teaching buttons or reply choices, and the ordinary `OperationHint` is hidden while choices are pending so the decision has one clear interaction owner. Node initialization is keyed by current node id, so resolving `pendingWait` cannot re-run the same node setup and hide newly revealed reply options. Choice groups focus their first enabled button when mounted and reject repeated submission; one option stretches to the available width, desktop choices use responsive columns, and portrait mobile choices stack vertically. Choice buttons use content-driven height so every wrapped line remains visible. Desktop may keep a bounded scrollbar on the action group; portrait mobile keeps the choice group overflow-visible and uses the existing `.mobile-tab-panel` as the sole vertical scroll owner, preserving bottom padding so the final button is fully visible at maximum scroll. Option and teaching action buttons default to light green and switch to the pink confirmation surface only on active press. Bright School reply option hover/focus is isolated in `src/styles/themes/bright-school/room/tutorial-choice-interactions.css` and mirrors the home utility-card lift, hard shadow, subtle saturation/brightness, and small tilt without applying that card motion to teaching action buttons. The room header hides message/settings/coordinate/move utility controls and keeps only exit/skip; member/spectator panels are omitted. The action bar is fully owned by the active teaching node: free battle buttons are hidden, continue/skill/counting/resign targets use a light-green button surface plus target emphasis, player move targets render a real child `.tutorial-target-ring` inside the board point, no-target skills still require a second-stage board click, wrong player clicks show guidance toasts, and NPC/system nodes ignore player clicks without toast. The target ring must keep the board point centering transform even under Bright School broad button-child resets; desktop uses a larger double-ring halo with a dark contrast outline and stronger gold glow, while mobile caps the ring back down so it does not cover too much of the board. Mobile move/press feedback must keep stones on intersections, ordinary mobile teaching action labels stay centered, reply choice labels remain left-aligned for scanning, and timer panels reuse the normal `TimeBar` layout without tutorial-specific centering overrides. The chat panel is read-only, uses compact message labels without hand/time/role suffixes, and records NPC/user dialogue for review. Entering or switching board setup reuses a full-screen `AssetPreloadScreen` for the 3-second "正在激烈对局中..." loading page, while exiting/skipping/returning from battle reuses the same full-screen template with "正在收拾棋盘...", disables the local teaching battle BGM before the transition, pauses global BGM during the transition, and then finishes or resumes ordinary story playback without restarting the old battle BGM. Each `loading.id` owns a newly mounted timed-progress component, so a new transition paints 0% immediately instead of reusing the previous transition's completed 100% before its effect resets state. Story-to-battle handoff renders the same full-screen preload surface inside `TutorialSessionModal` before route state changes, and the `tutorial-battle` Suspense fallback uses the same fixed-copy preload instead of the generic route loading page. Story-to-battle, battle-to-story, and board-setup handoffs arm the loading state before the next frame is painted, so users do not see a one-frame battle shell, setup placeholder, ordinary loading page, home screen flash, or completed-progress flash between story surfaces. If the board setup chooses no player character, the player side keeps the same panel size on desktop and mobile with empty portrait, role/tag, and skill placeholders while hiding rank/content; desktop battle tutorials size that empty portrait frame to the same outer height as real character portrait frames, and both desktop and mobile empty portrait frames use the player's black/white side background so they remain visually symmetric with the NPC side.
- Tutorial move nodes may suppress the board target ring with `targetHighlightEnabled: false`. A wrong click first checks `wrongMovePointId`/`wrongMoveNextNodeId`; matching branches optionally apply the player's move when `applyWrongMove` is true, then continue through the configured demonstration branch, while non-matching clicks keep the node active with its toast. `boardSetupLoadingEnabled: false` applies a reset in the current battle frame without the preload screen, which is reserved for authored silent retry restoration; ordinary setup transitions keep the shared three-second preload. `boardSetup.lastMovePointId` is rendered through the normal current-move marker until the first real move or skill clears it, but is not inserted into `game.history`, move counts, ko, or scoring. These behaviors share the same desktop and portrait-mobile board component.
- `src/styles/modals/tutorial-session.css` owns the tutorial battle modal layout. Desktop uses a bounded two-column battle surface with board stage plus action panel; mobile collapses to NPC bubble, board, then action area with viewport and safe-area caps. The board stage sets stable square dimensions so the shared `.board-wrap` does not inherit room-only mobile sizing, and short landscape has its own size cap.
- Mobile uses the same story order as desktop but lets the sheet fill most of the viewport with safe-area padding and touch-sized controls. The Bright School mobile modal shell explicitly preserves the onboarding modal's grid layout instead of forcing the shared flex-column modal fallback, keeps a parent-window padding gutter, and the portrait area keeps symmetric padding so the portrait and name stay horizontally centered. Mobile branch buttons stack vertically at equal width; when there are many options the action area can grow and squeeze the text region, with its own internal scroll cap preventing page overflow.
- The admin story workbench uses the `.admin-story-workbench-*` namespace for the bright desktop editing surface. Its desktop layout keeps the script library as a top horizontal selection strip, gives the automatic flow graph a full-width row, and places issues, the current-step editor, and preview summary in a lower support row so complex branch swimlanes are not squeezed by permanent side columns. The preview summary is intentionally only a full-screen launch surface; complete teaching battles are not embedded in the lower support card. Full-screen preview uses `.admin-story-workbench-fullscreen-preview` and then either `TutorialSessionModal` for pre-battle story or `TutorialBattleScreen` after `board-setup`, so battle tutorial nodes are inspected with the same full room layout users will see. The same admin surface now edits tutorial metadata: script-level initial board mode/stones, node `type`, `prompt`, `color`, `pointId`, `skillId`, and `board-setup` scene fields for player color, optional player character, NPC character/name, and entry copy. Initial stones also have a `可视化摆棋` dialog: it reuses `src/room/Board.jsx` with a temporary shared game state, passes `stoneJitter={false}`, keeps black/white/erase tools outside the board, preserves the coordinate textarea as the serialized source, and only writes back when the admin clicks `保存摆棋`; the dialog keeps its own scroll area so 13-line and 19-line boards use the same intersections, star points, stones, and click hit areas as the real battle board on desktop and mobile.
- The desktop automatic-flow canvas is a viewport-bounded scroll owner (`height: clamp(420px, 62dvh, 760px)`) so a long vertical node graph no longer pushes its native horizontal scrollbar below every node. The canvas remains one two-axis scroll surface, is keyboard-focusable with a visible focus ring, reserves stable scrollbar space, and contains desktop scroll chaining. At `980px` and below it returns to natural document height and normal scroll chaining so narrow layouts do not add a competing vertical gesture region.
- Player story layering is intentionally higher than warehouse and other app-level business modals on both desktop and mobile; skip/finish/close actions therefore target the story layer first, clear every story overlay state, then reveal the underlying modal state after the story closes. Backdrop clicks, the top-right close affordance, and the fast-forward/skip affordance open the same skip/close confirmation layer first, and only the confirmation action closes the story.
- Bright School modal shell safeguards include `.onboarding-story-modal`, keeping close buttons, touch targets, and modal sizing aligned with the existing player modal vocabulary.

## Mailbox UI And Mobile

- The home mailbox entry uses a mail icon in the existing top-right action cluster on desktop and in the compact mobile menu on phones. The badge combines unread mail and claimable attachment counts from the summary API, and the mobile menu renders it as an absolute corner badge so the number never wraps or changes button layout.
- Player mailbox UI uses a two-column list/detail layout on desktop. Portrait mobile stays list-first and replaces the list with the selected detail only after an explicit row click; the fixed back control returns to the same list.
- Empty player inboxes keep the same information-center layout as populated inboxes: the list region renders a compact empty state and the desktop detail pane stays reserved.
- Mail list rows keep unread/claimable/completed scan cues. The detail pane uses a crop-safe low-contrast natural-paper texture with no authored rules or runtime blur overlay; title, sender/time metadata and delete action stay fixed while the shared prose region scrolls, and only real attachments create the compact footer shelf.
- Mailbox mobile safety lives in `src/styles/mobile-adaptive/bright-school-portrait/mailbox-modal.css`, imported through the final Bright School portrait guard entry after chat fixes and before character detail fixes.

## 玩家窗口空状态

`WindowEmptyState` 保留调用方原有文案与语义容器，以 `aria-hidden` 的手绘纸页替代重复的粗虚线卡片。普通校园主题使用透明无边框容器、深墨色常规霞鹜文字与静态纸页；其他主题隐藏插画，保留文字。`window-empty-states.css` 在书签样式后、黑化样式前加载，显式覆盖旧空状态容器、伪元素和 SVG 的强规则。

普通空状态最小高度 154px，紧凑版为 88px；后者用于履历战绩、装饰区及公告/邮件列表。覆盖观战、好友、排行榜、成就、仓库、公告、邮件、回放、履历战绩、装饰与服装商店。加载提示与错误状态不复用空结果插画；观战只有存在房间时渲染表格，空结果不留空表头，也不会在加载失败时同时显示暂无房间。接入书签的观战窗口移除旧选项卡网格轨道，使正文/空状态在剩余内容区布局。

### 棋盘比例与队际赛头像
竖屏 `board-stage` 宽度仍取 `--mobile-room-board-size`，`max-width:100%` 随父容器收缩；舞台与 `board-wrap` 均使用 `height:auto`、`aspect-ratio:1`，父槽及舞台显式收缩网格轨道，防止旧固定高度与不同断点宽度形成非等比棋盘。队际赛采用 B「错落纸片」：独立斜边纸片轻微错落，浅黄／浅绿／浅粉纸面配棕色细边和轻硬阴影，关闭单人拱形底板。三张直立图片按出场顺序各自裁切，不显示角色名。手机队际信息使用姓名段位、提子计时、技能三行布局，信息条高108px、头像高96px，宽度为clamp(88px,27.7vw,108px)，为三位角色保留可辨识空间；普通单人布局保持不变。隐藏角色只显示问号，退场角色灰显。

移动端普通对弈信息区采用紧凑 A 独立卡片，由 `battle-paper-player-mobile.css` 统一负责：头像独占左侧两行；右上身份与时钟按6:4分宽，用户名和段位同行；右下三枚统计卡与居中技能卡同排，统计标签与数值不换行。两行间隔6px，总高约94px。独立卡片使用细边与1–2px硬阴影，技能样式拆至 `battle-paper-skill-mobile.css`。身份卡随棋色显示黑底浅字或浅底深字；只有当前回合时钟卡变黄；超频卡固定粉色，技能卡以角色主题色渐变。无整体卡片背景、无错落旋转。倒计时读条继续读取 TimeBar 主时间／读秒进度，保留次数与警示。棋盘优先按视口宽度减24px取正方形，最大480px，不再因信息区高度而缩小；短屏允许纵向滚动。桌面和黑化剧情保持原布局。

移动双行卡片统一补足内部留白：身份6×7px、时钟6×9px、统计5px、技能5×7px。名牌背景用户名不再被棋色字色覆盖，沿用名牌配套颜色和文字阴影；仅普通用户名与段位随身份卡变色。`battle-paper-nameplate-mobile.css` 将名牌限制在用户名列内，消除嵌套默认内边距，避免名牌裁切与重复留白。

移动身份卡通过 BattleUserIdentity 测量可用空间与原始名牌尺寸，使用统一scale等比缩放完整用户名组件，不省略或换行。名牌保留原始宽高比；特别长的名字先在名牌文字安全区内调整字号。ResizeObserver及字体加载完成后重新计算，桌面不启用缩放。

移动身份卡显式使用单行网格并垂直居中；隐藏无角色人机／教程沿用的空积分占位，避免额外隐式行将用户名推离卡片中心。桌面占位规则保持不变。

移动倒计时轨道显式清除旧主题2px边框、外边距和内边距：轨道总高4px时，上下边框会耗尽内容高度，使填充进度不可见。保持轨道实际内容高4px，填充宽度继续由TimeBar主时间／读秒比例控制。

对弈信息区用户名暂不展示装备的名牌背景：BattleUserIdentity 调用 UserIdentity 时传入 showNameplate=false，其他场景不变。信息区统一使用仅覆盖0–9的 WuWa 字体别名，其余字符回退到既有霞鹜字体；包括用户名、段位、时钟、统计和技能，桌面与移动共用。保留移动端完整用户名单行缩放。

桌面普通对弈信息区将棋钟与统计分为上下两行：独立棋钟卡保留当前回合黄色和6px倒计时读条；下方三张统计卡横向排列，提子／除子／超频标签与数值在卡片内同一行显示，标签14px、数字18px，文字与数字间距8px，卡片最小高度36px，超频为浅粉背景。技能卡取消负上边距，避免遮挡统计。仅修改非mobile-room-screen的桌面样式。

桌面棋钟收紧主数字与读秒次数间距，取消主数字4.2ch最小宽度，读秒次数仅显示数字，与秒数保持18px间距。当前回合为黄色抬起卡片（6px实体底座），非当前回合下沉5px并保留1px底座与内阴影；状态切换不改变布局占位，统计卡仍保持标签和数值同行。

桌面棋钟采用左右镜像的1.5度纸片倾斜，统计卡轻微交错倾斜并使用同色硬阴影，延续身份卡和技能卡的错落风格。移动布局不变。

移动端对弈信息区时钟、读秒次数、提子／除子／超频数值、技能次数和段位使用400常规字重，保留WuWa数字字体，避免合成粗体。

---

﻿# 性能、技术债与扩展路线

本文记录技术债优先级、后续扩展建议、实时性能和高频交互优化。新增性能优化、技术债清理、扩展路线或架构风险时优先更新本分篇。

## 当前结论

- CSS 体积和全局层叠仍是前端维护风险。共享基础、房间、棋盘、弹窗、商业/社交/仓库、响应式、移动对局、HUD、最终移动安全、后台管理和 lobby 样式已经逐步拆成 import-only 入口加领域子目录，低频后台与剧情教学 CSS 由 lazy 路由加载；剧情教学 Excel 导入反馈继续放在既有 `story-workbench` 子入口内，不新增根入口或超过 6000-byte 单文件合同。Bright School 旧的广域兜底清理结构已合并为 `src/styles/themes/bright-school/surface-contracts.css` 和 `surface-contracts/` 显式合同层，根入口保留 `base.css`、`gallery-polish.css`、`surface-contracts.css`、`component-repairs.css`、`qa-guard.css`；后续技术债重点不再是保留旧兜底，而是减少剩余高风险 `!important`、继续拆分未领域化的大文件，并为棋盘/技能/最终移动层建立可用视觉回归基线。Tailwind 长期迁移采用 Phase 1-7 路线，完整执行计划保存在 `.trellis/tasks/07-01-tailwind-migration-roadmap/tailwind-full-migration-plan.md`：Phase 1 新增 `src/styles/tailwind/tokens.css` 语义 token scaffold 和静态合同，Phase 2 已从后台审计表外壳开始试点 `tw:` utility 并删除 `.audit-table-wrap` 旧 CSS；Phase 3 已从 `src/ui/primitives/ScrollArea.jsx`、`src/ui/primitives/Badge.jsx`、`src/ui/primitives/EmptyState.jsx` 和 `src/ui/primitives/Button.jsx` 开始，把低风险 overflow、inline badge layout、admin table empty-cell layout 与 admin action alignment utilities 收口到 primitive，且 `src/admin/adminComponents.jsx` 通过 `AdminTableScroll` 让所有后台表格壳复用 `ScrollArea`，并通过 `AdminActionButton` 让后台动作按钮继续复用既有 `.primary-action`、`.secondary-action`、`.danger-action` 视觉合同。Phase 4 已从 `src/modals/modalComponents.jsx` 的 `ModalActionButton` 开始，只把 `ConfirmModal`、`MessageBoardModal`、`AnnouncementModal` 简单 retry/load-more 动作按钮、`PersonalizationModal` 保存按钮、`MailboxModal` 附件领取按钮、`FriendsOverlays` 对局模式取消按钮和 `UserProfileCard` 举报提交按钮的 alignment utility 迁入 wrapper，继续保留共享 modal/action、mailbox 与 friends/profile CSS 对颜色、间距、边框、阴影、禁用态、位置、字体、列表/详情布局、纸张背景、匹配模式选项、资料确认面板、社交操作和移动表现的视觉所有权。由于 Tailwind 通过单独导入 `theme.css` 和 `utilities.css` 来禁用 preflight，`src/styles/tailwind.css` 的 utilities 导入必须保留 `source("../")`，否则 `src/` JSX 中的 `tw:` pilot 类不会生成实际 CSS。后续再按 UI primitives、共享 modal/list/card/form、首页/大厅/商业非对局流程、Bright School token 化、最终移动安全层收缩的顺序推进；棋盘几何、Pixi canvas、技能演出、玩家按钮重设样式和移动对局控制暂不进入 Phase 1-7，Phase 8+ 只有在视觉回归基线和稳定性门禁就绪后才允许启动。
- Tailwind Phase 5 已从极窄首页流程切片开始：`src/home/homeComponents.jsx` 的 `HomeActionButton` 只接管匹配模式弹窗取消动作的 alignment utility，继续复用 `.secondary-action` 视觉合同；匹配模式选项、首页 artboard、utility entries、player plaque、商业卡片、移动安全层和所有对局控制仍保持 CSS-owned，直到对应完整表面有桌面/移动测试与视觉检查。
- 黑化决战的对局台词和假技能由 `mobile-adaptive/sigrika-corruption/room-presentation.css` 单独持有，使既有 `room-replay.css` 继续低于 6000 字节 owner 上限。倒计时最终覆盖拆到 `room-timers.css`；移动端残余文字和浮层又分别拆为 3039-byte `room-mobile-readability.css` 与 3235-byte `room-system-log.css`，后者通过 `.sigrika-duel-system-log` 跟随 Portal 到 room root 外，避免普通 Bright School 表单/纸张规则重新胜出。新增 55 个定向 `!important` 只用于已存在的子文字、表单和选中态赢家。该轮 CSS 基线为 672 个文件、1427819 个规范化字节、8352 个 `!important`、334 个含 `!important` 文件、260 个 media 文件；hardcoded hex 仍为 2469，reduced-motion 文件仍为 48，高 z-index 文件仍为 7，两个新 owner 均无媒体查询、硬编码颜色或数值型高 z-index。
- Tailwind Phase 6/7 已从合同型切片开始而不是实际迁移视觉规则：Phase 6 把 Bright School 的纸张、干净表面、墨色、边框、强调色和纸张阴影变量映射进 `src/styles/tailwind/tokens.css`，并由 `themeContract.test.js` 校验 Bright School token 与 Tailwind token 映射；Phase 7 只在 `src/styles/cssLayerInventory.js` 登记 `mobile-adaptive.css` 最终安全层收缩候选。当前不会删除或移动 `mobile-adaptive.css` 规则，直到对应组件/primitive 已同时拥有桌面和移动行为，并通过手机竖屏、小屏横屏、窄桌面和常规桌面检查。
- `src/main.jsx` 已瘦身为浏览器挂载入口，应用组合根迁移到 `src/app/App.jsx`，应用级弹窗可见性已收口到 `src/app/useOverlayState.js`，房间/回放/结果弹窗会话状态已收口到 `src/app/useRoomSessionState.js`，匹配等待/成功过渡状态已收口到 `src/app/useMatchSessionState.js`，直连约战 incoming banner 状态已收口到 `src/app/useIncomingDuelState.js`；App 级音频运行态已收口到 `src/app/useAudioRuntimeState.js`，负责本地音频设置、持久化和 socket 重连后的背景音恢复信号；下一步可继续把大厅辅助和启动状态编排拆成更聚焦的状态/动作 hook。服务端启动数据与 schema 初始化已收口到 `server/serverStartup.js`，并由 `SERVER_STARTUP_TASK_ORDER` 明确可审计顺序；Socket.IO 连接事件套件已收口到 `server/socketEvents.js`，速率保护与匹配、房间连接/恢复、对局/数子/求和/计分、聊天、约战、断线清理继续由各自 `server/socket*Events.js` 分组模块维护，生产静态资源托管已收口到 `server/staticAssets.js`，账号资产 legacy 字段解析、结构化同步和公开合并规则已收口到 `server/userAssets.js`；后续重点转为剩余数据模型兼容镜像字段、前端状态编排和 CSS 技术债。运行期图片和关键交互音频资源清单已先收口到 `src/shared/assetRegistry.js`，棋盘技能特效图片 URL 进一步收口到 `src/room/boardSkillEffectAssets.js`，后续可继续扩展到角色、后台资源目录和更完整的音频分类。
- Initial bundle debt is being reduced from low-risk surfaces first. Admin console, tutorial-battle route code, and the battle asset preload route now load through `React.lazy`; their route-only CSS or view code split out of the initial bundle where applicable. Low-frequency app overlays are lazy-loaded from `AppOverlays`; room gameplay, match/result lifecycle modals, toast, duel banner, and story playback stay synchronous until they have focused browser regression coverage. The room entry path now has an opportunistic playable-ready prewarm layer: home idle scheduling, desktop match-entry pointer/focus intent, mode-option pointer/focus intent, and match-start mode selection all call the same deduped helper so the battle preload view chunk and Pixi cache are warmed before the battle preload gate whenever the browser has time.
- Desktop home interaction uses two explicit hot-path boundaries without changing the rendered design. `AppRoutes` delegates the home surface to memoized `HomeRoute`, so overlay- or room-only app state changes do not rebuild `HomeScreen` when its consumed props are stable. The two large home entries render an animated `.home-entry-motion` wrapper around the filtered raster, and the six image utility entries render an animated `.utility-entry-motion` wrapper around `.utility-entry-art`; transforms and `will-change` belong to the wrapper, while `drop-shadow`, brightness and saturation remain on the image. Existing widths, offsets, tilts, shadows, active compression, reduced-motion and mobile feedback values remain unchanged. Future image replacement therefore continues through the image URL and transparent canvas/aspect ratio rather than baking shadows or motion into the asset.
- Character-detail modal hover hot paths keep the original button feedback and remove the full-viewport Chrome raster bottleneck. Real Chrome measurement showed the list deploy button and detail music button both enter hover and change computed style within about 4-6ms, so speculative button-level prepaint, paint-containment, and extra `will-change` patches were removed. `.nested-modal-backdrop` and its pointer-transparent `::before` paint layer both hold `backdrop-filter: none`; the paint layer uses a theme-owned static dark translucent fill instead of live background sampling so the detail surface stays visually prominent without restoring Chrome's raster cost. Button motion, keyboard focus, modal stacking, and reduced-motion behavior remain unchanged.
- Story guidance applies the same Chrome raster rule to both runtime phases. `.onboarding-story-backdrop` and `.tutorial-session-backdrop` use `rgba(35, 27, 31, 0.64)` static fills and keep both standard and WebKit `backdrop-filter` at `none`; the Bright School root contract repeats the deep-plum color at the theme override layer. Typewriter updates, branch-button hover/focus, and local tutorial-board changes therefore no longer invalidate a live full-viewport blur while story layout, stacking, skip confirmation, and mobile safe-area behavior remain unchanged.
- 实时性能已经通过轻量时钟、房间广播边界、结构共享和棋盘 memo 得到改善。
- Shared replay/mode/resume modal styling now follows the same import-only sub-entry pattern. `src/styles/modals/replay-mode-resume.css` delegates replay list/table, resume header actions, match mode/tabs, resume layout, achievement modal, personalization preview/grid, personalization picker, and embedded resume character records to `src/styles/modals/replay-mode-resume/`; modal and style contract tests read the expanded import tree.
- Shared board styling now follows the same import-only sub-entry pattern. `src/styles/room/board.css` delegates frame/coordinates, row-slash DOM effects, ambient fog, effect canvas/reduced-motion, point previews, stones/skill effects, latest-move/touch/void markers, and grid/scoring marks to `src/styles/room/board/`; board and style contract tests read the expanded import tree.
- Shared room player/timer/skill styling now follows the same sub-entry pattern. `src/styles/room/players-timers-skills.css` delegates side layout, player cards, capture tooltips, timers, skill chips, mobile tap tooltips, and color badges to `src/styles/room/players-timers-skills/`; style contract tests read the expanded import tree.
- Bright School narrow-desktop home-stage safety now follows the same sub-entry pattern. `src/styles/mobile-adaptive/home-narrow-desktop.css` delegates common region resets, wide-stage, compact-stage, micro-stage scroll, and short-height stack rules to `src/styles/mobile-adaptive/home-narrow-desktop/`; style contract tests read the expanded import tree.
- Bright School desktop student ID card styling now follows the same sub-entry pattern. `src/styles/themes/bright-school/home/student-id-card.css` delegates card shell/avatar, identity name, user identity tag, and mode stats rules to `src/styles/themes/bright-school/home/student-id-card/`; theme contract tests read the expanded import tree.
- Bright School final mobile profile/house records now follow the same sub-entry pattern. `src/styles/mobile-adaptive/bright-school-overrides/profile-house-records.css` delegates house/profile stat fixes, profile resume stats, character record dialog rules, and embedded resume character records to `src/styles/mobile-adaptive/bright-school-overrides/profile-house-records/`; style contract tests read the expanded import tree.
- CSS debt cleanup continues by moving the shared Startorch home terminal skin to an import-only entry. `src/styles/home-terminal.css` now delegates shell/background, top-strip, layout/player, image entries, utility/footer/motion, and mobile rules to `src/styles/home-terminal/`; `src/styles/styleContract.test.js` keeps the import order and approved nested directory list current. Remaining high-risk CSS targets now include other large style files that are not yet domainized.
- Bright School gallery polish now follows the same import-only pattern. `src/styles/themes/bright-school/gallery-polish.css` delegates theme tokens, home image entries/art, paper surfaces, chat paper grid, interaction states, and addendum notes to `src/styles/themes/bright-school/gallery-polish/`; theme tests read the expanded import tree.
- Bright School room readability now follows the same import-only pattern. `src/styles/themes/bright-school/room.css` delegates header/exit controls, player status, floating skill panels, player name controls, side tags, board coordinates, and flat control cleanup to `src/styles/themes/bright-school/room/`; room and theme tests read the expanded import tree.
- Bright School surface contracts now follow the same import-only pattern. `src/styles/themes/bright-school/surface-contracts.css` delegates explicit root/surface/control/form contracts, known HUD reset, home utility controls, handbook/profile cleanup, character-detail cleanup, commerce/social cleanup, room action cleanup, final explicit surfaces, announcement controls, and typography to `src/styles/themes/bright-school/surface-contracts/`; theme tests read the expanded import tree and assert old broad fallback strings stay absent.
- Bright School effects now follow the same import-only pattern. `src/styles/themes/bright-school/effects.css` delegates selected-control press depth, skill-action active/disabled states, board targeting, board marks, keyframes, and reduced-motion guards to `src/styles/themes/bright-school/effects/`; theme tests read the expanded import tree.
- Bright School modal cleanup now follows the same import-only pattern. `src/styles/themes/bright-school/modals.css` delegates handbook/decoration cleanup, surface cleanup, settings/lobby fixes, selected action states, resume/personalization layout, result-room popovers, and stage/decoration repair rules to `src/styles/themes/bright-school/modals/`; theme tests read the expanded import tree.
- Bright School portrait mobile home shell styling now follows the same sub-entry pattern: `src/styles/themes/bright-school/mobile/home-shell.css` owns import order and delegates shell base, top strip/menu, main stage, player plaque, and entries/utility/footer rules to `src/styles/themes/bright-school/mobile/home-shell/`, with theme contract tests guarding the nested import order.
- Bright School commerce shop polish now follows the same sub-entry pattern: `src/styles/themes/bright-school/commerce/shop.css` owns import order and delegates sidebar/wallet, product-grid, detail-dialog, and responsive rules to `src/styles/themes/bright-school/commerce/shop/`, with theme contract tests guarding the nested import order.
- Bright School commerce warehouse/profile polish now follows the same sub-entry pattern: `src/styles/themes/bright-school/commerce/warehouse-profile.css` owns import order and delegates typography/numeric text, profile stats, warehouse header, empty state, item cards, and mobile item cards to `src/styles/themes/bright-school/commerce/warehouse-profile/`; theme contract tests guard the nested import order.
- Startorch HUD hardening now follows the same sub-entry pattern: `src/styles/hud-components/hud-hardening.css` owns import order and delegates tokens/shell/scrollbars, inputs/settings/auth contrast, inventory tags, home hologram entries, character deploy/detail styling, shop pagination/owned badges, warehouse surfaces, and friend action rows to `src/styles/hud-components/hud-hardening/`; style contract tests guard the nested import order.
- Shared mobile battle shell/dock styling now follows the same sub-entry pattern: `src/styles/mobile-room/base-shell-dock.css` owns import order and delegates shell/header/menu, flat control reset, viewport/dock shell, player/timer strip, board viewport, dock tabs/actions, and decision/chat panels to `src/styles/mobile-room/base-shell-dock/`; style contract tests guard the nested import order.
- Final portrait mobile battle-room safety now follows the same sub-entry pattern: `src/styles/mobile-adaptive/mobile-room-portrait.css` owns import order and delegates shell/header/menu, neutral chrome reset, viewport shell, player strips, board viewport, dock panels, and action/decision controls to `src/styles/mobile-adaptive/mobile-room-portrait/`; style contract tests guard the nested import order.
- Startorch terminal modal system styling now follows the same sub-entry pattern: `src/styles/modals/terminal-system.css` owns import order and delegates terminal tokens/backdrop, modal chrome, close/header actions, terminal buttons, replay/profile surfaces, result modal layout, and outcome/resume actions to `src/styles/modals/terminal-system/`; style contract tests guard the nested import order.
- Shared gacha modal styling now follows the same sub-entry pattern: `src/styles/commerce/gacha.css` owns import order and delegates modal tabs, featured stack, machine stage, featured prize, control panel, list/result dialogs, and animations to `src/styles/commerce/gacha/`; style contract and gacha modal tests read the expanded import tree.
- Shared social/profile/leaderboard styling now follows the same sub-entry pattern: `src/styles/commerce/social-profile.css` owns import order and delegates modal shells, friends toolbar/search, friends list/status, friend actions/notices, duel request banner, leaderboard table, and owned decoration section rules to `src/styles/commerce/social-profile/`; style contract and social/leaderboard modal tests read the expanded import tree.
- General CSS architecture guard coverage now prevents regression while the remaining CSS debt is worked down: `src/styles/styleContract.test.js` rejects mixed import/concrete CSS entries and rejects new or expanded 6000-byte CSS files outside the recorded known-debt baseline.
- Bright School portrait mobile house/profile styling now follows the same sub-entry pattern: `src/styles/themes/bright-school/mobile/house-profile.css` owns import order and delegates shell/profile stats, character grid/cards, owned decorations, and character detail/music controls to `src/styles/themes/bright-school/mobile/house-profile/`; theme contract tests guard the nested import order.
- Bright School portrait mobile commerce and warehouse layout now follows the same sub-entry pattern: `src/styles/themes/bright-school/mobile/commerce-warehouse.css` owns import order and delegates shop layout, warehouse shell/grid, and warehouse item card rules to `src/styles/themes/bright-school/mobile/commerce-warehouse/`; the split also removes a duplicated shop mobile block while preserving the final warehouse grid declarations.
- Bright School portrait mobile list/settings/social layout now follows the same sub-entry pattern: `src/styles/themes/bright-school/mobile/lists-settings.css` owns import order and delegates list scroll widths, watch rows, friends rows, table shells, leaderboard cards, replay cards, and toolbar/card-border rules to `src/styles/themes/bright-school/mobile/lists-settings/`; theme contract tests guard the nested import order.
- The shared lobby, house manual, profile stat, match entry, and watch-list layer now follows the import-only domain pattern. `src/styles/lobby.css` delegates panel/profile, character card, match/watch entry, watch-list shell, and watch-list phone fallback rules to `src/styles/lobby/`, while feature tests read the expanded CSS import tree.
- The shared mobile home layout followed the same pattern: `src/styles/mobile-home.css` now owns import order for the 900px base portrait/tablet layout, the 620px narrow-phone fallback, and the 900px landscape fallback through `src/styles/mobile-home/`. This keeps mobile lobby responsive rules extensible without adding more concrete CSS to the root entry layer.
- Desktop home toolbox rules follow the same split pattern: shared base rules live in `src/styles/base/home-stage-toolbox.css`, while Bright School-specific desktop paper-card polish is an import-only `src/styles/themes/bright-school/home/utility-toolbox.css` sub-entry delegating to toolbox grid and interaction files.

## Realtime Performance Notes

- Room clock ticks no longer use the full `room:update` payload during normal play. `server/rooms.js` emits a lightweight `room:clock` event once per second with `roomCode`, `activeColor`, `serverNow`, and each player's current `time`.
- Player chat and room presence now use general room patch paths: `server/socketChatEvents.js` broadcasts `room:patch` with `type: "chat:append"`, while `server/socketRoomEvents.js` and `server/socketDisconnectEvents.js` use `broadcastRoomPresencePatch()` for disconnect, reconnect, spectator membership, and leave changes. `server/roomBroadcasts.js` increments room `revision`, emits `eventId`, `baseRevision`, and `revision`; full room updates and default patches still force persistence, but chat and presence patches explicitly opt into throttled persistence because connection lifecycle mutations already force-save the authoritative room before the lightweight presence broadcast. `src/app/roomPatch.js` applies `chat:append` idempotently and applies `presence:update` by replacing player/spectator/chat slices while preserving `game` and structurally sharing unchanged entries. `src/app/socketHandlers.js` filters missing-room, wrong-room, unknown, stale, and malformed patches before scheduling React state work; if the client sees a patch gap, it rejects the patch and emits `room:resume` to recover the authoritative snapshot. Duplicate gapped patch payloads for the same room/revision are debounced so a bad network burst cannot spam resume requests, and authoritative `room:update` / `room:resume` snapshots clear the debounce state. Full `room:update` remains the authoritative path for the joining/resuming socket, match found, moves, skills, phase changes, scoring, draw/counting flows, timeout transitions, and patch recovery.
- Full `room:update` remains the authoritative synchronization path for match found, the socket that just joined/resumed, moves, skills, phase changes, scoring, draw/counting flows, timeout transitions, and any patch-gap recovery.
- `server/roomBroadcasts.js` is the single backend emit boundary for room snapshot updates, lightweight clock updates, lightweight patch updates, room toasts, and close events. It skips disconnected participants, owns patch revision metadata, defaults patch persistence to forced writes, allows chat/presence callers to opt into throttled persistence, and builds `presence:update` from the existing room view projection so player/spectator payload shape stays aligned with full snapshots.
- `server/roomRuntime.js` is the backend room runtime adapter boundary. It wires `server/roomStatePersistence.js` into `server/roomBroadcasts.js`, centralizing forced/throttled persistence callbacks, per-room serialized snapshot writes, room-specific flush consistency points, full-room broadcast persistence injection, and room toast forwarding outside the lifecycle composition module.
- `server/roomPresence.js` is the backend participant-state boundary for players plus spectators. Room broadcasting, watch-room summaries, finished-room close extension, and empty-active-room closure all use it for connected-participant and all-players-disconnected decisions.
- `server/roomMatchmakingQueue.js` is the backend matchmaking queue boundary for waiting-player state. Matchmaking joins, leaves, disconnect cleanup, per-mode counts, and custom `canPair` filters go through this module, while room creation and match-success delivery stay in `server/rooms.js`.
- `server/roomFactory.js` is the backend room creation boundary. Initial room shape, random room code generation, player color assignment, mode-specific room-user projection, opening phase, and initial clock fields live there.
- `server/roomSkillMessages.js` is the backend skill-message formatting boundary. Skill use notices, custom `systemMessage` template replacement, point labels, and stone labels live there so room lifecycle code does not own display-string rules.
- `server/roomTimers.js` is the backend timer bookkeeping boundary for room intervals and tracked timeouts. Room lifecycle code should schedule through this module so timeout ids are removed after firing and cleared consistently during room shutdown or empty-room cancellation.
- `server/roomSystemMessages.js` is the backend room chat-log mutation boundary for system messages. Generic system notices, notice-list appends, and restored disconnect notices live there so room lifecycle code can decide when notices occur without owning message object shape or reconnect-recovery text deduplication.
- `server/roomActionValidation.js` is the backend room action point-validation boundary. Room action handlers delegate point-target validation there, while the module forwards board coordinate errors from `server/security.js` unchanged.
- `server/roomCloseLifecycle.js` is the backend room close lifecycle boundary. Finished-room close scheduling, invalid-room short close delay, connected-participant close extension, `room:closed` payload construction, persisted-room deletion trigger, empty-active-room invalidation, and empty-room close cancellation live there behind injected persistence/timer/broadcast dependencies; the `rooms.js` composition waits for pending snapshot upserts for the same room before deleting persisted close-cleanup state.
- `server/roomDeadlineScheduler.js` is the backend room deadline scheduling boundary. Opening delay, initial passive skill delay, counting-request timeout, draw-request timeout, result-review timeout, and restored pending-deadline scheduling live there behind injected room lookup, timer, broadcast, and lifecycle callbacks.
- `server/roomResultPersistence.js` is the backend room result persistence boundary. Finished-room record creation, invalid-result record skipping, draw mode-stat increments, decisive reward application, user stat writes, progress ledger entries, and item-effect cleanup operations live there so room lifecycle code only triggers result saving.
- `server/roomSkillResolution.js` is the backend skill-preview lifecycle boundary. Active/passive skill preview setup, persisted `pendingSkillResolution` snapshots, restored preview delay scheduling, preview payload metadata, and resolution completion side effects live there so new skill effects do not add more timing and preview-state branching to `server/rooms.js`.
- `server/roomClockLifecycle.js` is the backend per-room clock tick boundary. Playing-phase tick handling, disconnected-player empty-room scheduling, timeout finish handling, and the choice between lightweight `room:clock` and full room broadcasts live there so future clock-throttling or patch-broadcast work has one server-side entry point.
- `server/roomRestoreLifecycle.js` is the backend restored-room timer resume boundary. Finished-room close windows, opening deadlines, restored pending skill previews, active room deadlines, and empty-room close scheduling are selected there so process-restart recovery has one auditable scheduling entry point.
- `server/roomPersistenceRestoreLifecycle.js` is the backend persisted-room restore orchestration boundary. Loading persisted rows, parsing/hydrating snapshots, adding restored disconnect notices, registering rooms, invoking restore-time timer decisions, force-persisting resumable rooms, and continuing past bad rows now live outside `server/rooms.js`.
- `server/roomOpeningLifecycle.js` is the backend opening transition boundary. Opening completion now centralizes the transition to `playing`, game-start notice, `lastTick` refresh, full room broadcast, and initial passive-skill scheduling handoff.
- `server/roomConnectionLifecycle.js` is the backend room connection-state boundary. Player reconnects, spectator joins/leaves, socket disconnect cleanup, empty-room close handoff, and forced room persistence after connection changes live there; it receives socket lookup/register/unregister callbacks from `server/roomMembershipIndex.js`, so `detachSocket()` handles only rooms currently indexed for that socket instead of scanning every room.
- `server/roomRequestLifecycle.js` is the backend counting/draw/scoring request entry boundary. Room-code validation, room/player lookup, phase precondition checks, scoring point validation, and delegation into `server/roomScoringFlow.js` live there so future request types can share one validation and dispatch shape.
- `server/roomCreationLifecycle.js` is the backend room creation orchestration boundary. Matched matchmaking joins and direct duel acceptance share creation notices, room registration, one initial forced persistence, clock/preload scheduling, and one viewer-specific `match:found` delivery per player. It no longer follows `match:found` with a duplicate full-room broadcast.
- `server/roomActionLifecycle.js` is the backend gameplay action entry boundary. Game actions now share room-code validation, room lookup, point validation, player lookup, pending-skill rejection, test-action dispatch, skill-action dispatch, and standard action delegation in one focused module.
- `server/roomActionPhaseGuards.js` is the backend gameplay phase matrix. It keeps standard board actions and active skills from mutating opening, preview, scoring, review, or finished rooms before action-specific reducers run, while explicitly documenting the limited non-playing phases where resign remains accepted.
- `server/roomChatLifecycle.js` is the backend room chat entry boundary. Chat sends now share room-code validation, text normalization, room lookup, message id creation, current move-number capture, chat payload shape, and `{ room, message }` mutation result in one focused module so socket code can emit append-only chat patches without rebuilding a viewer-specific room snapshot.
- `server/roomQueries.js` is the backend room read-model boundary. Active-room lists and watch-room summaries can delegate to an injected `roomReadModel`; the current single-process fallback still scans current rooms for projection. Active membership checks and user room lookup delegate to `server/roomMembershipIndex.js` when available. The index is populated by room creation and persisted-room restore, maintained during room connection/leave/disconnect, cleared by room close and tests, and prefers unfinished active rooms over finished review rooms for the same user so reconnect/resume lands on the playable match first; participant counts and player summaries continue to come from `server/roomPresence.js`.
- `server/roomView.js` builds only the needed board projection for player viewers. Spectators use `game` as the canonical black projection and receive only the white difference projection as `gameViews.white`; the frontend keeps black/white switching compatible without serializing the black view twice.
- `server/serverLifecycle.js` guards local development startup and production shutdown: occupied port 3001 gets an explicit `EADDRINUSE` message, while SIGINT/SIGTERM and managed child shutdown use the drain/Socket.IO/HTTP/persistence/Prisma sequence.
- The frontend applies full `room:update` snapshots, live `room:resume` snapshots, and pending match-success room snapshots through `src/app/roomSnapshot.js`. Same-room snapshots are structurally shared before entering React state, so duplicate updates, reconnect resumes, or match-success follow-up updates can return the current room object and unchanged `game.points`, player entries, chat messages, and nested view data keep their existing references. Duplicate pending match-success snapshots are consumed without scheduling a fresh `{ startedAt, room }` wrapper. Room views also carry the latest patch `revision` so clients can validate future patch continuity.
- The frontend merges `room:clock` through `src/app/roomClock.js`, updating only changed player timer objects while preserving the existing `room.game` object reference. `src/app/socketHandlers.js` skips stale clock payloads before scheduling React state work, routes pending-match clocks only to the match transition state, skips unchanged pending-match clock payloads before scheduling `setMatchSuccess`, keeps duplicate `lobby:stats` and `match:waiting` payloads from replacing unchanged app-shell state objects, suppresses repeated `duel:incoming` request ids before scheduling banner/audio work, and normalizes incomplete full snapshots through `src/app/roomSnapshot.js` before structural sharing so missing `players`, `spectators`, `chat`, `points`, `history`, `captures`, or `skillUses` cannot crash UI recovery paths.
- `src/room/Board.jsx` is memoized with a board-specific prop comparison so timer-only updates can refresh player clocks without re-rendering the board. Handler identity changes intentionally re-render only the board shell to refresh `handlersRef.current`; `src/room/actions/useRoomPointActions.js` keeps point handlers `useCallback`-stable across player timer object churn, and `src/room/RoomBattleStage.jsx` passes stable board, action-bar, and floating-layer callbacks instead of inline room-widget props; individual point buttons keep a stable handler ref and remain memoized so local confirmation, marker, decoration, scoring, and click-handler updates do not force all 13-line or 19-line intersections to re-render. `src/room/ChatBox.jsx`, `src/room/RoomPeopleList.jsx`, `src/room/OperationHint.jsx`, and `src/room/ActionBar.jsx` are memoized with chat/member/action-hint/action-control comparators that ignore player time updates from `room:clock` while still rerendering for chat content, chat-name metadata, connection state, membership, user display metadata, phase/turn, winner, skill uses, decision locks, replay step, and request/scoring changes. `src/room/RoomScreen.jsx` also keeps pass/resign/exit confirmation handlers and coordinate/move toggles stable, while `RoomHeader` owns the finished-room close countdown timer locally so those ticks do not re-run the full room screen or recreate action-control props. Timed draw/counting/result request toasts use `timedRoomRequestEffectKey()` so player-time-only `room:clock` updates do not rerun request-toast effects.
- `src/room/PlayerInfo.jsx` is memoized with a player/game-slice comparison so room clock ticks only re-render the player panel whose timer or relevant game slice changed, which is especially important when item effects swap portraits to animated assets.
- Home and Bright School utility hover performance keeps the existing visible motion contract and promotes only the transform surfaces that already animate. `.home-image-entry`, Bright School `.utility-entry`, and `.utility-entry-art` keep their current transform/filter timing and reduced-motion behavior, while `will-change: transform` lets cloud-hosted builds avoid avoidable repaint jank without changing the effect.
- `src/app/roomUserSync.js` merges the current user from room payloads by comparing only fields present in the room user payload, rather than stringifying the whole current user. This keeps item/effect synchronization cheap as the local user state gains more client-only fields.
- `src/shared/gameBoard.js` resolves standard board point ids by direct row-major index before falling back to id search. This keeps capture, liberty, territory, skill, and replay logic from repeatedly scanning the full point array on normal 13x13 states; `src/shared/game.js` re-exports the helper for existing callers.
- `collectGroup` uses a cursor-based breadth-first queue instead of `Array.shift()`, avoiding repeated array reindexing during liberty/capture checks.
- Dead-stone expansion and territory flood-fill use the same cursor-based queue pattern, reducing extra array churn during scoring and result review.

## App Stability Debt Reduction

- App-level overlay debt is reduced by `src/app/overlayRegistry.js`. Overlay state keys, React prop names, setter names, close-all behavior, and modal dismissal ordering now share one registry. This protects `match:found`, room resume, replay entry, desktop Escape, and mobile/browser back handling from future overlay additions that forget one wiring path.
- App-shell composition debt is further reduced by `src/app/appShellProps.js`: `App.jsx` now derives registry-backed overlay props once, then builds route and overlay adapter objects for `AppRoutes` and `AppOverlays`. Future player-facing features should extend the registry or focused adapter contract plus tests instead of adding another long flat prop list to the composition root.
- `src/app/useMailboxSummary.js` and `src/app/useRecruitmentReadyState.js` move feature-specific polling and readyAt timing out of `src/app/App.jsx`, leaving the app composition root to connect feature hooks to routes/overlays. Future app-shell features should follow this pattern instead of adding more inline polling loops to `App.jsx`.
- `src/app/useIncomingDuelState.js` moves direct-duel incoming request state out of `App.jsx`, matching the app-shell hook pattern used for overlays, room sessions, match transitions, mailbox summary, recruitment timing, and audio runtime.
- App-level render failures are contained by `src/app/AppErrorBoundary.jsx`, and `AppRoutes` renders the normal preload recovery shell when `view="room"` lacks `room` or `user`. This keeps reconnect/startup races and unexpected child render errors from producing a blank page.
- `server/userAssets.js` now treats legacy string-field mirroring as source-scoped synchronization. Only rows with `source = "legacy"` are deleted during mirror cleanup, so structured rows written by achievements, gacha, mailbox, recruitment, or future feature systems survive legacy refresh.
- `server/roomBroadcasts.js` declares a tested persistence policy for realtime broadcast categories. Full room updates and default patches remain forced persistence paths; clock and presence patch categories remain lightweight/throttled. This is a guardrail for future protocol work, not a full room protocol rewrite.
# Quality Gate And Playwright Isolation

- `npm run check` starts with ESLint. The first enforced lint baseline covers the new test-database, HTTP/session, modal accessibility, and migrated modal modules with JavaScript recommended rules, React Hooks rules, and JSX accessibility rules; expand this maintained-file set as adjacent modules are changed.
- Modal keyboard behavior uses jsdom and Testing Library interaction tests in addition to the existing source/markup contract tests.
- Playwright E2E and stability runs must set a unique run-id-scoped `.tmp/playwright/*.db` `DATABASE_URL`, initialize a valid empty SQLite file before `prisma db push`, and never inherit or mutate `prisma/dev.db`. The npm suite runner owns final cleanup after Playwright has stopped its WebServer, because Windows may force-stop the server before child-process exit hooks can delete an open SQLite file.
# 2 核 2G 单实例容量保护与指标

当前默认软门槛为 500 在线用户、100 活跃房间和每房 20 名观战者，可通过 `MAX_ONLINE_USERS`、`MAX_ACTIVE_ROOMS`、`MAX_SPECTATORS_PER_ROOM` 下调。它们是未经目标云机压测前的保护线，不是容量承诺。达到任一门槛时停止接纳对应的新匹配、约战或观战，现有对局、玩家重连与原观战者重连继续工作。单实例 + SQLite 仍是当前唯一受支持的生产拓扑；在房间、匹配、在线状态共享化前不能用 PM2 cluster 或多副本扩容。

黑化西格莉卡决战的“全服同时一盘”同样依赖上述单实例拓扑：占用判断读取进程内活动房间表，并利用 Socket 事件循环中“最终检查后同步注册房间”避免同进程竞态。若未来改为多进程或多副本，必须先把该唯一名额迁移为带租约/恢复语义的共享协调状态；不能只在每个副本各自扫描内存房间。

管理员概况的服务健康区域会暴露在线/房间/匹配/观战计数、持久化 backlog、RSS/heap、进程 CPU、event-loop delay/utilization、动作 ack 成功/失败/重复/排空拒绝、软容量拒绝、恢复原因和结果。完整 `room:update` 会记录 viewer-specific 视图构建耗时，并每 50 次抽样一次 JSON 字节数，避免可观测本身成为显著负载。

Phase 2 已删除观战 payload 内重复的黑方完整视图、把首次房间创建的 `match:found + room:update` 收敛为一份权威快照，并以 100ms debounce 合并大厅统计广播。后台可用 `lobbyStatsBroadcastEmissions / lobbyStatsBroadcastRequests` 观察合并效果。后续 move/skill delta 必须继续沿用现有 revision、gap detection 和 full snapshot 恢复合同，不能用不连续增量替代权威恢复。

上线初期应以 `ack p95 < 200ms`、`event-loop delay p95 < 50ms`、`RSS < 1.2GB`、恢复成功率大于 99%、持久化与结果保存错误为 0 作为压测观察线。只有在实际 2 核 2G 主机完成包含重连和观战的阶梯压测后，才能提高软上限。后续容量工作主要是目标机阶梯压测、静态资源 CDN 分流，以及在有完整 revision/恢复测试后按动作类型继续扩展 delta protocol。

Phase 3 已把上述观察线做成 `npm run verify:capacity`：smoke profile 使用 20 Socket/5 房和 event-loop p95 150ms 本地诊断线验证工具链，target profile 使用 500 Socket/100 房、每房 2 名观战、7.5 秒动作间隔、20% 重连、event-loop p95 50ms 目标机验收线并执行一次 SIGTERM 恢复。脚本使用独立临时数据库和 `NODE_ENV=capacity`，通过仅限 verification 环境的认证夹具把首个容量账号提升为指标读取管理员，报告冷登录/静态入口、ack/reconnect/resume 分位数，以及真实管理员运行指标快照；`artifacts/capacity/` 仅保存本机报告且不提交。该入口禁止连接正式生产数据库，本机 smoke 通过也不是容量承诺。

阶段 3 的完整本地发布候选入口为 `npm run verify:release-candidate`，按 Prisma Client、迁移基线、生产配置、构建、双项目稳定性、SQLite 备份恢复演练和容量 smoke 的顺序失败即停。稳定性环境通过 run/context namespace 隔离认证与 API 限流桶，Aemeath 等非默认角色由仅限 stability/capacity 且显式启用测试 action 的认证夹具准备；生产环境不挂钩用户名、不读取 namespace 绕过限流，也不能使用夹具路由。`npm ci` 的 postinstall 与生产 prestart 都生成 Prisma Client，确保干净安装可直接进入验证。

SQLite 运维入口 `npm run backup:sqlite -- --source <db> --output <backup>` 拒绝源/目标相同、拒绝覆盖现有输出并默认拒绝仓库 `prisma/dev.db`，随后使用 `VACUUM INTO` 和 `integrity_check` 生成一致性备份。`npm run verify:backup-restore` 只在 `.tmp/backup-restore/` 创建迁移后的源库、备份与恢复副本，借助哨兵数据验证恢复点不会被源库后续写入污染；正式恢复仍要求停服、保存故障现场、校验完整性并记录备份时间点后的数据损失。

静态流量的生产边界由 `deploy/nginx/sigrikago.conf` 与共享的 `deploy/nginx/sigrikago-routes.conf` 固化：Nginx 直接发送并 gzip `dist` 文本产物，直接发送 `/assets/` 和 `/uploads/`，Node 只处理实时 Socket、API 与健康检查。Vite hash CSS/JS 使用一年 immutable；普通稳定文件名图片/音频使用 `no-cache` 条件请求，避免同 URL 替换后继续命中旧视觉资源；SPA HTML 同样 no-cache，并保留严格的同源脚本 CSP，只在 `worker-src` 放行 Pixi 动态图片解码需要的 Blob Worker。`deploy/update-production.sh` 先以 `set -a` 导出项目 `.env`，使生产检查、Prisma 和其他子进程继承 systemd 使用的同一配置；随后在停服前以私有权限完成数据库备份，再恢复 Nginx 可读的构建权限并完成仅快进拉取、`npm ci --include=dev` 锁定依赖安装、暂存目录构建、生产配置检查和 `nginx -t`。显式包含 devDependencies 是因为 Vite 属于构建期依赖，而生产 `NODE_ENV` 默认会省略它；该顺序不会在旧站仍服务时清空现有 `dist` 或激活不可读产物。实际停机窗口限制在迁移、完整非用户后台快照同步、完整产物切换、reload、启动与 readiness 等待，失败不会强推或重置仓库。登录资源清单采用带宽优先但仍按账号可达性裁剪的策略：商店/库存/招募/拥有角色与装饰图片、默认或已拥有音乐、拥有角色的技能/系统音声以 6 路有界并发在登录加载页完成；未购买音乐、棋谱数据、房间对手与 Pixi 技能资源不进入冷登录突发。单资源 8 秒超时与登录后低并发重试继续限制坏文件和弱网风险，战斗加载仍校验并阻塞当前房间实际需要的资源。

---

# 工作流、文档规则与历史记录

本文记录 Trellis 工作流、系统设计文档维护方式、编码安全和较难归类的历史更新。修改 Trellis 规则、文档生成方式、AI 协作约定或历史追踪方式时优先更新本分篇。

## 当前文档维护规则

- `docs/system-design.md` 是入口和目录，不再承载所有详细设计。
- 详细设计按主题写入 `docs/system-design/*.md`。
- 修改入口或分篇后运行 `npm run docs:system-design` 生成 `docs/system-design.html`。
- `npm run verify:battle-fixes` runs the focused regression suite for battle-board fixes, then regenerates and validates system-design HTML. Use it before handoff when changing board erase styling, skill targeting/release confirmation, ordinary capture counting, chat wrapping, or character skill seed/config behavior.
- `npm test` excludes local Codex scratch folders such as `.codex-run/` in addition to worktrees, e2e tests, and stability tests, so temporary staged snapshots cannot be collected as Vitest suites during handoff.
- 中文内容应使用 `apply_patch`、Node UTF-8 脚本或其它明确 UTF-8 的工具写入，避免 PowerShell 默认编码链路造成显示混乱。

## Lobby Stats And Blacklist Match Blocking

- The home header receives live lobby stats from `lobby:stats` and shows online presence as a transparent icon-plus-number tag; mode-specific matchmaking counts are shown inside the click-open match-mode picker as icon-plus-number chips.
- The backend matchmaking wait state is now a queue instead of a single `waitingPlayer`. `match:join` checks both users' blacklist relationships before pairing, skips incompatible candidates, and keeps all skipped candidates waiting for later compatible players.
- Direct duel requests also consult the target user's blacklist. If the target has blacklisted the requester, the target receives no incoming request; the requester receives the normal rejection event after a 3-second delay, matching an ordinary refusal without revealing blacklist state.
- Socket disconnects use a session cleanup grace window. When the last socket for a user disconnects, the account is marked offline and room disconnect handling runs immediately, but the login session is cleared only after 30 minutes unless a new socket for the same session reconnects first. This prevents browser backgrounding, network sleep, and Socket.IO transient reconnects during a game from turning into silent authentication failures and frozen room UI.
- Login conflict checks use the active online-socket index rather than the mere presence of an unexpired grace-window session. A refreshed or closed page loses its in-memory token and must log in again, but if the old socket is already gone the new login is allowed instead of showing a stale "already logged in" conflict.
- If the Node watch server restarts while a page still has a valid JWT in memory, HTTP and Socket.IO auth can adopt that token's `sid` when no active in-memory session exists for the user. Sessions that were explicitly cleared by logout, forced login, pending-login expiry, or the disconnect grace timer are revoked and cannot be adopted again.
- Restored unfinished rooms mark every persisted player without a live socket as disconnected and append missing `disconnect` system notices before room timers resume. This keeps reconnect recovery, the centered portrait `断线中` badge, chat history, and watch-list online counts consistent after server restarts.
- The frontend listens for Socket.IO `connect_error`. Authentication failures (`unauthorized` / `forbidden`) now clear local room/match state, return to the login screen, and show `登录已失效，请重新登录` instead of leaving the player on a stale board snapshot.

## Trellis Workflow Notes

- Trellis is installed in this repository with project workflow files under `.trellis/`, project-scoped AI skills under `.agents/skills/`, and Codex hook/subagent configuration under `.codex/`.
- `AGENTS.md` keeps the Trellis assistant instructions plus the project rule that every update must keep `docs/system-design.md` synchronized.
- Impeccable is installed as a project-scoped skill under `.agents/skills/impeccable`. Its detector hook is controlled by `.impeccable/config.json`, local consent is stored in `.impeccable/config.local.json`, Codex runs the PostToolUse manifest from `.codex/hooks.json`, and live-mode injection is preconfigured through `.impeccable/live/config.json` for the Vite `index.html` shell. Codex 0.142 parses `.codex/hooks.json` with a strict schema, so the shared manifest keeps only the top-level `hooks` object and stores descriptive notes in system-design docs instead of the JSON file.
- Use `py -3 ./.trellis/scripts/get_context.py` to inspect current Trellis state. Use `py -3 ./.trellis/scripts/task.py create "<title>" --slug <slug>` to create tracked tasks before multi-step work.
- The initialized Trellis context uses developer `Moming`, single-repo mode, and `backend` / `frontend` spec layers. A bootstrap guidelines task exists under `.trellis/tasks/00-bootstrap-guidelines/`.
